,
  "descriptive_entry1_arithmetic": {
    "note": "descriptive only; actual size is computed from the resolved close by stage7_log_entry.py; notional cap = 30% of paper equity",
    "equity": 100000,
    "notional_cap": 30000,
    "mnq_multiplier": 2,
    "illustrative_price": 27085.25,
    "illustrative_max_fraction": 0.554
  },
  "discipline": "observation only; no performance statistic computed on any window; holdout 2021+ spent and never touched; neither battery script run; H8/H9 never re-run or re-tuned; no rule, default, hypothesis, or amendment changed; paper trading only; nothing booked (the 2026-11-13 close has not happened)"
}

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_signal_snapshot.py (5475 bytes)
"""Stage 7: weekly signal snapshot (paper-trading decision card). OBSERVATION ONLY.

Produces the operational readout for rule sets T, U3 (parallel) and V
(parallel, registered 2026-11-12, docs/ruleset_v_spec.md) from the
freshest canonical weekly bars:

  - T signal at the last COMPLETED weekly close (12-week log return > 0)
  - U3 position size: min(1, 0.20 / vol8_ann) x 0.5 if AVFI state = reversal_down
  - V position size: target 0.20 annualized (0.15 if reversal_down),
    size = min(1, tgt / vol8_ann) when long
  - signal age (bar date of the last flip)
  - next decision point (the coming Friday close)

Discipline: this tool observes the CURRENT signal state, exactly as any live
participant would see it. It computes NO performance statistic on any window
(the sealed 2021+ holdout is never evaluated here), no returns, no Sharpe,
no equity. The 26-week forward test starts only on Andrea's decision.

Run any day; the card always refers to the last completed W-FRI week.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import date, timedelta

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi, DEFAULT_PARAMS

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
VOL_WIN, VOL_TARGET, THROTTLE = 8, 0.20, 0.5

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])

today = pd.Timestamp(date.today())
last_label = df["date"].iloc[-1]
# A weekly bar counts as COMPLETED only when its Friday label is strictly
# BEFORE today. On the Friday of the close itself (label == today) the week
# is still forming until the close; a same-day pre-close stage0c refresh
# would otherwise be read as a completed decision close.
if last_label >= today:
    last = df.iloc[-2]      # last week is forming; use the completed one
    forming = str(last_label.date())
else:
    last = df.iloc[-1]
    forming = None
assert last is not None and len(df) >= 25

pos = int(last.name)                     # iloc position of the completed bar
closes = df["close"]
mom12 = float(np.log(closes.iloc[pos] / closes.iloc[pos - 12]))
t_signal = mom12 > 0

rets = np.log(closes / closes.shift(1)).iloc[pos - VOL_WIN + 1: pos + 1]
vol8_ann = float(rets.std() * np.sqrt(52))
vol_factor = min(1.0, VOL_TARGET / vol8_ann) if vol8_ann > 0 else 1.0

av = compute_avfi(df)
state = str(av["state"].iloc[pos])

throttle = THROTTLE if state == "reversal_down" else 1.0
u3_size = (1.0 if t_signal else 0.0) * vol_factor * throttle

# rule set V (docs/ruleset_v_spec.md): graduated modulation of the TARGET,
# not of the position. Frozen: volTgt 0.20, defTgt 0.15, cap 1.0.
v_tgt = 0.15 if state == "reversal_down" else 0.20
v_vol_factor = min(1.0, v_tgt / vol8_ann) if vol8_ann > 0 else 1.0
v_size = v_vol_factor if t_signal else 0.0

# signal age: walk back to the last flip of the 12-week sign
sig_hist = (np.log(closes / closes.shift(12)) > 0)
age_i = pos
while age_i > 1 and sig_hist.iloc[age_i] == sig_hist.iloc[age_i - 1]:
    age_i -= 1
signal_since = str(df["date"].iloc[age_i].date())

next_friday = today + timedelta(days=(4 - today.dayofweek) % 7)

# Fetch-staleness warning (observation only): if the canonical fetch predates
# the card week's Friday, the card may be computed on a partial week.
report_path = REPO / "research" / "stage0c_report.json"
stale_fetch = False
if report_path.exists():
    try:
        fa = pd.Timestamp(json.loads(report_path.read_text()).get("fetched_at_utc"))
        stale_fetch = (not pd.isna(fa)) and (
            pd.Timestamp(last["date"]).date() >= fa.date())
    except Exception:
        stale_fetch = False

card = {
    "as_of_utc": str(pd.Timestamp.utcnow()),
    "data_note": ("fresh canonical weekly bars; last completed W-FRI close "
                  + str(last["date"].date()) +
                  (f"; week ending {forming} forming until Friday" if forming else "") +
                  ("; WARNING: canonical fetch predates the card week "
                   "(partial-week risk); rerun stage0c before booking"
                   if stale_fetch else "")),
    "last_completed_week": str(last["date"].date()),
    "last_close": float(last["close"]),
    "T_signal": "LONG" if t_signal else "FLAT",
    "T_12wk_logret": round(mom12, 4),
    "T_signal_since": signal_since,
    "U3": {
        "vol8_ann": round(vol8_ann, 4),
        "vol_factor": round(vol_factor, 3),
        "avfi_state": state,
        "throttle": throttle,
        "position_size": round(u3_size, 3),
        "status": "parallel-logged-no-claimed-support (post-battery, U3 failed its holdout test)",
    },
    "V": {
        "vol8_ann": round(vol8_ann, 4),
        "vol_factor": round(v_vol_factor, 3),
        "avfi_state": state,
        "target": v_tgt,
        "position_size": round(v_size, 3),
        "status": ("parallel-registered forward-track (ruleset_v_spec.md, "
                   "2026-11-12); design-bookkeeping only; no claimed support; "
                   "no p-value exists for V"),
    },
    "next_decision": f"Friday {next_friday.date()} close, effective next Monday open",
    "discipline": ("observation only; no performance statistic computed on any "
                   "window; sealed holdout never evaluated; paper only"),
    "template_ref": "docs/stage7_sunday_log_template.md",
}

(REPO / "research" / "stage7_snapshot_current.json").write_text(json.dumps(card, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(card, indent=2))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_signal_snapshot.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_sunday_rehearsal_ts_mirror.ts (14626 bytes)
/**
 * Stage 7 SUNDAY-SEQUENCE REHEARSAL MIRROR (TypeScript; no Python this session).
 * Validates, on REAL bundle fixtures (log text, canonical CSV tail, stage0c
 * report), the fixes made this session to the stage 7 booking tools:
 *
 *   (a) the date-derivation fix in stage7_log_entry.py (fill Monday and next
 *       decision close derived from the DECISION CLOSE, not from today);
 *   (b) the new fetch-staleness guards (entry generator + outcome resolver +
 *       snapshot warning) fed by stage0c_report.json's fetched_at_utc;
 *   (c) the full Sunday 2026-11-15 sequence handoffs, on a sandbox copy of
 *       the log, with a SYNTHETIC completed 2026-11-13 bar (the current
 *       canonical bar is a partial mid-week fetch, n_sessions=3; its OHLC is
 *       used as the synthetic completed values and every number derived from
 *       it is labelled SYNTHETIC and is NOT booked anywhere).
 *
 * Mirror scope: sequence mechanics, date math, guard ordering, regex handoffs
 * (equity parse, duplicate guard, plan classification). The AVFI state and
 * T-signal math are validated elsewhere (stage 1 / 1b reports) and are only
 * spot-checked here where the fixtures allow.
 *
 * Nothing in this file writes to the durable log, the paper state, or any
 * evaluation window. The 2021+ holdout is spent and never evaluated; no
 * performance statistic is computed; paper trading only.
 */

// ---- real fixtures (inlined from the durable bundle, fetched 2026-11-11) ----
// data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv tail (date, open, close, n_sessions)
const BARS: Array<[string, number, number, number]> = [
  ["2026-08-14", 29851.25, 30141.75, 5],
  ["2026-08-21", 30170.0, 29387.75, 5],
  ["2026-08-28", 29392.25, 29491.75, 5],
  ["2026-09-04", 29540.0, 29565.25, 5],
  ["2026-09-11", 29535.25, 29387.0, 4],
  ["2026-09-18", 28997.75, 29613.6796875, 5],
  ["2026-09-25", 29938.0, 30889.25, 5],
  ["2026-10-02", 30870.0, 31061.75, 5],
  ["2026-10-09", 31059.0, 31108.75, 5],
  ["2026-10-16", 31145.5, 29391.5, 5],
  ["2026-10-23", 29227.0, 29117.75, 5],
  ["2026-10-30", 29268.0, 28534.0, 5],
  ["2026-11-06", 28587.75, 27085.25, 5],
  ["2026-11-13", 26878.5, 28622.25, 3], // PARTIAL fetch (Wed); SYNTHETIC completion below
];
const FETCHED_AT = "2026-11-11T18:23:02Z"; // stage0c_report.json (real)

// docs/stage7_sunday_log.md, exact real lines that the tools parse
const LOG_ENTRY0_PLANNED =
  "- Planned action: none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.";
const LOG_ENTRY0_EQUITY = "- Paper equity: 100,000.00 USD (start)";
const LOG_TEXT_BEFORE =
  "## Entry 0: start-up (Wednesday 2026-11-11)\n\n" +
  "- Decision close: 2026-11-06 (Friday that closed)\n" +
  "- Price context: NQ close 27,085.25, 12-week log return -10.69%\n" +
  "- Rule signal (T): FLAT since 2026-10-30 decision close (computed from data)\n" +
  "- AVFI state: reversal_down; spread z: negative (regime context only)\n" +
  "- U3 parallel row: size 0.00 (flat signal; state throttle 0.5, vol factor 0.849)\n" +
  "- " + LOG_ENTRY0_PLANNED + "\n" +
  "- Pre-committed levels: next decision close 2026-11-13; if the 12-week log\n" +
  "  return of the weekly close turns positive, go long 1 MNQ notional\n" +
  "  fraction at Monday open (notional under 30% of paper equity); if still\n" +
  "  negative, stay flat. No stop (signal exits only).\n" +
  "- Discipline check: matches the registered rule. YES\n" +
  "- " + LOG_ENTRY0_EQUITY + "\n" +
  "- Outcome: pending (next session Sunday 2026-11-15)\n";

// ---- date helpers mirroring the Python semantics ----
function pyWeekday(iso: string): number {
  const d = new Date(iso + "T12:00:00Z");
  return (d.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7; // Mon=0 .. Sun=6, like Python's weekday()
}
function addDays(iso: string, n: number): string {
  const d = new Date(iso + "T12:00:00Z");
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

// OLD derivation (pre-fix, from stage7_log_entry.py as shipped): from TODAY
function oldDates(today: string): { next_friday: string; next_monday: string } {
  const next_friday = addDays(today, (4 - pyWeekday(today) + 7) % 7);
  return { next_friday, next_monday: addDays(next_friday, 3) };
}
// NEW derivation (fixed): from the DECISION CLOSE
function newDates(decisionClose: string): { next_friday: string; next_monday: string } {
  return { next_friday: addDays(decisionClose, 7), next_monday: addDays(decisionClose, 3) };
}

// staleness predicate shared by both new guards: stale iff bar Friday >= fetch date
function isStale(barFriday: string, fetchedAt: string): boolean {
  return barFriday >= fetchedAt.slice(0, 10);
}

// resolver week selection: first bar strictly AFTER the decision close
function resolvingWeek(decisionClose: string): [string, number, number, number] {
  const after = BARS.filter((b) => b[0] > decisionClose);
  if (after.length === 0) throw new Error("no bar after decision close");
  return after[0];
}

// plan classification (exact substring rules of stage7_outcome.py)
function classifyPlan(planned: string): string {
  if (planned.includes("go LONG") && planned.includes("buy")) return "enter";
  if (planned.includes("hold the existing paper position")) return "hold";
  if (planned.includes("signal exit at the Monday")) return "exit";
  return "flat";
}

// equity parse (exact regex of both tools): last "Paper equity: N USD" in text
function parseEquity(text: string): number | null {
  const m = text.match(/Paper equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD/g);
  if (!m) return null;
  return parseFloat(m[m.length - 1].replace(/Paper equity:\s*/, "").replace(/[\s,]*USD/, "").replace(/,/g, ""));
}

const results: Array<[string, boolean, string]> = [];
function check(id: string, ok: boolean, note: string) { results.push([id, ok, note]); }

function main() {
  const SUNDAY = "2026-11-15";
  const DC0 = "2026-11-06";
  const DC1 = "2026-11-13";

  // ===== R1: the OLD date derivation was wrong on a Sunday run =====
  const oldSun = oldDates(SUNDAY);
  // The week that resolves a dc-11-13 entry is the week labeled 11-20 (Monday
  // 11-16); it exists in the canonical file only after its own Friday close,
  // so derive it from the template semantics, not from the CSV fixture.
  const bookedWeekLabel = addDays(DC1, 7);
  const bookedMonday = addDays(DC1, 3);
  check("R1a", oldSun.next_monday === "2026-11-23",
    `old code on Sunday logs fill Monday ${oldSun.next_monday}`);
  check("R1b", bookedWeekLabel === "2026-11-20" && bookedMonday === "2026-11-16" && oldSun.next_monday !== bookedMonday,
    `resolver books the week labeled ${bookedWeekLabel} (Monday ${bookedMonday}): old text contradicts the booking`);
  // ...and would have skipped a paper-test week entirely:
  check("R1c", oldSun.next_monday === addDays(DC1, 10),
    "old code's fill Monday = decision close + 10 days (a whole week skipped in the 26-entry test)");

  // ===== R2: the NEW derivation matches the resolver and the template =====
  const newSun = newDates(DC1);
  check("R2a", newSun.next_monday === bookedMonday,
    `fixed code: fill Monday ${newSun.next_monday} == resolver's booked week Monday`);
  check("R2b", newSun.next_friday === "2026-11-20",
    `fixed code: next decision close ${newSun.next_friday} (decision close + 7)`);
  // Wednesday run (today's fixture): dc 2026-11-06
  const newWed = newDates(DC0);
  check("R2c", newWed.next_monday === "2026-11-09",
    `fixed code mid-week: dc ${DC0} -> fill Monday ${newWed.next_monday}, matching Entry 0's "week of 2026-11-09"`);

  // ===== R3: resolver week selection is consistent for Entry 0 =====
  const res0 = resolvingWeek(DC0);
  check("R3a", res0[0] === "2026-11-13" && addDays(res0[0], -4) === "2026-11-09",
    `Entry 0 resolves on the bar labeled ${res0[0]} (Monday ${addDays(res0[0], -4)})`);
  check("R3b", res0[1] === 26878.5,
    `Entry 0's fill (if any) at Monday open ${res0[1]} (bar open) - correct week's open`);

  // ===== R4: staleness guards on the real fetch fixture =====
  check("R4a", isStale("2026-11-13", FETCHED_AT) === true,
    "current partial 11-13 bar vs fetch 11-11: STALE -> new guards REFUSE (correct)");
  check("R4b", isStale("2026-11-06", FETCHED_AT) === false,
    "completed 11-06 bar vs fetch 11-11: fresh, passes");
  check("R4c", isStale("2026-11-13", "2026-11-15T09:00:00Z") === false,
    "after a Sunday stage0c refresh: 11-13 bar is fresh, passes");
  // holiday-week robustness: staleness keys on the FETCH DATE, not n_sessions
  check("R4d", isStale("2026-09-11", FETCHED_AT) === false,
    "holiday week (n_sessions=4, 09-11) is NOT flagged: guard does not use n_sessions");

  // ===== R5: full Sunday-sequence rehearsal on a sandbox log copy =====
  // step A: resolver on Entry 0 (real log text, real fetch state)
  const plan0 = classifyPlan(LOG_ENTRY0_PLANNED);
  check("R5a", plan0 === "flat", `Entry 0 plan classified '${plan0}'`);
  const eqBefore = parseEquity(LOG_TEXT_BEFORE);
  check("R5b", eqBefore === 100000.0, `equity parsed from Entry 0: ${eqBefore}`);
  // (stale guard would refuse on the current fixture: correct; assume the
  // Sunday run does stage0c first, so simulate the fresh fetch for booking)
  const close13 = 28622.25; // SYNTHETIC completed-bar value (partial fetch's close)
  const outcomeBlock =
    `- Outcome (resolved, week closing 2026-11-13): flat as planned; no position, no P&L. Week close ${close13.toLocaleString("en-US")}.\n` +
    `- Deviation from plan: none (booked from the logged plan and the canonical bars only)\n` +
    `- Paper equity: 100,000.00 USD (post-outcome, week closing 2026-11-13)`;
  const logAfterOutcome = LOG_TEXT_BEFORE + "\n" + outcomeBlock + "\n";
  const eqAfterOutcome = parseEquity(logAfterOutcome);
  check("R5c", eqAfterOutcome === 100000.0,
    `post-outcome equity parse by the entry generator: ${eqAfterOutcome} (regex survives the "(post-outcome...)" suffix)`);

  // step B: entry generator, fixed dates, on dc 2026-11-13 (SYNTHETIC card)
  const c13 = BARS[BARS.length - 1][2];
  const c12back = BARS[BARS.length - 13][2];
  const mom12 = Math.log(c13 / c12back);
  check("R5d", mom12 < 0 && Math.abs(mom12 * 100 + 2.64) < 0.02,
    `SYNTHETIC 12-wk log return at the 11-13 bar: ${(mom12 * 100).toFixed(2)}% -> T FLAT (matches session-9 descriptive readout -2.64%)`);
  const planned1 = `none; stay flat for the week of ${newSun.next_monday}. Flat is the decision.`;
  check("R5e", planned1.includes("2026-11-16"),
    `Entry 1 planned action covers the week of 2026-11-16 (no skipped week): "${planned1}"`);
  const entry1 =
    `## Entry 1: week of 2026-11-16 (Sunday session, decision close ${DC1})\n\n` +
    `- Decision close: ${DC1} (Friday that closed)\n` +
    `- Price context: NQ close ${c13.toLocaleString("en-US")}, 12-week log return ${(mom12 * 100).toFixed(2)}%\n` +
    `- Rule signal (T): FLAT since 2026-10-30 decision close (computed from data)\n` +
    `- U3 parallel row: size 0.00 (flat signal)\n` +
    `- Planned action: ${planned1}\n` +
    `- Pre-committed levels: no position; next decision close 2026-11-20; ...\n` +
    `- Paper equity: 100,000.00 USD\n` +
    `- Outcome: pending (next session after the 2026-11-20 close)\n`;
  const logAfterEntry1 = logAfterOutcome + "\n" + entry1;
  // duplicate guard: one entry per decision close
  check("R5f", !LOG_TEXT_BEFORE.includes("Decision close: 2026-11-13"),
    "no false duplicate at generation time (Entry 0's lowercase 'next decision close 2026-11-13' does not match the guard string)");
  check("R5g", logAfterEntry1.includes("Decision close: 2026-11-13"),
    "after append, a rerun for dc 2026-11-13 is correctly REFUSED");
  check("R5h", !logAfterEntry1.includes("Decision close: 2026-11-20"),
    "the NEXT week's entry (dc 2026-11-20) is not blocked by Entry 1's text");

  // step C: re-run the resolver after Entry 1 exists
  // pending selection mirrors the FIXED stage7_outcome.py: an entry is
  // pending iff it has a "- Outcome: pending" line AND no resolved block.
  // (Entry 0 keeps its original pending line forever - append-only log.)
  const isPendingBlock = (t: string) =>
    /^- Outcome: pending/m.test(t) && !/Outcome \(resolved/.test(t);
  const blocksAfterEntry1 = logAfterEntry1.split(/(?m)^(?=## )/).filter((b) => b.startsWith("## "));
  const pendingBlocks = blocksAfterEntry1.filter(isPendingBlock);
  check("R5i", pendingBlocks.length === 1 && pendingBlocks[0].includes("Decision close: 2026-11-13"),
    `after Entry 0 is resolved and Entry 1 appended: exactly one UNRESOLVED pending block (${pendingBlocks.length}), and it is Entry 1 - the OLD selector would have re-found resolved Entry 0 (its 'Outcome: pending' line never goes away) and refused forever`);
  const barsAfterDc1 = BARS.filter((b) => b[0] > DC1).length;
  check("R5j", barsAfterDc1 === 0,
    `no canonical bar after ${DC1} exists yet: the resolver correctly reports PENDING for Entry 1 until the 2026-11-20 close (nothing fabricated)`);

  // ===== R6: plan-classifier disambiguation (exact generator texts) =====
  const holdPlanned = "hold the existing paper position (0.55 MNQ, entry fill 27,000.00); signal still LONG, no new buy, no stop.";
  const holdPre = "no order; hold until the signal exits (first decision close with a negative 12-week log return, exit filled at the following Monday open - 0.3625 pts). Next decision close: 2026-11-20.";
  const exitPlanned = "signal exit at the Monday 2026-11-23 open: paper sell 0.55 MNQ (fill at open - 0.3625 pts); the 12-week log return turned negative, rule set T goes flat.";
  check("R6a", classifyPlan(holdPlanned) === "hold", "hold plan classified hold");
  check("R6b", classifyPlan(holdPre) === "flat-not-exit" ? false : classifyPlan(holdPre) !== "exit",
    "hold PRE-COMMITTED text (mentions the future exit) is NOT misread as an exit plan");
  check("R6c", classifyPlan(exitPlanned) === "exit", "exit plan classified exit");

  // ===== verdict =====
  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r[1]);
  const report = {
    mirror: "research/stage7_sunday_rehearsal_ts_mirror.ts",
    executed: new Date().toISOString(),
    python_available: false,
    fixtures: "real bundle log lines + canonical CSV tail + stage0c fetched_at (2026-11-11); 2026-11-13 bar values SYNTHETIC (partial fetch, n_sessions=3), nothing booked",
    checks: results.map(([id, ok, note]) => ({ id, pass: ok, note })),
    all_passed: failed.length === 0,
    summary: `${results.length - failed.length}/${results.length} checks pass`,
  };
  console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
  if (failed.length) throw new Error("REHEARSAL FAILED: " + failed.map((f) => f[0]).join(", "));
}

main();

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_sunday_rehearsal_ts_mirror.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage_v4_smoke.py (6312 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""stage_v4_smoke.py - formula smoke validation for the AVFI v4 draft dashboard layers.

Scope (TOOLING VALIDATION, NOT EVALUATION):
  * Yang-Zhang vol floor formulas (sigma_yz, sigma3_yz) on unsealed canonical data.
  * Prior-bar percentile-rank helper (pctRank) as shipped in avfi_research_v4_draft.pine.
  * Sanity checks only: RS components non-negative, sigma levels sane, ranks in [0,1]
    cross-checked against an independent searchsorted implementation, coverage start dates.
  * NO forward returns read. NO extremity occupancy computed. NO thresholds applied.
    Nothing here touches any registered series, any verdict, or any holdout.

Run: python stage_v4_smoke.py  (from pipeline/research, after activate.sh)
Output: stage_v4_smoke_report.json
"""
import json
import math

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = ".."
HL_VOL = 30.0
K_YZ = 0.34 / 2.34
RANK_WIN = 500
MIN_COV = 100


def ewm(x: pd.Series, halflife: float) -> pd.Series:
    return x.ewm(halflife=halflife, adjust=False).mean()


def pct_rank(src: pd.Series, win: int, min_cov: int) -> pd.Series:
    """Vectorized port of the Pine pctRank helper: current bar excluded,
    share of prior valid values strictly below current, coverage gate."""
    out = pd.Series(np.nan, index=src.index)
    v = src.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    n = len(v)
    for i in range(n):
        cur = v[i]
        if np.isnan(cur):
            continue
        lo = max(0, i - win)
        w = v[lo:i]  # strictly prior bars only
        w = w[~np.isnan(w)]
        if len(w) >= min_cov:
            out.iloc[i] = float(np.sum(w < cur)) / len(w)
    return out


def pct_rank_ref(src: pd.Series, win: int, min_cov: int, n_check: int = 200, seed: int = 7) -> dict:
    """Independent recompute on a random subsample via searchsorted (side='left'
    = strictly below). Returns max abs diff and the checked count."""
    v = src.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    idx_ok = [i for i in range(len(v)) if not np.isnan(v[i]) and i >= min_cov]
    if not idx_ok:
        return {"checked": 0, "max_abs_diff": None}
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pick = rng.choice(idx_ok, size=min(n_check, len(idx_ok)), replace=False)
    diffs = []
    for i in pick:
        lo = max(0, i - win)
        w = v[lo:i]
        w = w[~np.isnan(w)]
        if len(w) < min_cov:
            continue
        srt = np.sort(w)
        ref = np.searchsorted(srt, v[i], side="left") / len(w)
        lo2 = max(0, i - win)
        w2 = v[lo2:i]
        w2 = w2[~np.isnan(w2)]
        got = float(np.sum(w2 < v[i])) / len(w2)
        diffs.append(abs(ref - got))
    return {"checked": len(diffs), "max_abs_diff": (max(diffs) if diffs else None)}


def main() -> None:
    rep = {}
    df = pd.read_csv(f"{ROOT}/data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"]).set_index("date")
    av = pd.read_csv(f"{ROOT}/data/avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"]).set_index("date")
    rep["n_bars"] = int(len(df))
    rep["first_bar"] = str(df.index[0].date())
    rep["last_bar"] = str(df.index[-1].date())

    # ---- Yang-Zhang floor ----
    o_yz = np.log(df["open"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    c_yz = np.log(df["close"] / df["open"])
    u_yz = np.log(df["high"] / df["open"])
    d_yz = np.log(df["low"] / df["open"])
    rs_raw = u_yz * (u_yz - c_yz) + d_yz * (d_yz - c_yz)
    # sanity 1: Rogers-Satchell per-bar term is non-negative by construction
    rs_valid = rs_raw.dropna()
    rep["rs_raw_min"] = float(rs_valid.min())
    rep["rs_raw_negative_count"] = int((rs_valid < -1e-12).sum())
    rs_bar = rs_raw.clip(lower=0.0)
    var_o = ewm(o_yz * o_yz, HL_VOL)
    var_c = ewm(c_yz * c_yz, HL_VOL)
    var_rs = ewm(rs_bar, HL_VOL)
    sigma_yz = np.sqrt((var_o + K_YZ * var_c + (1.0 - K_YZ) * var_rs).clip(lower=0.0))
    # sanity 2: sigma levels sane (weekly grain, NQ: roughly 0.5%-15% weekly vol)
    s = sigma_yz.dropna()
    rep["sigma_yz_min"] = float(s.min())
    rep["sigma_yz_med"] = float(s.median())
    rep["sigma_yz_max"] = float(s.max())
    rep["sigma_yz_all_finite_positive"] = bool((s > 0).all() and np.isfinite(s).all())
    # sanity 3: floor semantics: sigma3_yz >= sigma_r (max floor) once both exist
    v34 = pd.read_csv(f"{ROOT}/data/avfi_v34_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"]).set_index("date")
    sigma_r = v34["sigma_r"]
    sigma3_yz = np.sqrt(np.maximum(sigma_r ** 2, sigma_yz ** 2))
    both = pd.DataFrame({"a": sigma3_yz, "b": sigma_r}).dropna()
    rep["sigma3_yz_ge_sigma_r"] = bool((both["a"] >= both["b"] - 1e-12).all())
    rep["sigma3_yz_first_date"] = str(sigma3_yz.dropna().index[0].date())
    # sanity 4: YZ vs GK ordering sanity (no strict law; report medians only)
    rep["sigma_gk_med"] = float(v34["sigma_gk"].dropna().median())
    rep["sigma_yz_over_gk_med"] = float(s.median() / v34["sigma_gk"].dropna().median())

    # ---- vol8 (registered formula) for the rank cross-check ----
    r_w = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    r_mean = r_w.rolling(8).mean()
    r_var = ((r_w - r_mean) ** 2).rolling(8).sum() / (8 - 1)
    vol8 = np.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

    # ---- pctRank smoke: |sp_z| from the registered series, vol8 recomputed ----
    abs_spz = av["sp_z"].abs()
    ext_spz = pct_rank(abs_spz, RANK_WIN, MIN_COV)
    ext_vol = pct_rank(vol8, RANK_WIN, MIN_COV)
    for name, ser in (("ext_pct_spz", ext_spz), ("ext_pct_vol", ext_vol)):
        ok = ser.dropna()
        rep[f"{name}_count"] = int(len(ok))
        rep[f"{name}_in_unit"] = bool(((ok >= 0.0) & (ok <= 1.0)).all())
        rep[f"{name}_first_date"] = (str(ok.index[0].date()) if len(ok) else None)
    rep["pct_rank_ref_check_spz"] = pct_rank_ref(abs_spz, RANK_WIN, MIN_COV)
    rep["pct_rank_ref_check_vol"] = pct_rank_ref(vol8, RANK_WIN, MIN_COV)
    # coverage gate: with 1365 bars and win 500, early bars must be na until 100 priors
    first_valid = ext_spz.dropna().index[0]
    pos = abs_spz.index.get_loc(first_valid)
    rep["coverage_gate_ok"] = bool(pos >= MIN_COV)

    rep["discipline"] = ("formula sanity only; no forward returns read; "
                         "no thresholds applied; no occupancy computed; nothing evaluated")
    with open("stage_v4_smoke_report.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(rep, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(rep, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage_v4_smoke.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/sunday_session.sh (3069 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Stage 7 SUNDAY RUNBOOK - the one-command weekly booking session.
#
# Packages the rehearsed, guard-hardened cadence (sessions 9, 11, 12, 21 of
# research/HOURLY_SESSION_LOG.md) into a single ordered command so the Sunday
# session cannot skip a step or run the tools out of order:
#
#   1. stage0c_canonical.py      fresh canonical bars (the fetch-staleness
#                                 guard in the booking tools ENFORCES this)
#   2. stage7_outcome.py         resolve the oldest pending entry's week
#                                 (booked from completed bars only; refuses
#                                 before the Friday close)
#   3. stage7_log_entry.py       generate the NEXT entry from the newest
#                                 completed decision close (never retroactive)
#
# Usage:
#   bash research/sunday_session.sh --preview   # draft only, log untouched
#   bash research/sunday_session.sh --append   # the real Sunday booking
#
# This script introduces NO rule, default, hypothesis, signal math, or
# convention: it only runs the existing frozen tools in the rehearsed order
# and, on --append, syncs the touched files back to this bundle. Every
# booking guard (completion, staleness, duplicate-close, out-of-order,
# double-booking) lives in the tools themselves and stays authoritative.
# If run before the due Sunday, the tools refuse (by design); nothing is
# fabricated. Holdout 2021+ is spent; no battery script; H8/H9 never re-run.
set -euo pipefail
BUNDLE_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
MODE="${1:-}"
if [ "$MODE" != "--preview" ] && [ "$MODE" != "--append" ]; then
  echo "Usage: bash research/sunday_session.sh --preview | --append" >&2
  exit 2
fi

# Reset-proof working-tree rebuild (same durable block as verify_all.sh:
# the stage scripts hard-code REPO=/home/user/projects/quant).
if [ "$BUNDLE_DIR" != "/home/user/projects/quant" ]; then
  mkdir -p /home/user/projects/quant
  cp -a "$BUNDLE_DIR/." /home/user/projects/quant/
fi
cd /home/user/projects/quant
source /home/user/skills/data-analysis/python/library/activate.sh

echo "== Sunday runbook ($MODE), $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) =="
echo "== step 1/3: stage0c canonical refresh =="
python3 research/stage0c_canonical.py

echo "== step 2/3: resolve the oldest pending week (stage7_outcome $MODE) =="
python3 research/stage7_outcome.py "$MODE"

echo "== step 3/3: generate the next entry (stage7_log_entry $MODE) =="
python3 research/stage7_log_entry.py "$MODE"

echo "== booking sequence complete =="
if [ "$MODE" = "--append" ]; then
  echo "== syncing touched files back to the bundle =="
  for f in data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv data/avfi_series.csv \
           research/stage0c_report.json research/stage7_snapshot_current.json \
           docs/stage7_sunday_log.md docs/stage7_paper_state.json; do
    [ -f "$f" ] && cp "$f" "$BUNDLE_DIR/$f" && echo "  synced $f"
  done
fi
echo "Reminder: outcomes for the NEW entry are appended only after its Friday"
echo "close, by the next Sunday run of this script (never retroactively)."

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/sunday_session.sh

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/test_meta_tools.py (4644 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""test_meta_tools.py - the selftest gate for the mandated meta-tools.

Landed 2026-11-19, session 134 (frontier.md V9). Run standalone (exit 0 on
pass). Wired into research/verify_all.sh as the appended stage "meta" check;
the prior 25 checks are untouched.

The tests enforce the LAW, not just the arithmetic:
- sealed/window refusals are BY CONSTRUCTION (NQ 2021+ spent, all 2021+ sealed)
- determinism of the stability harness (double run byte-identical)
- parity with the frozen C0 climatology counter (docs/smh_climato_report.json,
  session 62: n_windows 1048, cum12 0.4409) for the NQ pre-2021 window
- reporting-only invariants
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "src"))

from utils.base_rate import SealedWindowError, calculate_base_rate
from utils.estimator_stability import test_estimator_stability


def main() -> int:
    failures = []

    # --- LAW 1: refusals by construction (the seal) -------------------------
    sealed_cases = [
        ("nq", ("2000-09-22", "2021-01-02"), "a window end past the seal is refused"),
        ("nq", ("2015-01-01", "2024-06-30"), "the SPENT NQ 2021+ holdout is refused"),
        ("gold", ("2000-09-01", "2023-01-01"), "sealed GOLD 2021+ is refused"),
        ("btc", ("2018-01-01", "2026-11-16"), "sealed BTC 2021+ is refused"),
    ]
    for asset, win, why in sealed_cases:
        try:
            calculate_base_rate(asset, win)
            failures.append(f"REFUSAL MISSING: {asset} {win} ({why})")
        except SealedWindowError:
            pass
    try:
        calculate_base_rate("no_such_asset", ("2015-01-01", "2020-12-31"))
        failures.append("REFUSAL MISSING: unknown asset accepted")
    except ValueError:
        pass

    # --- LAW 2: determinism of the stability harness -----------------------
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=300)
    est = lambda a: float(np.mean(np.abs(a)))  # reporting harness input
    r1 = test_estimator_stability(x, est, n_boot=50, block=8, seed=42)
    r2 = test_estimator_stability(x, est, n_boot=50, block=8, seed=42)
    if r1 != r2:
        failures.append("stability harness not deterministic (seeded double run differs)")
    if not (r1["band_lo"] <= r1["point"] <= r1["band_hi"]):
        failures.append("stability band does not bracket the point estimate")

    # --- PARITY: NQ pre-2021 pool vs the frozen climatology anchors ---------
    # (docs/smh_climato_report.json, session 62: 1050 valid weeks; the frozen
    # C0 pool is KM-CENSORING-based: origins break at the first forward event
    # or NaN, giving 1048 windows and a KM cum12 of 0.4409. THIS tool is the
    # plain descriptive counter: complete 12-week segments only, a plain
    # fraction, no KM band, by construction a DIFFERENT statistic. The parity
    # anchors are therefore: the reproduced 1050 valid weeks exactly, and a
    # sanity relation on counts: this tool's complete-segment windows equal
    # 1050 - 12 (the final 12 valid origins lack a full forward segment), the
    # KM pool's 1048 equals 1050 - 2 (origins with at least one usable row).
    # The convention difference is stated here, never smoothed over.)
    nq = calculate_base_rate("nq", ("2000-09-22", "2021-01-01"), horizons=(12,))
    clim = json.loads((REPO / "research" / "smh_climato_report.json").read_text())
    c0 = clim["C0_overlapping_pool"]
    if nq["n_valid"] != 1050:
        failures.append(
            f"NQ parity valid weeks: got {nq['n_valid']}, frozen anchor 1050"
        )
    if nq["cum12_pool"]["n_windows"] != 1050 - 12:
        failures.append(
            f"NQ parity window arithmetic: got {nq['cum12_pool']['n_windows']}, "
            f"expected 1050 - 12 = {1050 - 12} (complete-segment convention)"
        )
    if not (0.38 <= nq["cum12_pool"]["cum"] <= 0.50):
        failures.append(
            f"NQ cum12 descriptive counter implausible vs the KM 0.4409: "
            f"got {nq['cum12_pool']['cum']:.4f}"
        )

    # --- LAW 3: the tool never reaches into the seal even endogenous --------
    # (a window ending one second before the seal is lawful and gives data)
    ok = calculate_base_rate("btc", ("2014-09-15", "2020-12-25"), horizons=(4,))
    if ok["n_valid"] == 0:
        failures.append("btc pre-2021 window returned zero valid weeks")

    if failures:
        print("FAIL meta-tools")
        for f in failures:
            print("  " + f)
        return 1
    print("PASS meta-tools")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/test_meta_tools.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/v4h_audit.py (21187 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""V4h booking audit: read-only verification of the live 4h paper-bot track.

Spec: docs/v4h_spec.md (REGISTERED 2026-11-12, frozen). Engine: research/v4h_bot.py
(the rule's ONLY booking machinery - this audit NEVER books, NEVER writes, and
NEVER touches the engine's state, log, card or report files).

Purpose (session 51, 12 Nov 2026): the Sunday session checklist carries
"verify V4h's first nightly bookings". Before this tool, verification was
manual eyeballing. This instrument makes it a checkable verdict: every booked
row is audited against the frozen spec's own booking conventions
(1-bar lag, |dpos| cost, multiplicative equity from 100,000, size cap,
target/state consistency, 4h bucket anchoring, registration cutoff,
append-only idempotence).

Modes:
  status                (default) audit the bundle's data/v4h/ files, print a
                        JSON verdict. READ-ONLY: no file is written anywhere.
  --recompute           additionally fetch fresh hourly data (network), rebuild
                        the 4h grid with the engine's own build_4h, and
                        cross-check every booked row's close/state/vol8h/
                        mom12h against the fresh recomputation. STILL
                        READ-ONLY; nothing is booked or written.
  selftest              pure in-memory test: a synthetic clean track must
                        PASS every check, and each seeded violation must be
                        CAUGHT. No network, no bundle reads or writes.

Discipline (hard-coded): no backtest, no historical booking, no performance
statistic beyond the identity checks on the booked rows themselves (equity,
positions, costs are bookkeeping identities, not strategy evaluation), no
p-value, no comparison against any benchmark. The 2021+ NQ holdout is SPENT
and irrelevant here by construction (the track starts 2026-11-12 and books
only completed bars after it). Paper only: a clean audit says the BOOKKEEPING
is faithful, never that the rule has an edge.
"""
import json
import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

BUNDLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
V4H_DIR = BUNDLE / "data" / "v4h"
STATE_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_state.json"
LOG_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_log.csv"
REPORT_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_run_report.json"

# frozen constants (docs/v4h_spec.md; duplicated from the engine ON PURPOSE so
# the audit is an independent check against the spec, not a copy of live state)
MOM_LEN, VOL_WIN, BARS_PER_WEEK = 12, 8, 30
VOL_TGT, DEF_TGT, CAP, COST = 0.20, 0.15, 1.0, 0.3625
REGISTRATION_UTC = pd.Timestamp("2026-11-12T01:42:18Z")   # track_start (Malta 02:42:18+01:00)
ANCHOR_HOUR = 18                                          # globex open, ET clock
START_HOURS_ET = {(ANCHOR_HOUR + 4 * k) % 24 for k in range(6)}  # {18,22,2,6,10,14}
START_EQUITY = 100000.0
LOG_COLS = ["bar_start", "bar_end", "close", "state4h", "vol8h_ann", "tgt",
            "mom12h", "pos_held", "dpos", "cost_frac", "equity_after",
            "pos_next_decided"]

# tolerances (the engine rounds: close 2dp, pos 4dp, cost_frac 6dp, equity 2dp;
# the chain is recomputed from ROUNDED inputs, so drift accumulates - the
# cumulative allowance is 0.06/row, which dominates the worst-case rounding
# error of cost_frac (5e-7 * equity ~ 0.05) at the current equity scale, plus
# a flat 0.02 for the terminal 2dp rounding)
CHAIN_ROW_TOL = 0.06
CHAIN_FLAT_TOL = 0.02


def _ts(s):
    t = pd.Timestamp(s)
    return t.tz_localize("UTC") if t.tz is None else t.tz_convert("UTC")


def audit_track(state, log, report):
    """Pure audit: state dict, log DataFrame, report dict -> (verdict, details).

    Every check is derived from the frozen spec's booking conventions:
      * bars are 4h buckets anchored at 18/22/02/06/10/14 ET,
      * bar_end = bar_start + 4h, every bar_end > track_start (registration),
      * no duplicate/strictly increasing bar keys (append-only idempotence),
      * pos_held[t] = pos_next_decided[t-1] (the 1-bar decision lag; the
        first booked row must hold 0 - registration initializes pos_next
        to 0, so a nonzero pos_held[0] implies pre-track booking),
      * dpos[t] = |pos_held[t] - pos_held[t-1]| (state pos_prev for row 0),
      * cost_frac[t] = dpos[t] * 0.3625 / close[t],
      * 0 <= pos <= 1 (cap), tgt in {0.15, 0.20} matching state4h,
      * pos_held = 0 whenever mom12h <= 0 (long-only, signal exits only),
      * equity chain: eq *= 1 + pos*ret - cost_frac from 100,000, where ret
        uses the previous 4h close - verifiable only between CONTIGUOUS
        booked buckets; a gap (skipped holiday bucket or engine catch-up
        boundary) marks the row ret-unverifiable, never FAIL,
      * state: equity ~= last equity_after, pos_next ~= last
        pos_next_decided, last_booked == last bar_end.
    """
    fails, warns, notes = [], [], []
    n = 0 if log is None else len(log)

    # --- state-level checks (hold with zero bookings) ---
    if not state.get("track_start"):
        fails.append("state: track_start missing")
        return {"verdict": "FAIL", "n_booked": n, "failures": fails,
                "warnings": warns, "notes": notes}
    ts = _ts(state["track_start"])
    if ts < REGISTRATION_UTC - pd.Timedelta(minutes=1):
        fails.append(f"state: track_start {ts} predates registration")
    if state.get("equity", 0) <= 0:
        fails.append("state: equity missing/nonpositive")

    if log is None or n == 0:
        notes.append("no bars booked yet (log empty/absent) - state-level "
                     "checks only; the track is armed, not evaluated")
        verdict = "FAIL" if fails else "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS"
        return {"verdict": verdict, "n_booked": 0, "failures": fails,
                "warnings": warns, "notes": notes}

    missing = [c for c in LOG_COLS if c not in log.columns]
    if missing:
        fails.append(f"log: missing columns {missing}")
        return {"verdict": "FAIL", "n_booked": n, "failures": fails,
                "warnings": warns, "notes": notes}
    df = log.reset_index(drop=True)

    starts = df["bar_start"].map(_ts)
    ends = df["bar_end"].map(_ts)

    # --- bucket geometry + registration cutoff ---
    starts_et = starts.dt.tz_convert("America/New_York")
    if not (ends > ts).all():
        bad = int((ends <= ts).sum())
        fails.append(f"log: {bad} bar(s) completed at/before track_start "
                     f"(pre-registration booking is forbidden by the spec)")
    if not (starts_et.dt.hour.isin(START_HOURS_ET)).all():
        bad = starts_et.loc[~starts_et.dt.hour.isin(START_HOURS_ET)]
        fails.append(f"log: {len(bad)} bar(s) not on a globex anchor hour "
                     f"(first offender {bad.iloc[0]})")
    if not ((ends - starts) == pd.Timedelta(hours=4)).all():
        fails.append("log: bar_end - bar_start != 4h somewhere")

    # --- append-only idempotence ---
    if starts.duplicated().any() or ends.duplicated().any():
        fails.append("log: duplicate bar keys (idempotence violation)")
    if not starts.is_monotonic_increasing:
        fails.append("log: bar_start not strictly increasing")

    # --- per-row rule identities ---
    pos = df["pos_held"].astype(float)
    nxt = df["pos_next_decided"].astype(float)
    dpos = df["dpos"].astype(float)
    cost = df["cost_frac"].astype(float)
    close = df["close"].astype(float)
    mom = pd.to_numeric(df["mom12h"], errors="coerce")
    tgt = df["tgt"].astype(float)
    st = df["state4h"].astype(str)

    if ((pos < 0) | (pos > CAP + 1e-9)).any():
        fails.append("log: pos_held outside [0, 1] (cap or sign violation)")
    if not tgt.isin([VOL_TGT, DEF_TGT]).all():
        fails.append("log: tgt outside {0.20, 0.15}")
    mism = (st == "reversal_down") != (tgt == DEF_TGT)
    if mism.any():
        fails.append(f"log: tgt/state4h mismatch in {int(mism.sum())} row(s) "
                     f"(reversal_down must carry 0.15)")
    # long-only check is on the DECISION, not the holding: pos_next_decided[t]
    # is what the rule chose at close t, so it must be 0 whenever mom12h[t] <=
    # 0. pos_held[t] (decided at t-1) may legitimately still be long while the
    # same-row mom has flipped - that is the 1-bar lag working, not a violation.
    flat_viol = (mom.notna() & (mom <= 0) & (nxt.abs() > 1e-9))
    if flat_viol.any():
        fails.append(f"log: {int(flat_viol.sum())} row(s) decide a position "
                     f"with mom12h <= 0 (long-only violation)")

    # sizing identity: nxt = min(1, tgt/vol8h) when long-able, else 0
    vol8 = pd.to_numeric(df["vol8h_ann"], errors="coerce")
    size_exp = pd.Series(0.0, index=df.index)
    ok_long = mom.notna() & (mom > 0) & vol8.notna() & (vol8 > 0)
    size_exp[ok_long] = (tgt / vol8).clip(upper=CAP)[ok_long]
    if (nxt - size_exp).abs().gt(2e-3).any():
        fails.append("log: pos_next_decided != min(1, tgt/vol8h) under the "
                     "long condition somewhere (sizing-identity violation)")

    # --- position/cost chain (1-bar lag) ---
    # Row 0's pos_held is not checked against the CURRENT state pos_prev
    # (that field advances every run and no longer holds the booking-time
    # value). The spec's registration invariant replaces it: the engine
    # initializes pos_next = 0 at track start, so the FIRST booked row
    # always holds 0; dpos[0] = pos_held[0] follows.
    prev_pos = pd.concat(
        [pd.Series([0.0]), pos.iloc[:-1]], ignore_index=True)
    if abs(float(pos.iloc[0])) > 1e-9:
        fails.append("log: first booked row holds a nonzero position "
                     "(registration initializes pos_next = 0; a nonzero "
                     "pos_held[0] implies pre-track booking)")
    # positional comparison via numpy (pandas would align the two slices by
    # index and compare row t against row t - fixed session 55; the selftest's
    # synthetic clean track caught the misalignment as a false lag violation)
    lag_diffs = nxt.iloc[:-1].to_numpy() - pos.iloc[1:].to_numpy()
    if max(abs(d) for d in lag_diffs) > 1e-4:
        fails.append("log: pos_held[t] != pos_next_decided[t-1] somewhere "
                     "(1-bar decision-lag violation)")
    if abs(float(nxt.iloc[-1]) - float(state.get("pos_next", 0.0))) > 1e-4:
        fails.append("state: pos_next != last row's pos_next_decided")
    dpos_exp = (pos - prev_pos).abs()
    if (dpos - dpos_exp).abs().gt(5e-4).any():
        fails.append("log: dpos != |pos_held[t] - pos_held[t-1]| somewhere")
    cost_exp = dpos_exp * COST / close
    if (cost - cost_exp).abs().gt(5e-6).any():
        fails.append("log: cost_frac != dpos * 0.3625 / close somewhere")

    # --- equity chain (contiguous rows only; checked at EVERY row, not just
    # the last - a terminal-only check is blind to interior corruption, a
    # defect found by the session-56 TS mirror before this instrument's first
    # real run) ---
    eq = START_EQUITY
    unver = 0
    prev_close, prev_end, since_sync = None, None, 0
    chain_fail = False
    for i in range(n):
        contiguous = prev_end is not None and starts.iloc[i] == prev_end
        if contiguous and prev_close:
            ret = close.iloc[i] / prev_close - 1.0
            eq *= (1.0 + pos.iloc[i] * ret - cost.iloc[i])
            since_sync += 1
            tol_i = CHAIN_FLAT_TOL + CHAIN_ROW_TOL * since_sync
            logged_i = float(df["equity_after"].iloc[i])
            if abs(eq - logged_i) > tol_i and not chain_fail:
                chain_fail = True   # report the FIRST mismatch only
                fails.append(f"log: equity chain mismatch at row {i} "
                             f"(recomputed {eq:.2f} vs logged {logged_i:.2f}, "
                             f"tol {tol_i:.2f})")
        else:
            unver += 1
            eq = float(df["equity_after"].iloc[i])   # resume from the log
            since_sync = 0
        prev_close, prev_end = close.iloc[i], ends.iloc[i]
    if unver:
        notes.append(f"{unver} row(s) ret-unverifiable (gap before the row: "
                     f"skipped holiday bucket or first booked row - the "
                     f"chain resumes from the logged equity there, by design)")
    if abs(float(state.get("equity", 0.0)) -
           float(df["equity_after"].iloc[-1])) > 0.02:
        fails.append("state: equity != last row's equity_after")

    # --- idempotence evidence ---
    lb = state.get("last_booked")
    if lb is None or _ts(lb) != ends.iloc[-1]:
        fails.append("state: last_booked != last row's bar_end")

    if report is not None and report.get("last_booked") not in (None, ""):
        if _ts(report["last_booked"]) != ends.iloc[-1]:
            warns.append("run report last_booked != last log row "
                         "(likely a stale report from before the latest "
                         "booking run; harmless if the nightly run follows)")

    verdict = "FAIL" if fails else "CLEAN"
    return {"verdict": verdict, "n_booked": n, "failures": fails,
            "warnings": warns, "notes": notes}


def load_bundle():
    state = json.loads(STATE_F.read_text())
    log = pd.read_csv(LOG_F) if LOG_F.exists() else None
    report = (json.loads(REPORT_F.read_text())
              if REPORT_F.exists() else None)
    return state, log, report


def status(recompute=False):
    state, log, report = load_bundle()
    out = audit_track(state, log, report)

    if recompute and log is not None and len(log):
        out["recompute"] = recompute_crosscheck(log)
        if out["recompute"]["verdict"] == "FAIL":
            out["verdict"] = "FAIL"
            out["failures"] = out.get("failures", []) + [
                "recompute: " + f for f in out["recompute"]["failures"]]

    out["mode"] = "status" + (" --recompute" if recompute else "")
    out["discipline"] = ("read-only booking audit; no backtest, no p-value, "
                         "no performance statistic, nothing written; a CLEAN "
                         "verdict certifies bookkeeping fidelity only, never "
                         "an edge")
    print(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
    return 0 if out["verdict"] in ("CLEAN", "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS") else 1


def recompute_crosscheck(log):
    """Network cross-check: rebuild the 4h grid from a fresh hourly fetch
    using the ENGINE'S OWN build_4h, then compare every booked row's close,
    state4h, vol8h_ann and mom12h against the fresh recomputation. Read-only."""
    sys.path.insert(0, str(BUNDLE / "research"))
    sys.path.insert(0, str(BUNDLE / "strategies"))
    import numpy as np
    import v4h_bot
    from avfi_replication import compute_avfi

    t_now = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC").timestamp()
    h1 = v4h_bot.fetch("1h", t_now - 730 * 86400, t_now)
    bars = v4h_bot.build_4h(h1)
    av4 = compute_avfi(bars)
    c = bars["close"]
    r4 = np.log(c / c.shift(1))
    vol8h = r4.rolling(VOL_WIN).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(BARS_PER_WEEK * 52.0)
    mom12h = np.log(c / c.shift(MOM_LEN))
    grid = pd.DataFrame({"bar_start": bars["date"],
                         "close": c, "state4h": av4["state"].astype(str),
                         "vol8h": vol8h, "mom12h": mom12h})
    grid["key"] = grid["bar_start"].map(_norm_key)

    fails = []
    checked = 0
    for _, row in log.iterrows():
        m = grid[grid["key"] == _norm_key(row["bar_start"])]
        if m.empty:
            fails.append(f"recompute: booked bar {row['bar_start']} not "
                         f"found in the fresh grid")
            continue
        g = m.iloc[0]
        checked += 1
        if abs(float(g["close"]) - float(row["close"])) > 0.02:
            fails.append(f"recompute: close mismatch at {row['bar_start']}")
        if str(g["state4h"]) != str(row["state4h"]):
            fails.append(f"recompute: state4h mismatch at {row['bar_start']}")
        if pd.notna(g["vol8h"]) and row["vol8h_ann"] is not None:
            if abs(float(g["vol8h"]) - float(row["vol8h_ann"])) > 5e-4:
                fails.append(f"recompute: vol8h mismatch at {row['bar_start']}")
        if pd.notna(g["mom12h"]) and row["mom12h"] is not None:
            if abs(float(g["mom12h"]) - float(row["mom12h"])) > 5e-4:
                fails.append(f"recompute: mom12h mismatch at {row['bar_start']}")
    return {"verdict": "FAIL" if fails else "CLEAN", "rows_checked": checked,
            "failures": fails[:20],
            "note": ("cross-check against a fresh fetch; minor close/state "
                     "revisions inside the lookback window are possible "
                     "Yahoo artefacts - investigate before trusting any "
                     "mismatch")}


def _norm_key(s):
    t = pd.Timestamp(s)
    return t.tz_convert("UTC").isoformat()


def selftest():
    """Pure in-memory test: a synthetic clean track must be CLEAN, and each
    seeded violation must be caught. No network, no bundle access."""
    base = pd.Timestamp("2026-11-12T18:00:00-05:00")   # 18:00 ET anchor
    closes = [20000.0, 20040.0, 20090.0, 20060.0, 20110.0, 20160.0]
    states = ["up_pressure", "up_pressure", "up_pressure",
              "up_pressure", "reversal_down", "reversal_down"]
    tgts = [VOL_TGT if s != "reversal_down" else DEF_TGT for s in states]
    moms = [0.0012, 0.0026, 0.0041, 0.0052, 0.0033, 0.0021]
    vol8 = [0.4431, 0.4398, 0.4231, 0.4187, 0.4512, 0.4466]
    # decisions built BY THE RULE: size = min(1, tgt/vol8h) when mom > 0
    sizes = [round(min(CAP, tgts[i] / vol8[i]), 4) if moms[i] > 0 else 0.0
             for i in range(6)]
    pos_held = [0.0] + sizes[:-1]                   # the 1-bar decision lag
    rows, eq, prev_pos = [], 100000.0, 0.0
    for i in range(6):
        t0 = base + pd.Timedelta(hours=4 * i)
        t1 = t0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)
        pos = pos_held[i]
        dpos = abs(pos - prev_pos)
        cost = round(dpos * COST / closes[i], 6)
        ret = closes[i] / closes[i - 1] - 1 if i else 0.0
        eq *= (1.0 + pos * ret - cost) if i else 1.0
        rows.append({"bar_start": str(t0.isoformat()), "bar_end": str(t1.isoformat()),
                     "close": round(closes[i], 2), "state4h": states[i],
                     "vol8h_ann": vol8[i], "tgt": tgts[i],
                     "mom12h": round(moms[i], 4), "pos_held": round(pos, 4),
                     "dpos": round(dpos, 4), "cost_frac": cost,
                     "equity_after": round(eq, 2),
                     "pos_next_decided": round(sizes[i], 4)})
        prev_pos = pos
    log = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    state = {"track_start": "2026-11-12T02:42:18.836823+01:00",
             "equity": round(eq, 2), "last_booked": rows[-1]["bar_end"],
             "pos_next": round(sizes[-1], 6), "pos_prev": round(pos_held[-1], 6)}
    report = {"status": "OK", "last_booked": rows[-1]["bar_end"]}

    r = audit_track(state, log, report)
    assert r["verdict"] == "CLEAN" and r["n_booked"] == 6, r

    def expect_fail(mutate, label):
        st2, lg2 = json.loads(json.dumps(state)), log.copy()
        mutate(st2, lg2)
        rr = audit_track(st2, lg2, report)
        assert rr["verdict"] == "FAIL" and any(label in f
                                               for f in rr["failures"]), \
            (label, rr["failures"])

    def m1(st, lg): st["track_start"] = "2019-01-01T00:00:00+01:00"
    expect_fail(m1, "predates registration")

    def m2(st, lg): lg.loc[3, "bar_start"] = lg.loc[2, "bar_start"]
    expect_fail(m2, "duplicate bar keys")

    def m3(st, lg): lg.loc[2, "pos_held"] = 1.7
    expect_fail(m3, "outside [0, 1]")

    def m4(st, lg): lg.loc[4, "tgt"] = 0.20
    expect_fail(m4, "tgt/state4h mismatch")

    def m5(st, lg): lg.loc[1, "pos_held"] = 0.3
    expect_fail(m5, "1-bar decision-lag violation")

    def m6(st, lg): lg.loc[3, "dpos"] = 0.9
    expect_fail(m6, "dpos !=")

    def m7(st, lg): lg.loc[3, "cost_frac"] = 0.5
    expect_fail(m7, "cost_frac !=")

    def m8(st, lg): lg.loc[4, "equity_after"] = lg.loc[4, "equity_after"] - 50.0
    expect_fail(m8, "equity chain mismatch")

    def m9(st, lg): lg.loc[2, "bar_start"] = str(
        (pd.Timestamp(lg.loc[2, "bar_start"]) + pd.Timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat())
    expect_fail(m9, "anchor hour")

    def m10(st, lg): st["equity"] = 999.0
    expect_fail(m10, "equity != last row")

    def m11(st, lg): st["last_booked"] = None
    expect_fail(m11, "last_booked !=")

    def m12(st, lg): lg.loc[5, "mom12h"] = -0.0025   # negative mom, pos held
    expect_fail(m12, "long-only violation")

    # zero-booking state must read CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS
    r0 = audit_track({"track_start": "2026-11-12T02:42:18+01:00",
                      "equity": 100000.0, "last_booked": None,
                      "pos_next": 0.0, "pos_prev": 0.0}, None, None)
    assert r0["verdict"] == "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS", r0

    print("SELFTEST PASS (v4h_audit: clean track CLEAN; 12 seeded "
          "violations caught; zero-booking state CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS)")
    return 0


def main():
    args = sys.argv[1:]
    if args and args[0] == "selftest":
        sys.exit(selftest())
    if args and args[0] == "--recompute":
        sys.exit(status(recompute=True))
    sys.exit(status())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
