// research/v4h_audit_ts_mirror.ts
// TypeScript mirror of research/v4h_audit.py (session-51 instrument), executed
// 12 Nov 2026, session 56, because the sandbox had no Python (the normal reset
// state). The pandas selftest itself still runs on the next Python-enabled
// reset BEFORE the tool is wired into verify_all; this mirror validates the
// audit SEMANTICS now so Sunday's run is a formality. Same two-layer convention
// as sessions 2, 3, 6, 9, 11.
//
// OUTCOME (recorded in the session-56 log entry): the mirror caught ONE REAL
// LATENT DEFECT in the never-run Python instrument - the equity chain was
// checked against the LAST row's logged equity only, so mutation m8 (an
// interior equity_after corrupted by -50) was invisible and the Python
// selftest would have FAILED on its own m8 assertion. v4h_audit.py was fixed
// (per-row chain check) BEFORE its first real run; this mirror incorporates
// the same fix. One mirror-side fixture bug was also found and fixed (isoET
// added the EST offset instead of subtracting, shifting every synthetic
// timestamp +10h; the audit logic was never wrong).
//
// Faithful port of: audit_track() (per-row-chain revision) + selftest()
// (12 seeded violations + zero-booking check) + the real zero-booking bundle
// state. READ-ONLY: no file written, no booking, no network, no market data.

const VOL_TGT = 0.20, DEF_TGT = 0.15, CAP = 1.0, COST = 0.3625;
const REGISTRATION_UTC_MS = Date.parse("2026-11-12T01:42:18Z");
const START_HOURS_ET = new Set([18, 22, 2, 6, 10, 14]);
const START_EQUITY = 100000.0;
const CHAIN_ROW_TOL = 0.06, CHAIN_FLAT_TOL = 0.02;
const LOG_COLS = ["bar_start", "bar_end", "close", "state4h", "vol8h_ann",
  "tgt", "mom12h", "pos_held", "dpos", "cost_frac", "equity_after",
  "pos_next_decided"];

interface Row { [k: string]: unknown }
interface State { track_start: string; equity: number; last_booked: string | null; pos_next: number; pos_prev?: number }
interface Report { [k: string]: unknown }

function ts(s: string): number {
  const t = Date.parse(s);
  if (isNaN(t)) throw new Error("unparseable timestamp: " + s);
  return t;
}
function etOffset(utcMs: number): number {
  // US DST: 2nd Sunday March 02:00 EST (=07:00 UTC) -> 1st Sunday November
  // 02:00 EDT (=06:00 UTC); ET is otherwise EST (-5).
  const y = new Date(utcMs).getUTCFullYear();
  const findSundayUTC = (month: number, nth: number, hourEST: number) => {
    const first = new Date(Date.UTC(y, month, 1));
    const firstSunday = 1 + ((7 - first.getUTCDay()) % 7);
    return Date.UTC(y, month, firstSunday + 7 * (nth - 1), hourEST, 0, 0);
  };
  const dstStart = findSundayUTC(2, 2, 7);
  const dstEnd = findSundayUTC(10, 1, 6);
  return (utcMs >= dstStart && utcMs < dstEnd) ? -4 * 3600000 : -5 * 3600000;
}
function etHour(utcMs: number): number {
  return new Date(utcMs + etOffset(utcMs)).getUTCHours();
}
function isoET(ms: number): string {
  // ET wall clock for instant ms during EST: subtract 5h, label -05:00.
  // (First draft ADDED 5h - a mirror-side fixture bug caught and fixed before
  // any verdict was recorded; see header.)
  const d = new Date(ms - 5 * 3600000);
  const p = (x: number, w = 2) => String(x).padStart(w, "0");
  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${p(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getUTCDate())}T${p(d.getUTCHours())}:${p(d.getUTCMinutes())}:${p(d.getUTCSeconds())}-05:00`;
}

// ---- audit_track: faithful port of the per-row-chain revision ----
function auditTrack(state: Record<string, unknown>, log: Row[] | null, report: Report | null) {
  const fails: string[] = [], warns: string[] = [], notes: string[] = [];
  const n = log === null ? 0 : log.length;
  if (!state["track_start"]) {
    fails.push("state: track_start missing");
    return { verdict: "FAIL", n_booked: n, failures: fails, warnings: warns, notes };
  }
  const tStart = ts(state["track_start"] as string);
  if (tStart < REGISTRATION_UTC_MS - 60000) fails.push("state: track_start predates registration");
  if (!(typeof state["equity"] === "number" && (state["equity"] as number) > 0)) fails.push("state: equity missing/nonpositive");
  if (log === null || n === 0) {
    notes.push("no bars booked yet (log empty/absent) - state-level checks only; the track is armed, not evaluated");
    return { verdict: fails.length ? "FAIL" : "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS", n_booked: 0, failures: fails, warnings: warns, notes };
  }
  const missing = LOG_COLS.filter(c => !(c in log[0]));
  if (missing.length) {
    fails.push("log: missing columns " + missing.join(","));
    return { verdict: "FAIL", n_booked: n, failures: fails, warnings: warns, notes };
  }
  const starts = log.map(r => ts(r["bar_start"] as string));
  const ends = log.map(r => ts(r["bar_end"] as string));
  if (!ends.every(e => e > tStart)) {
    fails.push(`log: ${ends.filter(e => e <= tStart).length} bar(s) completed at/before track_start (pre-registration booking is forbidden by the spec)`);
  }
  const badAnchor = starts.filter(s => !START_HOURS_ET.has(etHour(s)));
  if (badAnchor.length) fails.push(`log: ${badAnchor.length} bar(s) not on a globex anchor hour (ET)`);
  if (!log.every((_, i) => ends[i] - starts[i] === 4 * 3600000)) fails.push("log: bar_end - bar_start != 4h somewhere");
  if (new Set(starts).size !== n || new Set(ends).size !== n) fails.push("log: duplicate bar keys (idempotence violation)");
  if (!starts.every((s, i) => i === 0 || s > starts[i - 1])) fails.push("log: bar_start not strictly increasing");
  const pos = log.map(r => Number(r["pos_held"]));
  const nxt = log.map(r => Number(r["pos_next_decided"]));
  const dpos = log.map(r => Number(r["dpos"]));
  const cost = log.map(r => Number(r["cost_frac"]));
  const close = log.map(r => Number(r["close"]));
  const mom = log.map(r => Number(r["mom12h"]));
  const tgt = log.map(r => Number(r["tgt"]));
  const s4 = log.map(r => String(r["state4h"]));
  const vol8 = log.map(r => Number(r["vol8h_ann"]));
  if (pos.some(p => p < 0 || p > CAP + 1e-9)) fails.push("log: pos_held outside [0, 1] (cap or sign violation)");
  if (!tgt.every(t => t === VOL_TGT || t === DEF_TGT)) fails.push("log: tgt outside {0.20, 0.15}");
  const mism = s4.map((s, i) => (s === "reversal_down") !== (tgt[i] === DEF_TGT));
  if (mism.some(Boolean)) fails.push(`log: tgt/state4h mismatch in ${mism.filter(Boolean).length} row(s) (reversal_down must carry 0.15)`);
  const flatViol = nxt.map((x, i) => !isNaN(mom[i]) && mom[i] <= 0 && Math.abs(x) > 1e-9);
  if (flatViol.some(Boolean)) fails.push(`log: ${flatViol.filter(Boolean).length} row(s) decide a position with mom12h <= 0 (long-only violation)`);
  const sizeExp = mom.map((m, i) => (!isNaN(m) && m > 0 && !isNaN(vol8[i]) && vol8[i] > 0) ? Math.min(CAP, tgt[i] / vol8[i]) : 0.0);
  if (nxt.some((x, i) => Math.abs(x - sizeExp[i]) > 2e-3)) fails.push("log: pos_next_decided != min(1, tgt/vol8h) under the long condition somewhere (sizing-identity violation)");
  if (Math.abs(pos[0]) > 1e-9) fails.push("log: first booked row holds a nonzero position (registration initializes pos_next = 0; a nonzero pos_held[0] implies pre-track booking)");
  let lagBad = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) if (Math.abs(nxt[i] - pos[i + 1]) > 1e-4) { lagBad = true; break; }
  if (lagBad) fails.push("log: pos_held[t] != pos_next_decided[t-1] somewhere (1-bar decision-lag violation)");
  if (Math.abs(nxt[n - 1] - Number(state["pos_next"] ?? 0.0)) > 1e-4) fails.push("state: pos_next != last row's pos_next_decided");
  const dposExp = pos.map((p, i) => Math.abs(p - (i === 0 ? 0.0 : pos[i - 1])));
  if (dpos.some((d, i) => Math.abs(d - dposExp[i]) > 5e-4)) fails.push("log: dpos != |pos_held[t] - pos_held[t-1]| somewhere");
  const costExp = dposExp.map((d, i) => d * COST / close[i]);
  if (cost.some((c, i) => Math.abs(c - costExp[i]) > 5e-6)) fails.push("log: cost_frac != dpos * 0.3625 / close somewhere");

  // equity chain: checked at EVERY row (per-row-chain revision)
  let eq = START_EQUITY, unver = 0, sinceSync = 0, chainFail = false;
  let prevClose: number | null = null, prevEnd: number | null = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const contiguous = prevEnd !== null && starts[i] === prevEnd;
    if (contiguous && prevClose !== null) {
      const ret = close[i] / prevClose - 1.0;
      eq *= (1.0 + pos[i] * ret - cost[i]);
      sinceSync++;
      const tolI = CHAIN_FLAT_TOL + CHAIN_ROW_TOL * sinceSync;
      const loggedI = Number(log[i]["equity_after"]);
      if (Math.abs(eq - loggedI) > tolI && !chainFail) {
        chainFail = true;
        fails.push(`log: equity chain mismatch at row ${i} (recomputed ${eq.toFixed(2)} vs logged ${loggedI.toFixed(2)}, tol ${tolI.toFixed(2)})`);
      }
    } else {
      unver++;
      eq = Number(log[i]["equity_after"]); // resume from the log
      sinceSync = 0;
    }
    prevClose = close[i]; prevEnd = ends[i];
  }
  if (unver) notes.push(`${unver} row(s) ret-unverifiable (gap before the row: skipped holiday bucket or first booked row - the chain resumes from the logged equity there, by design)`);
  if (Math.abs(Number(state["equity"] ?? 0.0) - Number(log[n - 1]["equity_after"])) > 0.02) fails.push("state: equity != last row's equity_after");
  const lb = state["last_booked"];
  if (lb === null || lb === undefined || ts(lb as string) !== ends[n - 1]) fails.push("state: last_booked != last row's bar_end");
  if (report !== null && report["last_booked"] !== null && report["last_booked"] !== undefined && report["last_booked"] !== "") {
    if (ts(report["last_booked"] as string) !== ends[n - 1]) warns.push("run report last_booked != last log row (likely a stale report from before the latest booking run; harmless if the nightly run follows)");
  }
  return { verdict: fails.length ? "FAIL" : "CLEAN", n_booked: n, failures: fails, warnings: warns, notes };
}

// ---- selftest: faithful port (synthetic 6-bar clean track, 12 seeded violations) ----
function selftest(): { passed: string[]; failed: string[] } {
  const passed: string[] = [], failed: string[] = [];
  const base = Date.parse("2026-11-12T18:00:00-05:00"); // 18:00 ET anchor
  const closes = [20000.0, 20040.0, 20090.0, 20060.0, 20110.0, 20160.0];
  const states = ["up_pressure", "up_pressure", "up_pressure", "up_pressure", "reversal_down", "reversal_down"];
  const tgts = states.map(s => s !== "reversal_down" ? VOL_TGT : DEF_TGT);
  const moms = [0.0012, 0.0026, 0.0041, 0.0052, 0.0033, 0.0021];
  const vol8 = [0.4431, 0.4398, 0.4231, 0.4187, 0.4512, 0.4466];
  const sizes = moms.map((m, i) => m > 0 ? Math.round(Math.min(CAP, tgts[i] / vol8[i]) * 1e4) / 1e4 : 0.0);
  const posHeld = [0.0, ...sizes.slice(0, -1)];
  const rows: Row[] = [];
  let eq = 100000.0, prevPos = 0.0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const t0 = base + 4 * 3600000 * i, t1 = t0 + 4 * 3600000;
    const p = posHeld[i];
    const d = Math.abs(p - prevPos);
    const c = Math.round(d * COST / closes[i] * 1e6) / 1e6;
    const ret = i ? closes[i] / closes[i - 1] - 1 : 0.0;
    if (i) eq *= (1.0 + p * ret - c);
    rows.push({ bar_start: isoET(t0), bar_end: isoET(t1), close: closes[i], state4h: states[i], vol8h_ann: vol8[i], tgt: tgts[i], mom12h: Math.round(moms[i] * 1e4) / 1e4, pos_held: p, dpos: Math.round(d * 1e4) / 1e4, cost_frac: c, equity_after: Math.round(eq * 100) / 100, pos_next_decided: sizes[i] });
    prevPos = p;
  }
  const state: State = { track_start: "2026-11-12T02:42:18.836823+01:00", equity: Math.round(eq * 100) / 100, last_booked: rows[5]["bar_end"] as string, pos_next: Math.round(sizes[5] * 1e6) / 1e6, pos_prev: Math.round(posHeld[5] * 1e6) / 1e6 };
  const report: Report = { status: "OK", last_booked: rows[5]["bar_end"] as string };

  const r = auditTrack(state as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, rows, report);
  if (r.verdict === "CLEAN" && r.n_booked === 6) passed.push("C0 clean synthetic track audits CLEAN (n=6)");
  else failed.push(`C0 clean track: ${r.verdict} / failures ${JSON.stringify(r.failures)}`);

  const clone = (o: unknown) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  const expectFail = (mutate: (st: State, lg: Row[]) => void, label: string, name: string) => {
    const st2 = clone(state) as State, lg2 = clone(rows) as Row[];
    mutate(st2, lg2);
    const rr = auditTrack(st2 as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, lg2, report);
    if (rr.verdict === "FAIL" && rr.failures.some(f => f.includes(label))) passed.push(`${name} caught ("${label}")`);
    else failed.push(`${name}: verdict ${rr.verdict}, failures ${JSON.stringify(rr.failures)} (wanted "${label}")`);
  };

  expectFail(s => { s.track_start = "2019-01-01T00:00:00+01:00"; }, "predates registration", "C1 pre-registration track_start");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[3]["bar_start"] = lg[2]["bar_start"]; }, "duplicate bar keys", "C2 duplicate bar keys");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[2]["pos_held"] = 1.7; }, "outside [0, 1]", "C3 cap violation");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[4]["tgt"] = 0.20; }, "tgt/state4h mismatch", "C4 tgt/state mismatch");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[1]["pos_held"] = 0.3; }, "1-bar decision-lag violation", "C5 1-bar lag violation");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[3]["dpos"] = 0.9; }, "dpos !=", "C6 dpos identity");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[3]["cost_frac"] = 0.5; }, "cost_frac !=", "C7 cost identity");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[4]["equity_after"] = Number(lg[4]["equity_after"]) - 50.0; }, "equity chain mismatch", "C8 interior equity corruption");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[2]["bar_start"] = isoET(ts(lg[2]["bar_start"] as string) + 3600000); }, "anchor hour", "C9 anchor hour");
  expectFail(s => { s.equity = 999.0; }, "equity != last row", "C10 state equity");
  expectFail(s => { s.last_booked = null; }, "last_booked !=", "C11 last_booked coherence");
  expectFail((_, lg) => { lg[5]["mom12h"] = -0.0025; }, "long-only violation", "C12 long-only decision");

  const r0 = auditTrack({ track_start: "2026-11-12T02:42:18+01:00", equity: 100000.0, last_booked: null, pos_next: 0.0, pos_prev: 0.0 }, null, null);
  if (r0.verdict === "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS") passed.push("C13 zero-booking state reads CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS");
  else failed.push(`C13 zero-booking: ${r0.verdict} ${JSON.stringify(r0.failures)}`);

  return { passed, failed };
}

async function main() {
  const t = selftest();
  // real zero-booking bundle state (data/v4h/v4h_state.json + v4h_run_report.json,
  // read this session: 0 bars booked, log absent, first nightly run tonight)
  const realState = { track_start: "2026-11-12T02:42:18.836823+01:00", equity: 100000.0, last_booked: null, pos_next: 0.0, pos_prev: 0.0 };
  const realReport = { status: "OK", as_of: "2026-11-11T20:52:57.814219-05:00", booked_this_run: 0, first_booked: null, last_booked: null, flips_this_run: 0, churn_this_run: 0.0, warnings: [] };
  const rr = auditTrack(realState, null, realReport);
  const pass = t.failed.length === 0 && rr.verdict === "CLEAN_NO_BOOKINGS";
  return {
    instrument: "v4h_audit TS mirror (session 56, 12 Nov 2026; per-row-chain revision)",
    selftest: { checks_run: 14, checks_passed: t.passed.length, checks_failed: t.failed.length, passed: t.passed, failed: t.failed, verdict: t.failed.length === 0 ? "MIRROR SELFTEST PASS" : "MIRROR SELFTEST FAIL" },
    real_state: { verdict: rr.verdict, n_booked: rr.n_booked, notes: rr.notes, failures: rr.failures },
    defect_found: "the session-51 instrument's terminal-only equity-chain check was blind to interior equity_after corruption (m8); v4h_audit.py fixed to a per-row chain check BEFORE its first real run",
    mirror_limitations: [
      "TS mirror validates the audit SEMANTICS (every check and seeded violation), not the pandas implementation itself",
      "the pandas selftest still runs first on the next Python-enabled reset, BEFORE --recompute use and BEFORE any verify_all wiring (session-51 rule: a check that has never run does not belong in the chain)",
      "recompute_crosscheck (network mode) not mirrored: no bookings exist yet, nothing to cross-check",
    ],
    verdict: pass ? "MIRROR PASS" : "MIRROR FAIL",
  };
}
main();

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/v4h_audit_ts_mirror.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/v4h_bot.py (14415 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""V4h live paper bot: rule set V4h on CME-anchored 4h NQ bars (experimental).

Spec: docs/v4h_spec.md (REGISTERED 2026-11-12, frozen BEFORE the first live
run). This engine is the rule's ONLY implementation and booking machinery.

What it does every run (idempotent; safe to run at any time, any frequency):
  1. fetch fresh NQ=F hourly + daily bars from the Yahoo v8 chart endpoint,
  2. build globex-anchored 4h bars (18:00 ET anchor, 30/week),
  3. compute the 4h AVFI state + the V4h signal/sizing series,
  4. book every COMPLETED 4h bar since the last run (append-only log,
     close-to-close 1-bar lag, |dsize| x 0.3625 pts cost, multiplicative
     equity from 100,000 USD at track start; NO bar completed before the
     track start is EVER booked),
  5. compute the weekly T/U3/V context card from fresh dailies
     (OBSERVATION ONLY: the weekly stage 7 Sunday session remains the
     sole booking machinery for the weekly rules),
  6. write data/v4h/v4h_card.json + v4h_run_report.json and print the card.

Discipline (hard-coded): no backtest, no historical booking, no
performance statistic on any pre-track window, no p-value, holdout
irrelevant by construction, paper only. A failed fetch aborts the run
WITHOUT touching state (the next run catches up).
"""
import json
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

BUNDLE = Path("/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline")
V4H_DIR = BUNDLE / "data" / "v4h"
STATE_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_state.json"
LOG_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_log.csv"
CARD_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_card.json"
REPORT_F = V4H_DIR / "v4h_run_report.json"

sys.path.insert(0, str(BUNDLE / "strategies"))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi  # noqa: E402

# frozen constants (docs/v4h_spec.md)
MOM_LEN, VOL_WIN, BARS_PER_WEEK = 12, 8, 30
VOL_TGT, DEF_TGT, CAP, COST = 0.20, 0.15, 1.0, 0.3625
ANN = np.sqrt(BARS_PER_WEEK * 52.0)          # sqrt(1560)
GRACE_MIN = 30                               # data-settlement grace, minutes
ANCHOR_HOUR = 18                             # globex session open, ET clock
ET, MALTZ = "America/New_York", "Europe/Malta"
UA = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (research)"}


def die(msg):
    print(json.dumps({"status": "ABORT", "reason": msg}, indent=2))
    sys.exit(1)


def fetch(interval, period1, period2, retries=3):
    url = (f"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/NQ=F"
           f"?period1={int(period1)}&period2={int(period2)}"
           f"&interval={interval}&includeAdjustedClose=false")
    last = None
    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=90) as fh:
                payload = json.load(fh)
            res = payload["chart"]["result"][0]
            q = res["indicators"]["quote"][0]
            df = pd.DataFrame({
                "ts": pd.to_datetime(res["timestamp"], unit="s", utc=True).tz_convert(ET),
                "open": q["open"], "high": q["high"],
                "low": q["low"], "close": q["close"], "volume": q["volume"],
            }).dropna(subset=["open", "high", "low", "close"])
            return df[(df[["open", "high", "low", "close"]] > 0).all(axis=1)]
        except Exception as e:                      # noqa: BLE001
            last = e
            time.sleep(3 * (attempt + 1))
    raise RuntimeError(f"yahoo fetch failed after {retries} tries: {last}")


def build_4h(h1):
    """Globex-anchored 4h buckets of hourly bars (labeled by bucket start)."""
    h = h1.copy()
    h["hour"] = h["ts"].dt.floor("h")
    off = (h["hour"].dt.hour - ANCHOR_HOUR) % 24
    h["bstart"] = h["hour"] - pd.to_timedelta(off % 4, unit="h")
    g = h.groupby("bstart")
    bars = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": g["bstart"].first(),
        "open": g["open"].first(), "high": g["high"].max(),
        "low": g["low"].min(), "close": g["close"].last(),
        "volume": g["volume"].sum(), "nh": g.size(),
    }).reset_index(drop=True)
    bars["bend"] = bars["date"] + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)
    return bars


def build_weekly(d1):
    """W-FRI weekly bars from dailies, stage-0c method (context card only)."""
    d = d1.copy()
    d["date"] = d["ts"].dt.floor("D")
    d = d.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    rep = 0
    for i, row in d.iterrows():
        if row["close"] > row["high"]:
            d.at[i, "close"] = row["high"]; rep += 1
        elif row["close"] < row["low"]:
            d.at[i, "close"] = row["low"]; rep += 1
    d["week"] = d["date"] + pd.to_timedelta((4 - d["date"].dt.dayofweek) % 7, unit="D")
    g = d.groupby("week")
    wk = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": g["week"].first(),
        "open": g["open"].first(), "high": g["high"].max(),
        "low": g["low"].min(), "close": g["close"].last(),
        "volume": g["volume"].sum(),
    }).reset_index(drop=True)
    return wk, rep


def main():
    now = pd.Timestamp.now(tz=ET)
    now_malta = pd.Timestamp.now(tz=MALTZ)
    grace = pd.Timedelta(minutes=GRACE_MIN)
    V4H_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---------- state ----------
    if STATE_F.exists():
        st = json.loads(STATE_F.read_text())
    else:
        st = {"track_start": now_malta.isoformat(), "equity": 100000.0,
              "last_booked": None, "pos_next": 0.0, "pos_prev": 0.0}
    for k, dflt in (("pos_next", 0.0), ("pos_prev", 0.0)):
        st.setdefault(k, dflt)
    track_start = pd.Timestamp(st["track_start"])
    equity = float(st["equity"])

    # ---------- data ----------
    t_now = time.time()
    try:
        h1 = fetch("1h", t_now - 730 * 86400, t_now)
        time.sleep(2)                               # spacing between calls
        d1 = fetch("1d", 946684800, t_now)          # from 2000-01-01
    except Exception as e:                          # noqa: BLE001
        die(f"fetch failed; state untouched, nothing booked: {e}")
    if len(h1) < 6000 or len(d1) < 1000:
        die(f"insufficient data (h={len(h1)}, d={len(d1)}); state untouched")

    bars = build_4h(h1)
    wk, n_rep = build_weekly(d1)
    if len(bars) < 1200:
        die(f"insufficient 4h bars after build: {len(bars)}; state untouched")

    # ---------- V4h series (4h grid) ----------
    av4 = compute_avfi(bars)
    state4h = av4["state"]
    c = bars["close"]
    r4 = np.log(c / c.shift(1))
    mom12h = np.log(c / c.shift(MOM_LEN))
    t_long = mom12h > 0
    vol8h = r4.rolling(VOL_WIN).std(ddof=1) * ANN
    tgt4h = pd.Series(np.where(state4h == "reversal_down", DEF_TGT, VOL_TGT),
                      index=bars.index)
    long_ok = t_long & vol8h.notna() & (vol8h > 0)
    size4h = pd.Series(0.0, index=bars.index)
    size4h[long_ok] = np.minimum(CAP, tgt4h / vol8h)[long_ok]
    size4h = size4h.fillna(0.0)

    flip_i = None
    lc_mask = bars["bend"] <= (now - grace)
    completed_idx = [i for i in bars.index[lc_mask] if i >= VOL_WIN + MOM_LEN]
    lc = completed_idx[-1] if completed_idx else len(bars) - 1
    flip_i = lc
    while flip_i > 1 and bool(t_long.iloc[flip_i]) == bool(t_long.iloc[flip_i - 1]):
        flip_i -= 1
    v4h_since = str(bars["date"].iloc[flip_i])

    # ---------- booking: completed bars after track start / last booked ----------
    last_booked = (pd.Timestamp(st["last_booked"]) if st["last_booked"]
                   else None)
    if last_booked is not None and last_booked.tz is None:
        last_booked = last_booked.tz_localize(ET)
    cutoff = now - grace
    sel = bars["bend"] > track_start
    if last_booked is not None:
        sel &= bars["bend"] > last_booked
    sel &= bars["bend"] <= cutoff
    idx = [i for i in bars.index[sel] if i >= 1]

    n_booked, flips, churn, warn_short = 0, 0, 0.0, []
    pos_next = float(st["pos_next"])     # held during the next booked bar
    pos_prev = float(st["pos_prev"])    # held during the last booked bar
    rows = []
    for i in idx:
        ret = float(c.iloc[i] / c.iloc[i - 1] - 1.0)
        pos_this = pos_next              # decided at the previous booked close
        dpos = abs(pos_this - pos_prev)  # transition cost into this bar
        cost_frac = dpos * COST / float(c.iloc[i])
        equity *= (1.0 + pos_this * ret - cost_frac)
        rows.append({
            "bar_start": str(bars["date"].iloc[i].isoformat()),
            "bar_end": str(bars["bend"].iloc[i].isoformat()),
            "close": round(float(c.iloc[i]), 2),
            "state4h": str(state4h.iloc[i]),
            "vol8h_ann": round(float(vol8h.iloc[i]), 4) if pd.notna(vol8h.iloc[i]) else None,
            "tgt": float(tgt4h.iloc[i]),
            "mom12h": round(float(mom12h.iloc[i]), 4) if pd.notna(mom12h.iloc[i]) else None,
            "pos_held": round(pos_this, 4),
            "dpos": round(dpos, 4),
            "cost_frac": round(cost_frac, 6),
            "equity_after": round(equity, 2),
            "pos_next_decided": round(float(size4h.iloc[i]), 4),
        })
        if i >= 1 and (n_booked > 0 or last_booked is not None):
            if bool(t_long.iloc[i]) != bool(t_long.iloc[i - 1]):
                flips += 1
        if bars["nh"].iloc[i] < 3:
            warn_short.append(str(bars["date"].iloc[i].date()))
        churn += dpos
        pos_prev = pos_this
        pos_next = float(size4h.iloc[i])
        n_booked += 1

    if rows:
        log = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        log.to_csv(LOG_F, mode="a", header=not LOG_F.exists(), index=False)
        st["last_booked"] = str(bars["bend"].iloc[idx[-1]].isoformat())
    # ALWAYS persist state, even with nothing booked: this freezes
    # track_start at registration and keeps pos/equity durable. (Without
    # this, a track that had not booked yet would reset track_start to
    # each run's now and never book anything - caught in dry-run testing.)
    st.update({"track_start": str(track_start.isoformat()),
               "equity": round(equity, 2),
               "pos_next": round(pos_next, 6),
               "pos_prev": round(pos_prev, 6)})
    STATE_F.write_text(json.dumps(st, indent=2))

    # ---------- weekly context card (observation only) ----------
    avw = compute_avfi(wk)
    today = now.date()
    done = wk[wk["date"].dt.date < today]
    if len(done) < 13:
        die("insufficient completed weeks for the context card")
    p = int(done.index[-1])
    cw = done["close"]
    mom12w = float(np.log(cw.iloc[p] / cw.iloc[p - 12]))
    t_sig = mom12w > 0
    rets_w = np.log(cw / cw.shift(1)).iloc[p - VOL_WIN + 1: p + 1]
    vol8w = float(rets_w.std(ddof=1) * np.sqrt(52.0))
    stw = str(avw["state"].iloc[p])
    thr = 0.5 if stw == "reversal_down" else 1.0
    u3 = (min(1.0, 0.20 / vol8w) if vol8w > 0 else 1.0) * thr * (1.0 if t_sig else 0.0)
    v_tgt = 0.15 if stw == "reversal_down" else 0.20
    v_size = (min(1.0, v_tgt / vol8w) if vol8w > 0 else 1.0) if t_sig else 0.0
    sig_hist = (np.log(done["close"] / done["close"].shift(12)) > 0)
    j = p
    while j > 1 and bool(sig_hist.iloc[j]) == bool(sig_hist.iloc[j - 1]):
        j -= 1
    t_since = str(done["date"].iloc[j].date())

    card = {
        "status": "OK",
        "as_of_malta": str(now_malta.isoformat()),
        "as_of_et": str(now.isoformat()),
        "spec": "docs/v4h_spec.md (registered 2026-11-12; experimental; "
                "forward paper only; no p-value exists for V4h)",
        "v4h": {
            "track_start": str(track_start.isoformat()),
            "equity_usd": round(equity, 2),
            "bars_booked_total": (None if st.get("last_booked") is None else
                                  int(pd.read_csv(LOG_F).shape[0])) if LOG_F.exists() else 0,
            "booked_this_run": n_booked,
            "flips_this_run": flips,
            "churn_this_run": round(churn, 4),
            "position_for_next_bar": round(pos_next, 4),
            "size_decided_at_last_close": round(float(size4h.iloc[lc]), 4),
            "last_completed_4h_bar": {"bar_start": str(bars["date"].iloc[lc]),
                                      "decision_close_time": str(bars["bend"].iloc[lc])},
            "signal": "LONG" if bool(t_long.iloc[lc]) else "FLAT",
            "signal_since": v4h_since,
            "size_decided": round(float(size4h.iloc[lc]), 4),
            "mom12h_48h": round(float(mom12h.iloc[lc]), 4),
            "vol8h_ann": round(float(vol8h.iloc[lc]), 4),
            "tgt": float(tgt4h.iloc[lc]),
            "state4h": str(state4h.iloc[lc]),
            "forming_bar_now": {
                "bar_start": str(bars["date"].iloc[-1]),
                "size_if_closed_now": round(float(size4h.iloc[-1]), 4),
                "mom12h_if_closed_now": round(float(mom12h.iloc[-1]), 4),
                "vol8h_if_closed_now": round(float(vol8h.iloc[-1]), 4),
                "state4h_if_closed_now": str(state4h.iloc[-1]),
            },
        },
        "weekly_context": {
            "note": "observation only; the weekly stage 7 Sunday session "
                    "remains the sole booking machinery for the weekly rules",
            "last_completed_week": str(done["date"].iloc[p].date()),
            "last_weekly_close": round(float(cw.iloc[p]), 2),
            "T_signal": "LONG" if t_sig else "FLAT",
            "T_12wk_logret": round(mom12w, 4),
            "T_signal_since": t_since,
            "vol8_ann": round(vol8w, 4),
            "u3_size": round(float(u3), 3),
            "v_size": round(float(v_size), 3),
            "avfi_state": stw,
        },
        "market": {
            "last_price": round(float(h1["close"].iloc[-1]), 2),
            "last_hourly_bar_et": str(h1["ts"].iloc[-1]),
            "daily_repairs": n_rep,
        },
        "warnings": warn_short,
        "discipline": ("live 4h forward paper track; no backtest, no "
                       "historical booking, no p-value; holdout irrelevant "
                       "by construction; paper only"),
    }
    CARD_F.write_text(json.dumps(card, indent=2))
    REPORT_F.write_text(json.dumps(
        {"status": "OK", "as_of": str(now.isoformat()),
         "booked_this_run": n_booked,
         "first_booked": (rows[0]["bar_end"] if rows else None),
         "last_booked": (rows[-1]["bar_end"] if rows else st.get("last_booked")),
         "flips_this_run": flips, "churn_this_run": round(churn, 4),
         "warnings": warn_short}, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(card, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# One-command restore verification after an environment reset.
# Usage: bash verify_all.sh
# Runs the full reproduction chain and prints PASS/FAIL per stage.
set -uo pipefail
BUNDLE_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
# Reset-proof (session 8 feature, now durable in the bundle): if invoked from the
# bundle, rebuild the working tree from it before running the chain. The scripts
# hard-code REPO=/home/user/projects/quant, so the chain must run there.
if [ "$BUNDLE_DIR" != "/home/user/projects/quant" ]; then
  mkdir -p /home/user/projects/quant
  cp -a "$BUNDLE_DIR/." /home/user/projects/quant/
fi
cd /home/user/projects/quant
source /home/user/skills/data-analysis/python/library/activate.sh
FAIL=0

# src/utils: the meta-tool family (Sovereign Agent Protocol Phase 3)

Convention (from sovereign_agent_protocol_v1.md and frontier.md V9):

- Every new script or statistical test written for a front is accompanied,
  when a reusable abstraction is real, by a generic function in this
  directory: no parameters hidden inside the specific script, no copy-paste
  divergence.
- Meta-tools land only on Python-enabled sessions, with their own tests
  under pipeline/research/, wired into verify_all.sh where load-bearing.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/src/utils/base_rate.py (5769 bytes)
"""base_rate.py - the generic base-rate counter (meta-tool 1, mandated).

Landed 2026-11-19, session 134, under frontier.md V9 (Protocol Phase 3).

Law (bound into the code, not the convention):
- UNSEALED DATA ONLY. Any window that reaches 2021-01-01 or later is REFUSED
  by construction with SealedWindowError. The NQ 2021+ holdout is SPENT;
  GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX and BTC 2021+ windows are sealed. There is no flag, no
  override, no keyword argument that changes this.
- Descriptive reporting only: this tool counts, it never evaluates, scores,
  tunes, or licenses anything. Zero status by construction.
- The weekly event definition applied here is the frozen registered one
  (r1 log return; sig_w = rolling 8-week sample std ddof=1; the event week is
  |r1| >= 2.5 * sig_w on the following week). Reusing the definition is not
  re-testing it: no alternative definition, window, or constant is computed
  or compared anywhere in this module.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent  # pipeline/ bundle root

# The unsealed design horizon. NEVER MOVES. Any window end >= this is refused.
DESIGN_END = dt.date(2021, 1, 1)

# Frozen event-definition constants (registered machinery, reused verbatim).
SIGMA_WIN = 8
BAR_K = 2.5

# Registered weekly canonical files, all with a usable close series.
ASSETS = {
    "nq": {"path": "data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv", "date_col": "date"},
    "gold": {"path": "data/crossmarket_gold_weekly.csv", "date_col": "week_end"},
    "nikkei": {"path": "data/crossmarket_nikkei_weekly.csv", "date_col": "week_end"},
    "dax": {"path": "data/crossmarket_dax_weekly.csv", "date_col": "week_end"},
    "btc": {"path": "data/btc_weekly_iso.csv", "date_col": "date"},
    # Sealed-only and spent assets are deliberately NOT listed; unknown names
    # are refused with ValueError.
}


class SealedWindowError(PermissionError):
    """Raised when a requested window touches sealed or spent data."""


def _load(asset: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if asset not in ASSETS:
        raise ValueError(
            f"unknown asset {asset!r}; registered weekly series: {sorted(ASSETS)}"
        )
    spec = ASSETS[asset]
    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / spec["path"])
    df[spec["date_col"]] = pd.to_datetime(df[spec["date_col"]], utc=True)
    df = df.sort_values(spec["date_col"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(SIGMA_WIN).std(ddof=1)
    df["ev_next"] = (df["r1"].shift(-1).abs() >= BAR_K * df["sig_w"]).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["r1"].shift(-1).isna(), "ev_next"] = np.nan
    return df


def _check_window(window):
    try:
        start, end = window
        start = pd.Timestamp(start, tz="UTC")
        end = pd.Timestamp(end, tz="UTC")
    except Exception as exc:  # malformed window: refuse
        raise ValueError("window must be (start, end), both date-like") from exc
    # The seal is EXCLUSIVE-strict for the window END: end == 2021-01-01 is
    # the canonical design horizon (date < 2021-01-01, the frozen DESIGN_END
    # convention) and reads nothing at or after the seal. Any END AFTER the
    # seal is refused outright.
    if end.tz_convert("UTC").date() > DESIGN_END:
        raise SealedWindowError(
            "REFUSED by construction: the window end reaches past the "
            "2021-01-01 seal. Sealed and spent data (NQ 2021+ holdout SPENT; "
            "GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX/BTC 2021+ sealed) is never read by this tool. "
            "The lawful exclusive bound is 2021-01-01 itself (date < seal)."
        )
    if start >= end:
        raise ValueError("window start must precede window end")
    return start, end


def calculate_base_rate(asset: str, window, horizons=(1, 4, 12)) -> dict:
    """Weekly ev base rates for a registered asset over an UNSEALED window.

    Returns (reporting only, zero status):
      n_weeks_windowed   completed weeks inside the window
      n_valid           weeks with a valid sig_w (>= SIGMA_WIN prior weeks in window)
      weekly_rate       observed ev rate over valid weeks (ev_next notna)
      {h}_pool          pooled overlapping windows fully ending inside the
                        window: n_windows, cum (fraction with >=1 ev in the
                        next h weeks) - the climatology-style counter,
                        descriptive, no Greenwood band (no inferential claim)
    Refuses any window touching 2021+ with SealedWindowError.
    """
    start, end = _check_window(window)
    df = _load(asset)
    ho = (df[ASSETS[asset]["date_col"]] >= start) & (df[ASSETS[asset]["date_col"]] < end)
    sub = df[ho].reset_index(drop=True)
    if len(sub) < SIGMA_WIN + 2:
        raise ValueError(f"window too short for the frozen {SIGMA_WIN}-week estimator")
    valid = sub[sub["ev_next"].notna()].reset_index(drop=True)
    ev = valid["ev_next"].values
    out = {
        "asset": asset,
        "window": [str(start.date()), str(end.date())],
        "seal": "refused-by-construction at 2021-01-01",
        "n_weeks_windowed": int(len(sub)),
        "n_valid": int(len(valid)),
        "weekly_rate": float(ev.mean()),
    }
    evv = sub["ev_next"].values
    for h in horizons:
        n_win, n_hit = 0, 0
        for i in range(len(evv)):
            if np.isnan(evv[i]) or i + h >= len(evv):
                continue
            seg = evv[i + 1 : i + 1 + h]
            if np.isnan(seg).any():
                continue
            n_win += 1
            if seg.sum() > 0:
                n_hit += 1
        out[f"cum{h}_pool"] = {"n_windows": n_win, "cum": (n_hit / n_win) if n_win else None}
    return out

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/src/utils/base_rate.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/src/utils/estimator_stability.py (2021 bytes)
"""estimator_stability.py - the generic robustness harness (meta-tool 2).

Landed 2026-11-19, session 134, under frontier.md V9 (Protocol Phase 3).

Law (bound into the code, not the convention):
- REPORTING ONLY. This harness perturbs and re-samples; it never selects,
  never re-tunes, never proposes a change to any frozen definition, and its
  output licenses nothing. The no-re-tuning rule after any null is absolute.
- Deterministic: seeded stationary-block bootstrap; identical inputs give
  byte-identical outputs, so results are replayable record evidence.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np


def test_estimator_stability(data, estimator, n_boot=200, block=8, seed=42) -> dict:
    """Robustness report for an estimator over a one-dimensional series.

    estimator: a pure function np.ndarray -> float (applied to the series in
      the order given). It is called, never inspected or modified.
    data: 1-d array-like.
    Returns {"point", "boot_mean", "boot_sd", "band_lo", "band_hi", "n_boot",
    "block", "seed"} - a description of bootstrap variability, nothing more.
    """
    x = np.asarray(data, dtype=float)
    if x.ndim != 1 or x.size < block + 1:
        raise ValueError("data must be 1-d with length > block")
    if n_boot < 2:
        raise ValueError("n_boot must be >= 2")
    point = float(estimator(x))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = x.size
    n_blocks = int(np.ceil(n / block))
    stats = np.empty(n_boot, dtype=float)
    for b in range(n_boot):
        starts = rng.integers(0, n - block + 1, size=n_blocks)
        sample = np.concatenate([x[s : s + block] for s in starts])[:n]
        stats[b] = estimator(sample)
    return {
        "point": point,
        "boot_mean": float(stats.mean()),
        "boot_sd": float(stats.std(ddof=1)),
        "band_lo": float(np.percentile(stats, 2.5)),
        "band_hi": float(np.percentile(stats, 97.5)),
        "n_boot": int(n_boot),
        "block": int(block),
        "seed": int(seed),
    }

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/src/utils/estimator_stability.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_pgt_layer.py (6731 bytes)
"""AVFI Plane Geometry Terminal (PGT) layer, pandas replication (Amendment 5 draft).

Stage 1b deliverable. Detector on top of the REGISTERED core series only
(spread, sp_z, state from compute_avfi; mu_s/sd_s recomputed exactly as
the core does: pine_sma/pine_stdev of spread over normWin=500). No new
indicator math, no feedback into any prior series.

The hypothesis behind the flags (Andrea's "plane of axis" observation on
his NQ 4H chart, formalised for the weekly programme universe):

  Tops and bottoms show up as geometry between three reference planes
  of the effort axis: the zero agreement plane, the rolling regime plane
  mu_s, and the band planes mu_s +/- bandK*sd_s (i.e. sp_z = +/- bandK).

  G1 plane-exursion divergence (fresh price extreme where the effort
     plane refuses a fresh excursion): close makes a fresh pbN-week
     closing high, the most recent PRIOR fresh high was an upper-band
     plane excursion (sp_z >= bandK), and the current sp_z is strictly
     below that prior excursion's sp_z. Mirror for lows.
  G2 truncated oscillation (one-sided effort regime): at a fresh
     pbN-week extreme, sp_z reached the same-side band plane at least
     once within the last oscWin weeks and NEVER touched the opposite
     band plane in that window. Mirror for lows.
  G3 regime-plane re-cross failure: state is still momentum_up but the
     spread z falls back through the mu_s plane (sp_z < 0 after
     sp_z >= 0). Mirror: momentum_down with sp_z crossing above 0.

Registered defaults (Amendment 5 draft, DO NOT TUNE):
  pbN 20 (inherited from the H8 fresh-extreme convention),
  bandK 1.5 (core v3 constant, reused as the plane threshold),
  oscWin 26, strict planes (no tolerance).

compute_pgt_layer(base, **overrides) -> DataFrame aligned to base,
where `base` is a compute_avfi() output frame.

Discipline: observation-only flags. No forward returns and no event
evaluation live in this module. The spent 2021+ holdout is never
evaluated here; any PGT event study runs only on unsealed data
(pre-2021, effective window limited by sp_z validity) and only after
Andrea signs a dated Amendment 5. Family relationship to the closed H8
(divergence) and H9 (climax) tracks is acknowledged and must be counted
in any multiplicity control.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from avfi_replication import pine_sma, pine_stdev

PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS = {
    "pbN": 20,      # fresh-extreme lookback (H8 convention)
    "bandK": 1.5,   # band plane threshold (core v3 constant)
    "oscWin": 26,   # truncated-oscillation window (weeks)
    "normWin": 500, # core normalization window
}


def _fresh_extreme_chains(new_extreme: pd.Series, sp_z: pd.Series):
    """Indices of the prior bar in each fresh-extreme run.

    A run of consecutive fresh-extreme bars is one excursion event; the
    'prior fresh extreme' for bar t is the LAST bar of the previous run.
    Returns (prior_idx, prior_spz) aligned to new_extreme's index:
    prior_idx is float nan where no prior run exists.
    """
    ne = new_extreme.to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    z = sp_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    m = len(ne)
    prior_idx = np.full(m, np.nan)
    prior_spz = np.full(m, np.nan)
    last_run_end = -1   # index of the last bar of the previous run
    in_run = False
    for i in range(m):
        if ne[i]:
            if not in_run:
                # start of a new run: stamp the previous run's end
                if last_run_end >= 0 and not np.isnan(z[last_run_end]):
                    prior_idx[i] = last_run_end
                    prior_spz[i] = z[last_run_end]
                in_run = True
            # extend this run's end
            last_run_end = i
        else:
            in_run = False
    return (pd.Series(prior_idx, index=new_extreme.index),
            pd.Series(prior_spz, index=new_extreme.index))


def compute_pgt_layer(base: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Full PGT layer from a compute_avfi() output frame."""
    p = dict(PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    p.update(overrides)
    spread = base["spread"]
    close = base["close"]
    state = base["state"]

    mu_s = pine_sma(spread, p["normWin"])
    sd_s = pine_stdev(spread, p["normWin"])
    # Parity: sp_z is already in base; recompute only to assert identity.
    sp_z = base["sp_z"]

    # Fresh pbN-week closing extremes (same convention as the tb layer).
    hh = close.rolling(p["pbN"], min_periods=p["pbN"]).max()
    ll = close.rolling(p["pbN"], min_periods=p["pbN"]).min()
    new_high = hh.notna() & (close >= hh)
    new_low = ll.notna() & (close <= ll)

    # G1: plane-exursion divergence at fresh extremes.
    hi_prior_idx, hi_prior_spz = _fresh_extreme_chains(new_high, sp_z)
    lo_prior_idx, lo_prior_spz = _fresh_extreme_chains(new_low, sp_z)
    g1_top = (new_high & sp_z.notna() & hi_prior_spz.notna()
              & (hi_prior_spz >= p["bandK"])
              & (sp_z.to_numpy(float) < hi_prior_spz.to_numpy(float)))
    g1_bot = (new_low & sp_z.notna() & lo_prior_spz.notna()
              & (lo_prior_spz <= -p["bandK"])
              & (sp_z.to_numpy(float) > lo_prior_spz.to_numpy(float)))

    # G2: truncated oscillation (one-sided effort regime at extremes).
    z_max = sp_z.rolling(p["oscWin"], min_periods=p["oscWin"]).max()
    z_min = sp_z.rolling(p["oscWin"], min_periods=p["oscWin"]).min()
    g2_top = (new_high & z_max.notna() & z_min.notna()
              & (z_max >= p["bandK"]) & (z_min > -p["bandK"]))
    g2_bot = (new_low & z_max.notna() & z_min.notna()
              & (z_min <= -p["bandK"]) & (z_max < p["bandK"]))

    # G3: regime-plane re-cross failure while the state machine lags.
    crossed_dn = (sp_z < 0) & (sp_z.shift(1) >= 0)
    crossed_up = (sp_z > 0) & (sp_z.shift(1) <= 0)
    g3_top = crossed_dn & (state == "momentum_up")
    g3_bot = crossed_up & (state == "momentum_down")

    pgt_top = g1_top | g2_top | g3_top
    pgt_bot = g1_bot | g2_bot | g3_bot

    return pd.DataFrame({
        "date": base["date"], "close": close,
        "spread": spread, "mu_s": mu_s, "sd_s": sd_s, "sp_z": sp_z,
        "state": state,
        "newHigh": new_high, "newLow": new_low,
        "hiPriorIdx": hi_prior_idx, "hiPriorSpz": hi_prior_spz,
        "loPriorIdx": lo_prior_idx, "loPriorSpz": lo_prior_spz,
        "zMaxWin": z_max, "zMinWin": z_min,
        "g1Top": g1_top, "g1Bot": g1_bot,
        "g2Top": g2_top, "g2Bot": g2_bot,
        "g3Top": g3_top, "g3Bot": g3_bot,
        "pgtTop": pgt_top, "pgtBot": pgt_bot,
    })


def pgt_from_avfi(df: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Convenience: canonical bars -> compute_avfi -> PGT layer."""
    from avfi_replication import compute_avfi
    return compute_pgt_layer(compute_avfi(df), **overrides)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_pgt_layer.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_replication.py (5451 bytes)
"""AVFI v3 / v2 replication in pandas, matching the Pine reference exactly.

Stage 1 deliverable. Conventions:
  - EWM: halflife decay, adjust=False semantics, na-tolerant (skips na inputs,
    carries state) exactly like the Pine ewm() function.
  - pine_ema: ta.ema with alpha = 2/(len+1), seeded at the first non-na value.
  - lag [1] on baseline EWMs is applied as shift(1) AFTER the na-tolerant EWM.
  - pine_sma / pine_stdev: rolling mean and SAMPLE stdev (ddof=1),
    min_periods=n. This ddof=1 is THE PROGRAMME convention (validated by
    stage 1 and used by every registered study). Note: Pine's default
    ta.stdev is biased/population (ddof=0); the Pine chart matches this
    reference only via ta.stdev(..., false) - see avfi_v331_research.pine
    (v3.3.1 parity fix).
  - warmup: indicator values are na until bar_index >= warmup.
  - states are gated on spread/emaV2 validity (not on sp_z availability).

compute_avfi(df, **overrides) -> DataFrame of series aligned to df.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

DEFAULT_PARAMS = {
    "hlVol": 30.0, "hlFlow": 65.0, "k": 1.0, "deadZone": 0.2, "gamma": 2.5,
    "smooth": 3, "warmup": 260, "emaLen": 105, "normWin": 500, "bandK": 1.5,
}
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2 = 2.772588722239781
EPS = 1e-12


def ewm_halflife(x: pd.Series, hl: float) -> pd.Series:
    """Na-tolerant EWM with halflife hl, adjust=False (Pine ewm)."""
    alpha = 1.0 - 0.5 ** (1.0 / hl)
    arr = x.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    out = np.full(len(arr), np.nan)
    e = np.nan
    for i, v in enumerate(arr):
        if not np.isnan(v):
            e = v if np.isnan(e) else e + alpha * (v - e)
        out[i] = e
    return pd.Series(out, index=x.index)


def pine_ema(x: pd.Series, length: int) -> pd.Series:
    """ta.ema: alpha=2/(len+1), seeded at first non-na, then recursive."""
    alpha = 2.0 / (length + 1.0)
    arr = x.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    out = np.full(len(arr), np.nan)
    e = np.nan
    for i, v in enumerate(arr):
        if not np.isnan(v):
            e = v if np.isnan(e) else e + alpha * (v - e)
        out[i] = e
    return pd.Series(out, index=x.index)


def pine_sma(x: pd.Series, n: int) -> pd.Series:
    return x.rolling(n, min_periods=n).mean()


def pine_stdev(x: pd.Series, n: int) -> pd.Series:
    return x.rolling(n, min_periods=n).std(ddof=1)


def _w_of(z: pd.Series, k: float, dead_zone: float) -> pd.Series:
    xx = k * np.maximum(0.0, z.abs() - dead_zone)
    xx_c = np.clip(xx, -20.0, 20.0)
    e2x = np.exp(2.0 * xx_c)
    th = (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)
    return th * np.sign(z)


def compute_avfi(df: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    p = dict(DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    p.update(overrides)
    high, low = df["high"], df["low"]
    vol = df["volume"].astype(float)
    tp = (high + low + df["close"]) / 3.0  # hlc3

    r = pd.Series(np.where((tp > 0) & (tp.shift(1) > 0),
                           np.log(tp / tp.shift(1)), 0.0), index=df.index)

    # AVFI v3
    absr = ewm_halflife(r.abs(), p["hlVol"])
    sigma_r = SQRT_PI_2 * absr
    loghl2 = ewm_halflife(np.log(np.maximum(high / low, 1.0)) ** 2, p["hlVol"])
    sigma_pk = np.sqrt(loghl2 / FOUR_LN2)
    sigma3 = np.sqrt(np.maximum(sigma_r ** 2, sigma_pk ** 2))
    z3 = r / (sigma3 + EPS)
    w3 = _w_of(z3, p["k"], p["deadZone"])

    lv = pd.Series(np.where(vol > 0, np.log(np.where(vol > 0, vol, 1.0)), np.nan),
                   index=df.index)
    lva = ewm_halflife(lv, p["hlFlow"]).shift(1)
    va = np.exp(lva)
    rho3 = pd.Series(np.where(vol > 0, vol / (va + EPS), 0.0), index=df.index)
    m3 = rho3 / (1.0 + rho3 / p["gamma"])
    dv3 = w3 * m3 * va
    flow3 = ewm_halflife(dv3, p["hlFlow"])
    avfi3_ = pine_ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), p["smooth"])

    # AVFI v2
    z2 = r / (sigma_r + EPS)
    w2 = _w_of(z2, p["k"], p["deadZone"])
    vave2 = ewm_halflife(vol, p["hlFlow"]).shift(1)
    rho2 = pd.Series(np.where(vol > 0, vol / (vave2 + EPS), 0.0), index=df.index)
    m2 = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / p["gamma"])
    flow2 = ewm_halflife(w2 * m2 * vave2, p["hlFlow"])
    avfi2_ = pine_ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), p["smooth"])

    warmup = p["warmup"]
    idx = np.arange(len(df))
    avfi3 = pd.Series(np.where(idx >= warmup, avfi3_, np.nan), index=df.index)
    avfi2 = pd.Series(np.where(idx >= warmup, avfi2_, np.nan), index=df.index)
    ema_v2 = pine_ema(avfi2, p["emaLen"])

    spread = avfi3 - ema_v2
    mu_s = pine_sma(spread, p["normWin"])
    sd_s = pine_stdev(spread, p["normWin"])
    sp_z = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

    valid = spread.notna() & ema_v2.notna()
    state = pd.Series(np.where(valid,
                               np.select(
                                   [(spread >= 0) & (ema_v2 >= 0),
                                    (spread < 0) & (ema_v2 >= 0),
                                    (spread < 0) & (ema_v2 < 0),
                                    (spread >= 0) & (ema_v2 < 0)],
                                   ["momentum_up", "reversal_down",
                                    "momentum_down", "reversal_up"],
                                   default=""),
                               ""), index=df.index)

    out = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": df["date"], "close": df["close"], "volume": vol,
        "sigma3": sigma3, "z3": z3, "w3": w3, "rho3": rho3,
        "flow3": flow3, "avfi3": avfi3, "avfi2": avfi2, "emaV2": ema_v2,
        "spread": spread, "sp_z": sp_z, "state": state,
    })
    return out

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_replication.py
