// ── v4 curvature layer (descriptive) ───────────────────────────────
grpCurv   = "Spread curvature (descriptive; H8 fade family tested null)"
showCurv  = input.bool(false, "Curvature line (curvature/sd_s, clamped ±3)",    group=grpCurv)

// ── v4 extremity exports (Amendment 11 DRAFT, do not tune) ────────
grpExt    = "Extremity exports (Amendment 11 DRAFT; descriptive, unevaluated)"
extMinCov = input.int(100,  "Pctile min valid prior bars (coverage gate)", minval=10, group=grpExt)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781
K_YZ      = 0.34 / 2.34   // Yang-Zhang 2000 optimal weight, large-n limit
SPZ_EXT   = 2.0           // |spread z| extremity threshold (Amd 11 draft)
RZ_EXT    = 2.0           // |robust z| extremity threshold (Amd 11 draft)
RHO_EXT   = 2.0           // volume-surprise burst threshold (Amd 11 draft)
ASYM_HI   = 2.0           // down/up vol asymmetry high tail (Amd 11 draft)
ASYM_LO   = 0.5           // down/up vol asymmetry low tail (Amd 11 draft)

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Prior-bar percentile rank helper (v4; non-repainting core math) ─
// Counts, among the `win` PRIOR bars (current bar excluded by
// construction: the loop starts at i = 1), the share of valid values
// strictly below the current one. Coverage gate: na until at least
// `minCov` valid prior values exist. This is a RANK, not a quantile:
// no interpolation, no current-bar self-inclusion.
pctRank(float src, int win, int minCov) =>
    float result = na
    if not na(src)
        int cnt = 0
        int tot = 0
        for i = 1 to win
            float v = src[i]
            if not na(v)
                tot += 1
                if v < src
                    cnt += 1
        result := tot >= minCov ? cnt * 1.0 / tot : na
    result

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity per v3.3.1 finding
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v3.4 MEASUREMENT LAYERS (unchanged by v4; everything above untouched)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 1. Garman-Klass OHLC vol floor.
float u_gk   = math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0))
float c_gk   = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : 0.0
float varGk  = math.max(0.5 * u_gk * u_gk - (2.0 * math.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk * c_gk, 0.0)
float sigmaGK  = math.sqrt(math.max(ewm(varGk, hlVol), EPS))
float sigma3GK = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaGK * sigmaGK))

// 2. Close-location-value flow variant (Amendment 4 withdrawn; descriptive).
float clv      = math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, (2.0 * close - high - low) / math.max(high - low, EPS)))
float dv3Clv   = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + qK * clv)
float flow3Clv = ewm(dv3Clv, hlFlow)
float avfi3Clv_ = ta.ema(flow3Clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
float avfi3Clv  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3Clv_ : na
float spreadClv = avfi3Clv - emaV2
float mu_c  = ta.sma(spreadClv, normWin)
float sd_c  = ta.stdev(spreadClv, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity
float spZClv = (spreadClv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

// 3. Up/down vol asymmetry (descriptive only).
float absR   = math.abs(r)
float absRUp = r > 0 ? absR : na
float absRDn = r < 0 ? absR : na
float sigmaUp = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRUp, hlVol)
float sigmaDn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRDn, hlVol)
float asym    = sigmaDn / (sigmaUp + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: YANG-ZHANG VOL FLOOR (Tsay ch.3; PK -> GK -> YZ)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Yang-Zhang (2000): σ²_YZ = σ²_o + k·σ²_c + (1−k)·σ²_RS with the
// paper's optimal k = 0.34/(1.34+(n+1)/(n−1)) → 0.34/2.34 at large n.
// o = ln(O/C[1]) overnight gap; c = ln(C/O); Rogers-Satchell per-bar
// rs = u(u−c) + d(d−c) with u = ln(H/O), d = ln(L/O), non-negative by
// construction (u >= 0 >= d; H >= C, L <= C inside the range); the
// clamp guards data errors only. EWM(hlVol) adaptation as in the GK
// layer: per-bar raw second moments (drift² is second-order at the
// weekly horizon, the same approximation the GK layer makes).
// Adds what GK lacks: overnight gaps and drift robustness. DESCRIPTIVE
// FLOOR ONLY; feeds nothing back.
float oYz = open > 0 and not na(close[1]) and close[1] > 0 ? math.log(open / close[1]) : na
float cYz = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : na
float uYz = open > 0 ? math.log(high / open) : na
float dYz = open > 0 ? math.log(low / open) : na
float rsBar = na(uYz) or na(cYz) or na(dYz) ? na : math.max(uYz * (uYz - cYz) + dYz * (dYz - cYz), 0.0)
float varOYz = ewm(oYz * oYz, hlVol)
float varCYz = ewm(cYz * cYz, hlVol)
float varRS  = ewm(rsBar, hlVol)
float sigmaYZ = math.sqrt(math.max(varOYz + K_YZ * varCYz + (1.0 - K_YZ) * varRS, 0.0))
float sigma3YZ = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaYZ * sigmaYZ))

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: SPREAD VELOCITY + CURVATURE (second derivative)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// velocity = first difference of the spread; curvature = second
// difference. HONEST CAVEAT: the spread is near-integrated (lag-1
// ~0.985), so differences are small and noisy; curvature is noise-
// dominated at the weekly grain and NO reversal claim is licensed by
// it. The fade-at-extremes family (H8) was executed and returned
// NULL. Descriptive display only; curvZ = curvature / sd_s is a
// scale-free DISPLAY normalization, not a registered series.
float velocity  = ta.change(spread)
float curvature = ta.change(velocity)
float curvZ     = na(curvature) or na(sd_s) ? na : curvature / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (v3.2; weekly-chart logic, observation only)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution). REGISTERED
// nearest-rank percentiles; the linear-interpolation substitution is
// REFUSED (post-null registered definition, H8/H9 executed on it).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft flags (executed NULL; descriptive only)
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft flags (executed NULL; descriptive only)
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (prior-bar moments only: mu_s[1], sd_s[1]).
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── PGT plane-geometry terminal layer (v3.5; Amendment 5 withdrawn) ─
bool zWinOK  = not na(sp_z[oscWin - 1])
float zMax26 = zWinOK ? ta.highest(sp_z, oscWin) : na
float zMin26 = zWinOK ? ta.lowest(sp_z, oscWin)  : na

var float hiRunEndSpz = na
var bool  hiInRun = false
var float loRunEndSpz = na
var bool  loInRun = false
float hiPriorSpz = na
float loPriorSpz = na
if newHigh and not hiInRun
    hiPriorSpz := hiRunEndSpz
if newLow and not loInRun
    loPriorSpz := loRunEndSpz

bool g1Top = newHigh and not na(sp_z) and not na(hiPriorSpz) and hiPriorSpz >= bandK and sp_z < hiPriorSpz
bool g1Bot = newLow  and not na(sp_z) and not na(loPriorSpz) and loPriorSpz <= -bandK and sp_z > loPriorSpz
bool g2Top = newHigh and not na(zMax26) and zMax26 >= bandK and zMin26 > -bandK
bool g2Bot = newLow  and not na(zMin26) and zMin26 <= -bandK and zMax26 < bandK
bool crossedDn = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z < 0 and sp_z[1] >= 0
bool crossedUp = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z > 0 and sp_z[1] <= 0
bool g3Top = crossedDn and stateNow == "Momentum up"
bool g3Bot = crossedUp and stateNow == "Momentum down"
bool pgtTop = g1Top or g2Top or g3Top
bool pgtBot = g1Bot or g2Bot or g3Bot

if newHigh
    hiInRun := true
    hiRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    hiInRun := false
if newLow
    loInRun := true
    loRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    loInRun := false

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) parity.
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (throttle regime; U3 failed its holdout test, parallel-logged)
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: SMH COMPRESSION STATE (registered definition)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// sig_w8 = sqrt(r_var): the 8-week sample stdev of weekly log returns
// BEFORE annualization — exactly the registered SMH trigger series.
// The cut LOW = 0.01739 is the FROZEN registered constant (sits at
// the 27.52nd percentile of the pre-2021 sig_w distribution). The
// registered trigger is the CROSS into the region; this layer shows
// the state and a running weeks-in-region counter, both descriptive
// bookkeeping. DESIGN BOOKKEEPING ONLY: pre-2021 cum12 0.634 [0.487,
// 0.782] vs matched climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471]; the dependence
// audit (block 70) shows the independence band understates the
// rival's uncertainty 3.4x-6.1x, so "disjoint bands" does NOT survive
// dependence-honest construction. Proof object = the forward log
// (EMPTY; kill lines 3/8/19/31). NOT a signal.
float sigW8 = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var)
bool  inCompression = weeklyOK and not na(sigW8) and sigW8 <= lowCutIn
var int weeksInComp = 0
weeksInComp := inCompression ? weeksInComp + 1 : 0
bool compCross = inCompression and not inCompression[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: PRIMED DRAFT STATE (Andrea's spec; UNEVALUATED)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Andrea's chat spec, verbatim semantics, grain-translated to the
// weekly registered universe (his spec used daily units on a weekly
// chart; see the hardening notes for the translation table):
//   rvol_pct   = prior-bar percentile rank of the ATR over 52 weeks
//   comp_weeks = consecutive weeks with rvol_pct <= 33
//   range_pos  = (close - lowest(low, 52w)) / (highest(high,52w) - lowest(low,52w))
//   is_primed  = comp_weeks >= 20 and rvol_pct <= 33 and range_pos <= 0.35
// This is a DRAFT DEFINITION. It is NOT the registered SMH machinery
// (which is sig_w8 vs LOW above), it has never been evaluated, no
// finding exists, and no "significance" is claimed or implied. The
// teal background makes the draft state visible for observation only.
float atrRaw  = ta.atr(atrLen)
float rvolPct = na(atrRaw) ? na : pctRank(atrRaw, rvolWin, rvolWin) * 100.0
var int compWeeks = 0
if not na(rvolPct) and rvolPct <= primedCut
    compWeeks += 1
else
    compWeeks := 0
float loR = ta.lowest(low, rangeWin)
float hiR = ta.highest(high, rangeWin)
float rangePos = (close - loR) / math.max(hiR - loR, syminfo.mintick)
bool isPrimed = weeklyOK and not na(rvolPct) and compWeeks >= compMin and rvolPct <= primedCut and not na(rangePos) and rangePos <= rangeCut
bool primedCross = isPrimed and not isPrimed[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: EXTREMITY EXPORTS (Amendment 11 DRAFT; hard-gated)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// All ranks use the PRIOR-bar pctRank helper (current bar excluded);
// all flags use fixed constants declared above (Amendment 11 draft
// cells; see docs/amendment11_extremity_structure.md). DESCRIPTIVE
// ONLY: no backtest, no forward return, no evaluation is performed or
// licensed by these series. They exist so the eventual cross-check
// paste and the hard-gated Python engine read the SAME cells.
float absSpz   = na(sp_z) ? na : math.abs(sp_z)
float absRz    = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.abs(sp_rz)
float extPctSpz = pctRank(absSpz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctRz  = pctRank(absRz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctMom = pctRank(na(mom12) ? na : math.abs(mom12), normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctVol = pctRank(vol8, normWin, extMinCov)
bool  spzHi    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z >= SPZ_EXT
bool  spzLo    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z <= -SPZ_EXT
bool  spzAbs2  = spzHi or spzLo
bool  rzAbs2   = not na(sp_rz) and math.abs(sp_rz) >= RZ_EXT
bool  rhoBurst = not na(rho) and rho >= RHO_EXT
bool  asymHi   = not na(asym) and asym >= ASYM_HI
bool  asymLo   = not na(asym) and asym <= ASYM_LO
bool  anyExt   = spzAbs2 or rzAbs2 or rhoBurst or asymHi or asymLo

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers (v4: confirmed-bar gated, no intrabar flicker)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)

// PGT markers (confirmed-bar gated)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top",    shape.triangledown, location.top,    color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", shape.triangleup,   location.bottom, color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Top and not g1Top, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation top",    shape.circle, location.top,    color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Bot and not g1Bot, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation bottom", shape.circle, location.bottom, color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)

// Robust z display-clamped (registered value untouched; display copy only)
float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)

// v4 curvature display line (curvature/sd_s, clamped ±3; descriptive)
float curvDisp = na(curvZ) ? na : math.max(-3.0, math.min(3.0, curvZ))
plot(showCurv ? curvDisp : na, "Spread curvature (curv/sd_s, clamped; descriptive)", color=color.new(color.gray, 60), linewidth=1)

// Backgrounds (all labeled, all observation only)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")
bgcolor(showSmhBg and inCompression ? color.new(color.blue, 90) : na, title="SMH compression region (registered; bookkeeping)")
bgcolor(showPrimedBg and isPrimed ? color.new(color.teal, 80) : na, title="Primed (DRAFT, unevaluated)")
plotshape(showRoll and rollWindow, "Roll/expiry window", shape.diamond, location.bottom, color.new(color.gray, 30), size=size.tiny)

// ── Data Window exports (observation only; NOTHING evaluated) ─────
// PASTE CROSS-CHECK (standing item): the titles below use the EXACT
// pandas column names of the registered CSVs, so the check is a
// mechanical name-by-name comparison — export the chart's own weekly
// OHLCV, run the pandas engine on that export, compare at the same
// week_end date. FLAG EXPORTS ARE NA-PRESERVING: na during warmup,
// never 0; a 0 always means "computed and false" (pandas NaN parity).
// PLOT BUDGET: 60 of the 64 plot() limit. NOT exported because each is
// an exact boolean function of exported series: cPos/cNeg (feed
// cusumAlarm), tbFlagged (= any TB flag AND nearBreak), climaxTop /
// climaxBot (= climax AND newHigh / newLow), spzAbs2 (= spzHi OR
// spzLo). Derive them in the cross-check workbook, never re-plot them.

// registered core (avfi_series.csv parity; "state" is a string column)
plot(sigma3, "sigma3", display=display.data_window)
plot(rho,    "rho3",   display=display.data_window)
plot(spread, "spread", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_z,   "sp_z",   display=display.data_window)

// research overlay + the SMH trigger series (registered)
plot(mom12, "mom12",  display=display.data_window)
plot(vol8,  "vol8",   display=display.data_window)
plot(sigW8, "sig_w8", display=display.data_window)

// tops/bottoms layer (avfi_v32_tb_series.csv parity)
plot(med_s,  "med_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q25_s,  "q25_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q75_s,  "q75_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(rsig_s, "rsig_s", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_rz,  "sp_rz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(na(hhN)   ? na : newHigh ? 1 : 0, "newHigh",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(llN)   ? na : newLow  ? 1 : 0, "newLow",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : divTop  ? 1 : 0, "divTop",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : divBot  ? 1 : 0, "divBot",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(rho)   ? na : climax  ? 1 : 0, "climax",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sd_s[1]) ? na : cusumAlarm ? 1 : 0, "cusumAlarm", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sd_s[1]) ? na : nearBreak   ? 1 : 0, "nearBreak",   display=display.data_window)

// v3.4 measurement layers (avfi_v34_series.csv parity)
plot(sigmaGK,  "sigma_gk",  display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3GK, "sigma3_gk", display=display.data_window)
plot(clv,      "clv",       display=display.data_window)
plot(spZClv,   "sp_z_clv",  display=display.data_window)
plot(asym,     "asym",      display=display.data_window)

// PGT plane-geometry layer (v3.5 lineage)
plot(hiPriorSpz, "hiPriorSpz", display=display.data_window)
plot(loPriorSpz, "loPriorSpz", display=display.data_window)
plot(zMax26,     "zMax26",     display=display.data_window)
plot(zMin26,     "zMin26",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)   ? na : g1Top ? 1 : 0, "g1Top", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)   ? na : g1Bot ? 1 : 0, "g1Bot", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(zMax26) ? na : g2Top ? 1 : 0, "g2Top", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(zMin26) ? na : g2Bot ? 1 : 0, "g2Bot", display=display.data_window)

// v4 layers (all descriptive; Amendment 11 draft cells unevaluated)
plot(sigmaYZ,   "sigma_yz",   display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3YZ,  "sigma3_yz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(velocity,  "velocity",   display=display.data_window)
plot(curvature, "curvature", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctSpz, "ext_pct_spz", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctRz,  "ext_pct_rz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctMom, "ext_pct_mom", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctVol, "ext_pct_vol", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)  ? na : spzHi    ? 1 : 0, "ext_spz_hi",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)  ? na : spzLo    ? 1 : 0, "ext_spz_lo",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : rzAbs2   ? 1 : 0, "ext_rz_abs2",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(rho)   ? na : rhoBurst ? 1 : 0, "ext_rho_burst", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(asym)  ? na : asymHi   ? 1 : 0, "ext_asym_hi",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(asym)  ? na : asymLo   ? 1 : 0, "ext_asym_lo",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sigW8)   ? na : inCompression ? 1 : 0, "smh_in_compression", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(rvolPct) ? na : isPrimed       ? 1 : 0, "primed_draft",      display=display.data_window)

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 36, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 35)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header (v4: proxy-volume + live-bar badges)
    string hdr2 = "paper only" + tfNote + (hasRealVol ? "" : " | PROXY VOL") + (barstate.isrealtime ? " | LIVE BAR (forming)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, hdr2, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? cHead : color.new(color.red, 40), text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_tb_layer.py (6704 bytes)
"""AVFI v3.2 tops/bottoms layer, pandas replication (Amendment 3 draft, H8/H9).

Stage 1b deliverable. Replicates strategies/avfi_v32_topbottom.pine exactly:

  - robust IQR spread z (sp_rz): median/quartiles by Pine's
    ta.percentile_nearest_rank over the PRIOR bar's normWin window
    (spread[1] rolling), so the current bar is excluded from its own
    reference distribution. rsig_s = (q75 - q25)/1.349 + EPS.
  - fresh pbN-week closing extremes (newHigh / newLow).
  - H8 draft flags: divTop / divBot (fresh extreme + flow fade).
  - H9 draft flags: climaxTop / climaxBot (rho >= climRho and |z3| <=
    deadZone at a fresh extreme). Uses z_of(sigma3) = z3 and rho3 from
    compute_avfi.
  - CUSUM regime-break guard: one-sided CUSUM on spread around the
    PRIOR-bar rolling mean mu_s[1] with sd_s[1] scale, drift allowance
    cusumK, alarm at cusumH, reset after alarm, breakHold-week flag
    window (nearBreak / tbFlagged).

Registered defaults (Amendment 3 draft, DO NOT TUNE):
  pbN 20, fadeZ 0.0, climRho 1.5, cusumK 0.5, cusumH 4.0, breakHold 13,
  normWin 500. deadZone 0.2 (core v3 constant, reused by the climax test).

compute_tb_layer(base, closes, **overrides) -> DataFrame aligned to base,
where `base` is the compute_avfi() output (needs spread, z3, rho3) and
`closes` the matching close series (base already carries close, so a
convenience wrapper tb_from_avfi(df) handles the full path).

Discipline: observation-only flags. No forward returns, no performance
statistic, no event evaluation lives in this module. The sealed 2021+
holdout is never evaluated here; the H8/H9 event studies run only on
unsealed data (1999-2020) and only after Andrea signs off Amendment 3.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from avfi_replication import pine_sma, pine_stdev, EPS

TB_DEFAULT_PARAMS = {
    "pbN": 20, "fadeZ": 0.0, "climRho": 1.5,
    "cusumK": 0.5, "cusumH": 4.0, "breakHold": 13,
    "normWin": 500, "deadZone": 0.2,
}


def percentile_nearest_rank(x: pd.Series, n: int, pct: float) -> pd.Series:
    """Pine ta.percentile_nearest_rank(x, n, pct).

    Nearest-rank method: rank = ceil(pct/100 * n) (clamped to [1, n]),
    value = ascending-sorted window[rank-1]. A window containing any na
    yields na (Pine rolling semantics: na inputs poison the window).
    """
    def _pnr(win):
        if np.isnan(win).any():
            return np.nan
        s = np.sort(win)
        rank = int(np.ceil(pct / 100.0 * len(s)))
        rank = min(max(rank, 1), len(s))
        return s[rank - 1]
    return x.rolling(n, min_periods=n).apply(_pnr, raw=True)


def robust_z(spread: pd.Series, norm_win: int):
    """Prior-bar-window robust (IQR) z of the spread.

    med/q75/q25 over spread.shift(1) rolling norm_win: at bar t the
    window is spread[t-norm_win] .. spread[t-1]; the current bar never
    enters its own reference distribution (no self-contamination).
    """
    prior = spread.shift(1)
    med = percentile_nearest_rank(prior, norm_win, 50)
    q75 = percentile_nearest_rank(prior, norm_win, 75)
    q25 = percentile_nearest_rank(prior, norm_win, 25)
    rsig = (q75 - q25) / 1.349 + EPS
    sp_rz = (spread - med) / rsig
    return med, q75, q25, rsig, sp_rz


def cusum_guard(spread: pd.Series, mu_s: pd.Series, sd_s: pd.Series,
                cusum_k: float, cusum_h: float, break_hold: int):
    """Pine-ordered one-sided CUSUM on the spread.

    Per bar, exactly like the Pine:
      if spread or mu_s[1] or sd_s[1] is na: cPos = cNeg = 0
      else: accumulate cPos/cNeg with drift allowance k, scale sd_s[1]
      alarm = sd_s[1] not na and max(cPos, cNeg) > h * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
      if alarm: reset both to 0 and stamp last_break_index
    near_break: index distance to the last alarm <= break_hold.
    """
    sp = spread.to_numpy(float)
    mu1 = mu_s.shift(1).to_numpy(float)
    sd1 = sd_s.shift(1).to_numpy(float)
    m = len(sp)
    cpos = np.zeros(m)
    cneg = np.zeros(m)
    alarm = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
    last_break = np.full(m, -1, dtype=int)
    cp = cn = 0.0
    last = -1
    for i in range(m):
        if np.isnan(sp[i]) or np.isnan(mu1[i]) or np.isnan(sd1[i]):
            cp = cn = 0.0
        else:
            cp = max(0.0, cp + (sp[i] - mu1[i]) - cusum_k * sd1[i])
            cn = max(0.0, cn + (mu1[i] - sp[i]) - cusum_k * sd1[i])
        a = (not np.isnan(sd1[i])) and max(cp, cn) > cusum_h * (sd1[i] + EPS)
        if a:
            cp = cn = 0.0
            last = i
        cpos[i] = cp
        cneg[i] = cn
        alarm[i] = a
        last_break[i] = last
    idx = np.arange(m)
    near_break = (last_break >= 0) & (idx - last_break <= break_hold)
    return (pd.Series(cpos, index=spread.index),
            pd.Series(cneg, index=spread.index),
            pd.Series(alarm, index=spread.index),
            pd.Series(near_break, index=spread.index))


def compute_tb_layer(base: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Full v3.2 tops/bottoms layer from a compute_avfi() output frame."""
    p = dict(TB_DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    p.update(overrides)
    spread = base["spread"]
    close = base["close"]

    med, q75, q25, rsig, sp_rz = robust_z(spread, p["normWin"])

    hh = close.rolling(p["pbN"], min_periods=p["pbN"]).max()
    ll = close.rolling(p["pbN"], min_periods=p["pbN"]).min()
    new_high = hh.notna() & (close >= hh)
    new_low = ll.notna() & (close <= ll)

    div_top = new_high & sp_rz.notna() & (sp_rz <= p["fadeZ"])
    div_bot = new_low & sp_rz.notna() & (sp_rz >= -p["fadeZ"])

    climax = base["rho3"].notna() & (base["rho3"] >= p["climRho"]) & \
        (base["z3"].abs() <= p["deadZone"])
    climax_top = climax & new_high
    climax_bot = climax & new_low

    mu_s = pine_sma(spread, p["normWin"])
    sd_s = pine_stdev(spread, p["normWin"])
    cpos, cneg, cusum_alarm, near_break = cusum_guard(
        spread, mu_s, sd_s, p["cusumK"], p["cusumH"], p["breakHold"])

    tb_flagged = (div_top | div_bot | climax_top | climax_bot) & near_break

    return pd.DataFrame({
        "date": base["date"], "close": close,
        "spread": spread, "med_s": med, "q25_s": q25, "q75_s": q75,
        "rsig_s": rsig, "sp_rz": sp_rz,
        "newHigh": new_high, "newLow": new_low,
        "divTop": div_top, "divBot": div_bot,
        "climax": climax, "climaxTop": climax_top, "climaxBot": climax_bot,
        "cPos": cpos, "cNeg": cneg, "cusumAlarm": cusum_alarm,
        "nearBreak": near_break, "tbFlagged": tb_flagged,
    })


def tb_from_avfi(df: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Convenience: canonical bars -> compute_avfi -> tops/bottoms layer."""
    from avfi_replication import compute_avfi
    return compute_tb_layer(compute_avfi(df), **overrides)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_tb_layer.py

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (new in v3.1). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 12, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 11)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper only" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, thStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(u3Size, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "U3 paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=u3Size > 0 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // cadence + provenance
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, "12w / 8w / 20% / 0.5", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, "sealed 2021+", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v31_research.pine

// AVFI v3.2 TOPS/BOTTOMS RESEARCH EDITION (Nov 2026)
//
// Adds the tops/bottoms layer the programme never had, on top of v3.1:
//   * robust IQR-based spread z (median/quartiles, PRIOR-bar window so
//     the current bar cannot shrink its own extreme; fat-tail robust)
//   * wave-free divergence flags (Amendment 3 draft, H8): fresh 20-week
//     price extreme with flow fade against it — rule set B's core
//     without the wave-count dependency
//   * effort-vs-result climax flags (Amendment 3 draft, H9, Williams/
//     VSA): volume surprise rho >= 1.5 with |z| <= dead zone at fresh
//     price extremes
//   * CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8's standing guard,
//     now implemented): alarms on spread drift, flags divergence events
//     within 13 weeks of a detected break
//   * top/bottom markers, robust-z line, panel rows, alerts
//
// v3.1 additions retained: 4-state regime readout, rule set T signal,
// U3 paper size, weekly decision panel. Original "AVFI v3 vs v2 [spread
// axis]" core VERBATIM below (pandas replication parity, 7 unit tests;
// changing it would break replication). Reference copies
// avfi_v3_reference.pine and avfi_v31_research.pine stay untouched.
//
// Discipline: INDICATOR, not strategy. No equity curve, no win rate, no
// backtest stats. The sealed 2021+ holdout is spent only by the
// registered one-shot battery on Andrea's explicit go, never by a
// TradingView strategy tester. H8/H9 are DRAFT hypotheses: their
// defaults are registered BEFORE any evaluation; re-tuning after seeing
// results requires a new dated amendment plus multiplicity accounting.
// Paper only, nothing here is live advice.
//
// Designed for the weekly NQ (NASDAQ-100 E-mini) chart, W-FRI bars.
//
// ── v3.2.1 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ──────────────────────────────────
// Observability and display only. Every computed series, flag, and
// registered constant is identical to v3.2 (stage 1b tested, T8-T15):

//     window - the event class the H8/H9 studies will key on)
//   * new alert: momentum_down regime entry (complements the

//   * research panel: fresh-20w-extreme row, TB-flags-since-break
//     counter row, climax direction on the effort>result row

// New display-only state: tbSinceBreak counter. No hypothesis uses it.
// v3.2 (avfi_v32_topbottom.pine) stays untouched as the registered

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────

thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3 draft, do not tune) ─
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (draft H8/H9)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)
rzClamp   = input.float(6.0, "Robust z display clamp (visual only)",   minval=1.0, step=1.0, group=grpTB)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (new in v3.2). Weekly-chart logic, observation
// only. Registered in Amendment 3 draft BEFORE any evaluation.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution: a mean/stdev z is
// inflated by the very extreme it tries to measure, and weekly returns
// are fat-tailed, so quartiles beat moments here).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft: wave-free flow fade at price extremes (rule set B core,
// without the wave-count dependency). Top = new high with flow
// innovation below its median; bottom = mirror.
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft: effort-vs-result climax (Williams/VSA). Big volume effort
// (rho vs geometric-mean baseline) with a dead price result (|z| inside
// the dead zone) at a fresh extreme = absorption.
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8 standing guard).
// One-sided CUSUM on the spread around the prior-bar rolling mean,
// drift allowance k, alarm h in sigma units; resets after an alarm.
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
// Display-only counter (v3.2.1): TB flags landed since the last CUSUM
// break. Observability; no series or hypothesis reads it.
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers + robust z ────────────────────────────────
// Markers on weekly charts only (v3.2.1): pbN is registered in weeks.
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divTop, "Top fade (H8 draft)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divBot, "Bottom fade (H8 draft)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (H9 draft)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (H9 draft)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)
// Robust z display-clamped (v3.2.1): the value is untouched; only the
// plotted copy is clamped so a 200-sigma spike cannot squash the pane.
float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 20, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 19)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper only" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, thStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(u3Size, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "U3 paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=u3Size > 0 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // tops/bottoms layer (v3.2)
    string rzStr = na(sp_rz) ? "n/a" : (sp_rz > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_rz, "0.##") + " σ (IQR)"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Robust spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, rzStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Top fade (20w high)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, divTop ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divTop ? cFlat : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Bottom fade (20w low)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, divBot ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divBot ? cLong : na, text_size=size.small)
    string clStr = climax ? "ρ=" + str.tostring(rho, "0.0") + (climaxTop and climaxBot ? " @both" : climaxTop ? " @high" : climaxBot ? " @low" : "") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 12, "Effort > result", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 12, clStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=climax ? color.new(color.orange, 40) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string bkStr = nearBreak ? "within " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastBreakBar) + "w" : "quiet"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 13, "CUSUM break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 13, bkStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=nearBreak ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string fxStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : newHigh and newLow ? "high & low" : newHigh ? "20w high" : newLow ? "20w low" : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 14, "Fresh 20w extreme", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 14, fxStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string tbStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : tbSinceBreak == 0 ? "none" : str.tostring(tbSinceBreak) + (tbFlagged ? " (now)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 15, "TB flags since break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 15, tbStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tbFlagged ? color.new(color.orange, 55) : na, text_size=size.small)

    // cadence + provenance
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 16, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 16, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 17, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 17, "12w / 8w / 20w / 0.5", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 18, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 18, "sealed 2021+", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 19, "H8/H9 layer", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 19, "draft, untested", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Momentum down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Momentum down"), "Momentum-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered momentum_down (deep-bear regime, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (H8 draft)", "AVFI v3.2.1: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (draft H8, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (H8 draft)", "AVFI v3.2.1: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (draft H8, paper)")
alertcondition(climaxTop or climaxBot, "Climax effort>result (H9 draft)", "AVFI v3.2.1: volume surprise with dead price result at a fresh extreme (draft H9, paper)")
alertcondition(cusumAlarm, "CUSUM regime break", "AVFI v3.2.1: spread CUSUM regime-break alarm (z-score window suspect)")
alertcondition(tbFlagged, "TB flag in break window", "AVFI v3.2.1: tops/bottoms flag inside CUSUM break window (draft H8/H9 composite, paper)")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v321_tb_research.pine

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v32_topbottom.pine (22429 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v3.2 [spread axis + tops/bottoms layer]", shorttitle="AVFI v3.2", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=1000)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AVFI v3.2 TOPS/BOTTOMS RESEARCH EDITION (Nov 2026)
//
// Adds the tops/bottoms layer the programme never had, on top of v3.1:
//   * robust IQR-based spread z (median/quartiles, PRIOR-bar window so
//     the current bar cannot shrink its own extreme; fat-tail robust)
//   * wave-free divergence flags (Amendment 3 draft, H8): fresh 20-week
//     price extreme with flow fade against it — rule set B's core
//     without the wave-count dependency
//   * effort-vs-result climax flags (Amendment 3 draft, H9, Williams/
//     VSA): volume surprise rho >= 1.5 with |z| <= dead zone at fresh
//     price extremes
//   * CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8's standing guard,
//     now implemented): alarms on spread drift, flags divergence events
//     within 13 weeks of a detected break
//   * top/bottom markers, robust-z line, panel rows, alerts
//
// v3.1 additions retained: 4-state regime readout, rule set T signal,
// U3 paper size, weekly decision panel. Original "AVFI v3 vs v2 [spread
// axis]" core VERBATIM below (pandas replication parity, 7 unit tests;
// changing it would break replication). Reference copies
// avfi_v3_reference.pine and avfi_v31_research.pine stay untouched.
//
// Discipline: INDICATOR, not strategy. No equity curve, no win rate, no
// backtest stats. The sealed 2021+ holdout is spent only by the
// registered one-shot battery on Andrea's explicit go, never by a
// TradingView strategy tester. H8/H9 are DRAFT hypotheses: their
// defaults are registered BEFORE any evaluation; re-tuning after seeing
// results requires a new dated amendment plus multiplicity accounting.
// Paper only, nothing here is live advice.
//
// Designed for the weekly NQ (NASDAQ-100 E-mini) chart, W-FRI bars.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3 draft, do not tune) ─
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (draft H8/H9)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (new in v3.2). Weekly-chart logic, observation
// only. Registered in Amendment 3 draft BEFORE any evaluation.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution: a mean/stdev z is
// inflated by the very extreme it tries to measure, and weekly returns
// are fat-tailed, so quartiles beat moments here).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft: wave-free flow fade at price extremes (rule set B core,
// without the wave-count dependency). Top = new high with flow
// innovation below its median; bottom = mirror.
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft: effort-vs-result climax (Williams/VSA). Big volume effort
// (rho vs geometric-mean baseline) with a dead price result (|z| inside
// the dead zone) at a fresh extreme = absorption.
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8 standing guard).
// One-sided CUSUM on the spread around the prior-bar rolling mean,
// drift allowance k, alarm h in sigma units; resets after an alarm.
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
