plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers + robust z ────────────────────────────────
plotshape(showTB and divTop, "Top fade (H8 draft)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and divBot, "Bottom fade (H8 draft)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (H9 draft)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (H9 draft)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)
plot(showRZ ? sp_rz : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 18, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 17)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper only" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, thStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(u3Size, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "U3 paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=u3Size > 0 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // tops/bottoms layer (v3.2)
    string rzStr = na(sp_rz) ? "n/a" : (sp_rz > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_rz, "0.##") + " σ (IQR)"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Robust spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, rzStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Top fade (20w high)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, divTop ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divTop ? cFlat : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Bottom fade (20w low)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, divBot ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divBot ? cLong : na, text_size=size.small)
    string clStr = climax ? "ρ=" + str.tostring(rho, "0.0") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 12, "Effort > result", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 12, clStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=climax ? color.new(color.orange, 40) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string bkStr = nearBreak ? "within " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastBreakBar) + "w" : "quiet"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 13, "CUSUM break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 13, bkStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=nearBreak ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : na, text_size=size.small)

    // cadence + provenance
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 14, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 14, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 15, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 15, "12w / 8w / 20w / 0.5", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 16, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 16, "sealed 2021+", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 17, "H8/H9 layer", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 17, "draft, untested", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (H8 draft)", "AVFI v3.2: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (draft H8, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (H8 draft)", "AVFI v3.2: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (draft H8, paper)")
alertcondition(climaxTop or climaxBot, "Climax effort>result (H9 draft)", "AVFI v3.2: volume surprise with dead price result at a fresh extreme (draft H9, paper)")
alertcondition(cusumAlarm, "CUSUM regime break", "AVFI v3.2: spread CUSUM regime-break alarm (z-score window suspect)")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v32_topbottom.pine

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v331_research.pine (30615 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v3.3.1 [spread axis + tops/bottoms layer]", shorttitle="AVFI v3.3.1", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=1000)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AVFI v3.3 RESEARCH EDITION (Nov 2026)
//
// Adds the tops/bottoms layer the programme never had, on top of v3.1:
//   * robust IQR-based spread z (median/quartiles, PRIOR-bar window so
//     the current bar cannot shrink its own extreme; fat-tail robust)
//   * wave-free divergence flags (Amendment 3 draft, H8): fresh 20-week
//     price extreme with flow fade against it — rule set B's core
//     without the wave-count dependency
//   * effort-vs-result climax flags (Amendment 3 draft, H9, Williams/
//     VSA): volume surprise rho >= 1.5 with |z| <= dead zone at fresh
//     price extremes
//   * CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8's standing guard,
//     now implemented): alarms on spread drift, flags divergence events
//     within 13 weeks of a detected break
//   * top/bottom markers, robust-z line, panel rows, alerts
//
// v3.1 additions retained: 4-state regime readout, rule set T signal,
// U3 paper size, weekly decision panel. Original "AVFI v3 vs v2 [spread
// axis]" core VERBATIM below (pandas replication parity, 7 unit tests;
// changing it would break replication). Reference copies
// avfi_v3_reference.pine and avfi_v31_research.pine stay untouched.
//
// Discipline: INDICATOR, not strategy. No equity curve, no win rate, no
// backtest stats. STATUS (11 Nov 2026): the sealed 2021+ holdout has
// been SPENT by the registered single-open battery (Andrea's explicit
// go; verdict: nothing passes) and is never evaluated again. The H8/H9
// tops/bottoms event studies were EXECUTED after Andrea signed
// Amendment 3 and returned NULLS: the fade/climax flags below are
// descriptive observability only, never signals, and their frozen
// defaults are never re-tuned. Any new hypothesis needs a new dated,
// signed amendment before evaluation. Paper only, nothing here is live
// advice.
//
// Designed for the weekly NQ (NASDAQ-100 E-mini) chart, W-FRI bars.
//
// ── v3.2.1 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ──────────────────────────────────
// Observability and display only. Every computed series, flag, and
// registered constant is identical to v3.2 (stage 1b tested, T8-T15):
//   * new alert: tbFlagged composite (TB flag inside a CUSUM break
//     window; the H8/H9 studies keyed on this class and returned nulls)
//   * new alert: momentum_down regime entry (complements the
//     reversal-down alert; the H6 deep-bear regime)
//   * research panel: fresh-20w-extreme row, TB-flags-since-break
//     counter row, climax direction on the effort>result row
//   * robust z line gets a visual clamp input (sp_rz itself untouched;
//     spikes to 200+ sigma otherwise squash the pane scale)
//   * top/bottom markers gated to weekly charts (pbN is in weeks; on a
//     daily chart 20 bars are 20 days and the markers would mislead)
// New display-only state: tbSinceBreak counter. No hypothesis uses it.
// v3.2 (avfi_v32_topbottom.pine) stays untouched as the registered
// edition that stage 1b validated.
//
// ── v3.3 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ────────────────────────────────────
// Truth-in-display + one new observation layer; every computed series,
// flag, and registered constant is identical to v3.2 (stage 1b T8-T15):
//   * panel and alerts corrected to the post-battery, post-H8/H9 state
//     (holdout SPENT; tops/bottoms flags descriptive, tested null)
//   * new: quarterly roll/expiry artifact-window marker (stage 0d
//     evidence: 7 of 11 canonical-vs-index divergence weeks sit in
//     expiry/roll-adjacent windows; continuous-contract splices can
//     distort weekly bars and the indicator's prints there). Marks the
//     week of the third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec plus the following
//     (roll) week. Observation only, never a filter.
//   * new: volume-source panel row (real volume vs proxy fallback; a
//     no-volume symbol silently changes the indicator's semantics)
//
// ── v3.3.1 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ──────────────────────────────────
// ONE parity fix in the sd_s computation; no flag, marker, alert,
// constant, or default changes anywhere else.
//   * sd_s was ta.stdev(spread, normWin). Pine's ta.stdev is BIASED /
//     population (ddof=0) by default, while the validated pandas
//     reference (avfi_replication.pine_stdev) uses SAMPLE stdev
//     (ddof=1) - the convention every registered study ran on (stage 3
//     backtest, battery, H8/H9). The v3.3 chart was therefore running
//     ~0.1% off the validated series (sqrt(500/499) scale on sd_s).
//     Fixed with the native biased=false argument. A manual
//     sum((x-mu_s)^2)/(n-1) was drafted first and REJECTED in
//     validation: its na-poisoning delays sd_s validity by normWin
//     bars (bar ~1258 vs the reference's 759), which would have
//     silently shifted CUSUM resets across the early window.
//   * Validated BEFORE shipping (canonical 1365 bars, both
//     conventions computed in pandas): ZERO flag differences - CUSUM
//     alarms 62/62, nearBreak weeks 329/329, tbFlagged 27/27
//     (unsealed), divTop/climax populations unchanged; only displayed
//     sp_z/band values shift, by at most 0.0042. This makes the
//     standing TradingView paste cross-check exact (the panel sp_z
//     must equal the stage 7 snapshot card, which is computed in the
//     pandas convention).
//   * avfi_v33_research.pine stays untouched as shipped; the pandas
//     reference and all registered editions (v3, v3.1, v3.2, v3.2.1)
//     are untouched. The verbatim rule protects the PANDAS
//     replication - this fix moves the PINE toward it, not away.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3 draft, do not tune) ─
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (descriptive; H8/H9 tested null)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)
rzClamp   = input.float(6.0, "Robust z display clamp (visual only)",   minval=1.0, step=1.0, group=grpTB)

// ── Artifact window parameters (v3.3, observation only) ────────────
grpRoll   = "Artifact windows (observation only)"
showRoll  = input.bool(true, "Mark quarterly roll/expiry weeks",        group=grpRoll)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
// v3.3.1 parity fix: SAMPLE stdev (ddof=1) via the biased=false
// argument, matching the validated pandas reference (pine_stdev).
// Pine's default ta.stdev is biased/population (ddof=0), which put the
// chart's sd_s/sp_z/CUSUM scale ~0.1% off the registered series. A
// manual sum((x-mu)^2)/(n-1) was drafted first and REJECTED: it is na
// until spread-mu_s has normWin consecutive valid bars (bar ~1258),
// silently delaying sp_z/CUSUM by 499 weeks vs the reference (valid
// from bar 759). The native biased=false call keeps the exact na
// semantics of the original line.
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (new in v3.2). Weekly-chart logic, observation
// only. Registered in Amendment 3 draft BEFORE any evaluation.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution: a mean/stdev z is
// inflated by the very extreme it tries to measure, and weekly returns
// are fat-tailed, so quartiles beat moments here).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft: wave-free flow fade at price extremes (rule set B core,
// without the wave-count dependency). Top = new high with flow
// innovation below its median; bottom = mirror.
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft: effort-vs-result climax (Williams/VSA). Big volume effort
// (rho vs geometric-mean baseline) with a dead price result (|z| inside
// the dead zone) at a fresh extreme = absorption.
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8 standing guard).
// One-sided CUSUM on the spread around the prior-bar rolling mean,
// drift allowance k, alarm h in sigma units; resets after an alarm.
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
// Display-only counter (v3.2.1): TB flags landed since the last CUSUM
// break. Observability; no series or hypothesis reads it.
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
// Stage 0d evidence: 7 of 11 canonical-vs-index divergence weeks sit in
// expiry/roll-adjacent windows. The third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec is
// quarterly expiry; the following week is the roll week. W-FRI weekly
// bars open ON the Friday, so the expiry bar's time IS the third
// Friday. On other weekly conventions the marker silently stays off
// (check the panel row).
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers + robust z ────────────────────────────────
// Markers on weekly charts only (v3.2.1): pbN is registered in weeks.
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)
// Robust z display-clamped (v3.2.1): the value is untouched; only the
// plotted copy is clamped so a 200-sigma spike cannot squash the pane.
float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")
plotshape(showRoll and rollWindow, "Roll/expiry window", shape.diamond, location.bottom, color.new(color.gray, 30), size=size.tiny)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 22, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 21)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper only" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, thStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(u3Size, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "U3 paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=u3Size > 0 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // tops/bottoms layer (v3.2)
    string rzStr = na(sp_rz) ? "n/a" : (sp_rz > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_rz, "0.##") + " σ (IQR)"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Robust spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, rzStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Top fade (20w high)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, divTop ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divTop ? cFlat : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Bottom fade (20w low)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, divBot ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divBot ? cLong : na, text_size=size.small)
    string clStr = climax ? "ρ=" + str.tostring(rho, "0.0") + (climaxTop and climaxBot ? " @both" : climaxTop ? " @high" : climaxBot ? " @low" : "") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 12, "Effort > result", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 12, clStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=climax ? color.new(color.orange, 40) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string bkStr = nearBreak ? "within " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastBreakBar) + "w" : "quiet"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 13, "CUSUM break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 13, bkStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=nearBreak ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string fxStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : newHigh and newLow ? "high & low" : newHigh ? "20w high" : newLow ? "20w low" : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 14, "Fresh 20w extreme", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 14, fxStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string tbStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : tbSinceBreak == 0 ? "none" : str.tostring(tbSinceBreak) + (tbFlagged ? " (now)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 15, "TB flags since break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 15, tbStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tbFlagged ? color.new(color.orange, 55) : na, text_size=size.small)

    // artifact + provenance (v3.3)
    string rwStr = not timeframe.isweekly ? "n/a (weekly)" : rollWindow ? (isExpiryWk ? "expiry wk" : "roll wk") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 16, "Roll/expiry window", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 16, rwStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=rollWindow ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 17, "Volume source", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 17, hasRealVol ? "real" : "PROXY (no volume)", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? na : color.new(color.red, 55), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 18, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 18, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 19, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 19, "12w / 8w / 20w / 0.5", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 20, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 20, "spent 2021+ (battery)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 21, "Tops/bottoms flags", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 21, "descriptive (tested null)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Momentum down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Momentum down"), "Momentum-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered momentum_down (deep-bear regime, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(climaxTop or climaxBot, "Climax effort>result (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: volume surprise with dead price result at a fresh extreme (descriptive; H9 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(cusumAlarm, "CUSUM regime break", "AVFI v3.3: spread CUSUM regime-break alarm (z-score window suspect)")
alertcondition(tbFlagged, "TB flag in break window", "AVFI v3.3: tops/bottoms flag inside CUSUM break window (descriptive; tested null, paper)")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v331_research.pine

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v33_research.pine (28270 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v3.3 [spread axis + tops/bottoms layer]", shorttitle="AVFI v3.3", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=1000)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AVFI v3.3 RESEARCH EDITION (Nov 2026)
//
// Adds the tops/bottoms layer the programme never had, on top of v3.1:
//   * robust IQR-based spread z (median/quartiles, PRIOR-bar window so
//     the current bar cannot shrink its own extreme; fat-tail robust)
//   * wave-free divergence flags (Amendment 3 draft, H8): fresh 20-week
//     price extreme with flow fade against it — rule set B's core
//     without the wave-count dependency
//   * effort-vs-result climax flags (Amendment 3 draft, H9, Williams/
//     VSA): volume surprise rho >= 1.5 with |z| <= dead zone at fresh
//     price extremes
//   * CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8's standing guard,
//     now implemented): alarms on spread drift, flags divergence events
//     within 13 weeks of a detected break
//   * top/bottom markers, robust-z line, panel rows, alerts
//
// v3.1 additions retained: 4-state regime readout, rule set T signal,
// U3 paper size, weekly decision panel. Original "AVFI v3 vs v2 [spread
// axis]" core VERBATIM below (pandas replication parity, 7 unit tests;
// changing it would break replication). Reference copies
// avfi_v3_reference.pine and avfi_v31_research.pine stay untouched.
//
// Discipline: INDICATOR, not strategy. No equity curve, no win rate, no
// backtest stats. STATUS (11 Nov 2026): the sealed 2021+ holdout has
// been SPENT by the registered single-open battery (Andrea's explicit
// go; verdict: nothing passes) and is never evaluated again. The H8/H9
// tops/bottoms event studies were EXECUTED after Andrea signed
// Amendment 3 and returned NULLS: the fade/climax flags below are
// descriptive observability only, never signals, and their frozen
// defaults are never re-tuned. Any new hypothesis needs a new dated,
// signed amendment before evaluation. Paper only, nothing here is live
// advice.
//
// Designed for the weekly NQ (NASDAQ-100 E-mini) chart, W-FRI bars.
//
// ── v3.2.1 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ──────────────────────────────────
// Observability and display only. Every computed series, flag, and
// registered constant is identical to v3.2 (stage 1b tested, T8-T15):
//   * new alert: tbFlagged composite (TB flag inside a CUSUM break
//     window; the H8/H9 studies keyed on this class and returned nulls)
//   * new alert: momentum_down regime entry (complements the
//     reversal-down alert; the H6 deep-bear regime)
//   * research panel: fresh-20w-extreme row, TB-flags-since-break
//     counter row, climax direction on the effort>result row
//   * robust z line gets a visual clamp input (sp_rz itself untouched;
//     spikes to 200+ sigma otherwise squash the pane scale)
//   * top/bottom markers gated to weekly charts (pbN is in weeks; on a
//     daily chart 20 bars are 20 days and the markers would mislead)
// New display-only state: tbSinceBreak counter. No hypothesis uses it.
// v3.2 (avfi_v32_topbottom.pine) stays untouched as the registered
// edition that stage 1b validated.
//
// ── v3.3 changelog (11 Nov 2026) ────────────────────────────────────
// Truth-in-display + one new observation layer; every computed series,
// flag, and registered constant is identical to v3.2 (stage 1b T8-T15):
//   * panel and alerts corrected to the post-battery, post-H8/H9 state
//     (holdout SPENT; tops/bottoms flags descriptive, tested null)
//   * new: quarterly roll/expiry artifact-window marker (stage 0d
//     evidence: 7 of 11 canonical-vs-index divergence weeks sit in
//     expiry/roll-adjacent windows; continuous-contract splices can
//     distort weekly bars and the indicator's prints there). Marks the
//     week of the third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec plus the following
//     (roll) week. Observation only, never a filter.
//   * new: volume-source panel row (real volume vs proxy fallback; a
//     no-volume symbol silently changes the indicator's semantics)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3 draft, do not tune) ─
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (descriptive; H8/H9 tested null)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)
rzClamp   = input.float(6.0, "Robust z display clamp (visual only)",   minval=1.0, step=1.0, group=grpTB)

// ── Artifact window parameters (v3.3, observation only) ────────────
grpRoll   = "Artifact windows (observation only)"
showRoll  = input.bool(true, "Mark quarterly roll/expiry weeks",        group=grpRoll)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (new in v3.2). Weekly-chart logic, observation
// only. Registered in Amendment 3 draft BEFORE any evaluation.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution: a mean/stdev z is
// inflated by the very extreme it tries to measure, and weekly returns
// are fat-tailed, so quartiles beat moments here).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft: wave-free flow fade at price extremes (rule set B core,
// without the wave-count dependency). Top = new high with flow
// innovation below its median; bottom = mirror.
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft: effort-vs-result climax (Williams/VSA). Big volume effort
// (rho vs geometric-mean baseline) with a dead price result (|z| inside
// the dead zone) at a fresh extreme = absorption.
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8 standing guard).
// One-sided CUSUM on the spread around the prior-bar rolling mean,
// drift allowance k, alarm h in sigma units; resets after an alarm.
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
// Display-only counter (v3.2.1): TB flags landed since the last CUSUM
// break. Observability; no series or hypothesis reads it.
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
// Stage 0d evidence: 7 of 11 canonical-vs-index divergence weeks sit in
// expiry/roll-adjacent windows. The third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec is
// quarterly expiry; the following week is the roll week. W-FRI weekly
// bars open ON the Friday, so the expiry bar's time IS the third
// Friday. On other weekly conventions the marker silently stays off
// (check the panel row).
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers + robust z ────────────────────────────────
// Markers on weekly charts only (v3.2.1): pbN is registered in weeks.
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)
// Robust z display-clamped (v3.2.1): the value is untouched; only the
// plotted copy is clamped so a 200-sigma spike cannot squash the pane.
float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")
plotshape(showRoll and rollWindow, "Roll/expiry window", shape.diamond, location.bottom, color.new(color.gray, 30), size=size.tiny)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 22, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 21)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper only" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, tLongLast ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLongLast ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12Last) ? "n/a" : (mom12Last > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12Last * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen) + "w"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string thStr = "x" + str.tostring(thrAmt, "0.0") + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (active)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "throttle", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, thStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = timeframe.isweekly ? str.tostring(u3Size, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "U3 paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=u3Size > 0 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // tops/bottoms layer (v3.2)
    string rzStr = na(sp_rz) ? "n/a" : (sp_rz > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_rz, "0.##") + " σ (IQR)"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Robust spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, rzStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Top fade (20w high)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, divTop ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divTop ? cFlat : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Bottom fade (20w low)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, divBot ? "FADE" : "—", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=divBot ? cLong : na, text_size=size.small)
    string clStr = climax ? "ρ=" + str.tostring(rho, "0.0") + (climaxTop and climaxBot ? " @both" : climaxTop ? " @high" : climaxBot ? " @low" : "") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 12, "Effort > result", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 12, clStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=climax ? color.new(color.orange, 40) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string bkStr = nearBreak ? "within " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastBreakBar) + "w" : "quiet"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 13, "CUSUM break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 13, bkStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=nearBreak ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string fxStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : newHigh and newLow ? "high & low" : newHigh ? "20w high" : newLow ? "20w low" : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 14, "Fresh 20w extreme", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 14, fxStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string tbStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : tbSinceBreak == 0 ? "none" : str.tostring(tbSinceBreak) + (tbFlagged ? " (now)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 15, "TB flags since break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 15, tbStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tbFlagged ? color.new(color.orange, 55) : na, text_size=size.small)

    // artifact + provenance (v3.3)
    string rwStr = not timeframe.isweekly ? "n/a (weekly)" : rollWindow ? (isExpiryWk ? "expiry wk" : "roll wk") : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 16, "Roll/expiry window", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 16, rwStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=rollWindow ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 17, "Volume source", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 17, hasRealVol ? "real" : "PROXY (no volume)", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? na : color.new(color.red, 55), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 18, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 18, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 19, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 19, "12w / 8w / 20w / 0.5", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 20, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 20, "spent 2021+ (battery)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 21, "Tops/bottoms flags", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 21, "descriptive (tested null)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Momentum down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Momentum down"), "Momentum-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered momentum_down (deep-bear regime, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v34_layer.py (6756 bytes)
"""AVFI v3.4 measurement layer (pandas), matching the Pine v3.4 edition 1:1.

Book-grounded improvements to the AVFI's core measurements. The v3.1 core
(sigma floor, gate, flow, spread, states) is preserved VERBATIM via
avfi_replication.compute_avfi; nothing in this file feeds back into the
core series, and the frozen research constants are inherited unchanged.

The three layers, each tied to the programme's book corpus:

1. Garman-Klass OHLC volatility (Tsay, Analysis of Financial Time Series,
   ch. 3 range vol: Yang-Zhang/Parkinson/Garman-Klass; Levy, Computational
   Finance Part III). The core floors vol with a Parkinson term built from
   log(H/L) only. The GK estimator also uses the open:
       var_gk_w = 0.5*u^2 - (2*ln2 - 1)*c^2,   u = ln(H/L), c = ln(C/O)
   Since O and C lie inside [L, H], |c| <= u, so var_gk_w >= 0.1137*u^2 >= 0:
   non-negative by construction (an EPS guard is kept anyway). GK is a more
   efficient range estimator than Parkinson; the layer asks whether the
   floor's extra efficiency changes the flow at all.
       sigma_gk   = sqrt(EWM(var_gk_w, hlVol))          (na-tolerant EWM)
       sigma3_gk  = sqrt(max(sigma_r^2, sigma_gk^2))    (same floor shape)

2. Close-location value as the VSA "result" (Williams, Master the Markets:
   the close's position in the bar's range IS the result of the effort; an
   up bar closing at its low is supply, closing at its high is demand).
       clv = (2*C - H - L) / max(H - L, EPS)   in [-1, +1]
   The flow gets a multiplicative quality factor, qK frozen at 0.5:
       dv3_clv = w3 * m3 * va * (1 + qK * clv)
   (up week closing at the high: x1.5; at the low: x0.5; mid-range: x1.0)
   then the parallel chain flow3_clv -> avfi3_clv -> spread_clv -> sp_z_clv
   with the SAME inherited constants (hlFlow, smooth, warmup, emaLen on the
   untouched emaV2, normWin). Attribution stays clean: exactly one flow
   input differs from the core.

3. Up/down vol asymmetry (Tsay ch. 3 GJR/EGARCH leverage; Javaheri,
   Inside Volatility Arbitrage: SPX leverage rho ~= -0.39; Williams:
   selling pressure shows on down moves). Separate na-tolerant EWMs of |r|
   over up weeks and down weeks only:
       sigma_up = SQRT_PI_2 * EWM(|r| where r > 0, hlVol)
       sigma_dn = SQRT_PI_2 * EWM(|r| where r < 0, hlVol)
       asym     = sigma_dn / (sigma_up + EPS)    (>1: down-vol dominance)
   DESCRIPTIVE ONLY: asym does not modify the flow; it is exposed for
   regime documentation, and any use requires a new signed amendment.

Discipline: this layer computes measurement series only. No forward
returns, no hypothesis evaluation (H10 waits on the signed Amendment 4),
no performance statistic on any window, holdout untouched beyond
availability/occupancy counts. Paper trading only.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from avfi_replication import (DEFAULT_PARAMS, EPS, SQRT_PI_2, FOUR_LN2,
                              ewm_halflife, pine_ema, pine_sma, pine_stdev)

V33_DEFAULT_PARAMS = {
    "qK": 0.5,     # CLV flow weight, FROZEN draft default (Amendment 4)
}


def compute_v33_layer(df: pd.DataFrame, av: pd.DataFrame,
                      **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    p = dict(V33_DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    p.update(overrides)
    core = dict(DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    hlVol, hlFlow = core["hlVol"], core["hlFlow"]
    smooth, warmup = core["smooth"], core["warmup"]
    normWin = core["normWin"]

    high, low, close, open_ = df["high"], df["low"], df["close"], df["open"]
    vol = df["volume"].astype(float)
    tp = (high + low + close) / 3.0

    # core inputs recomputed on the same code path (parity-tested T22)
    r = pd.Series(np.where((tp > 0) & (tp.shift(1) > 0),
                           np.log(tp / tp.shift(1)), 0.0), index=df.index)
    sigma_r = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm_halflife(r.abs(), hlVol)
    lv = pd.Series(np.where(vol > 0, np.log(np.where(vol > 0, vol, 1.0)), np.nan),
                   index=df.index)
    lva = ewm_halflife(lv, hlFlow).shift(1)
    va = np.exp(lva)
    # w3 / m3 identical to the core (see compute_avfi); emaV2 reused from av
    loghl2 = ewm_halflife(np.log(np.maximum(high / low, 1.0)) ** 2, hlVol)
    sigma_pk = np.sqrt(loghl2 / FOUR_LN2)
    sigma3 = np.sqrt(np.maximum(sigma_r ** 2, sigma_pk ** 2))
    z3 = r / (sigma3 + EPS)
    xx = core["k"] * np.maximum(0.0, z3.abs() - core["deadZone"])
    xx_c = np.clip(xx, -20.0, 20.0)
    w3 = ((np.exp(2 * xx_c) - 1.0) / (np.exp(2 * xx_c) + 1.0)) * np.sign(z3)
    rho3 = pd.Series(np.where(vol > 0, vol / (va + EPS), 0.0), index=df.index)
    m3 = rho3 / (1.0 + rho3 / core["gamma"])

    # ── 1. Garman-Klass floor ─────────────────────────────────────
    u = np.log(np.maximum(high / low, 1.0))
    c_ok = (open_ > 0) & (close > 0)
    c_gk = pd.Series(np.where(c_ok, np.log(np.where(c_ok, close / open_, 1.0)),
                              0.0), index=df.index)
    var_gk_w = np.maximum(0.5 * u ** 2 - (2.0 * np.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk ** 2,
                          0.0)
    sigma_gk = np.sqrt(np.maximum(ewm_halflife(var_gk_w, hlVol), EPS))
    sigma3_gk = np.sqrt(np.maximum(sigma_r ** 2, sigma_gk ** 2))

    # ── 2. Close-location value flow variant ──────────────────────
    rng = np.maximum(high - low, EPS)
    clv = pd.Series((2.0 * close - high - low) / rng, index=df.index)
    clv = clv.clip(-1.0, 1.0)
    dv3_clv = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + p["qK"] * clv)
    flow3_clv = ewm_halflife(pd.Series(dv3_clv, index=df.index), hlFlow)
    avfi3_clv_ = pine_ema(flow3_clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
    idx = np.arange(len(df))
    avfi3_clv = pd.Series(np.where(idx >= warmup, avfi3_clv_, np.nan),
                          index=df.index)
    spread_clv = avfi3_clv - av["emaV2"]
    mu_c = pine_sma(spread_clv, normWin)
    sd_c = pine_stdev(spread_clv, normWin)
    sp_z_clv = (spread_clv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

    # ── 3. Up/down vol asymmetry (descriptive only) ──────────────
    absr = r.abs()
    absr_up = pd.Series(np.where(r > 0, absr, np.nan), index=df.index)
    absr_dn = pd.Series(np.where(r < 0, absr, np.nan), index=df.index)
    sigma_up = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm_halflife(absr_up, hlVol)
    sigma_dn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm_halflife(absr_dn, hlVol)
    asym = sigma_dn / (sigma_up + EPS)

    return pd.DataFrame({
        "date": df["date"],
        "sigma_r": sigma_r, "sigma_pk": sigma_pk, "sigma_gk": sigma_gk,
        "sigma3": sigma3, "sigma3_gk": sigma3_gk,
        "clv": clv, "dv3_clv": pd.Series(dv3_clv, index=df.index),
        "avfi3_clv": avfi3_clv, "spread_clv": spread_clv,
        "sp_z_clv": sp_z_clv,
        "sigma_up": sigma_up, "sigma_dn": sigma_dn, "asym": asym,
    })

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v34_layer.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v34_research.pine (33013 bytes)

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
