// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
defTgt  = input.float(15.0, "V reversal-down target % ann (frozen)", minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3 draft, do not tune) ─
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (descriptive; H8/H9 tested null)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)
rzClamp   = input.float(6.0, "Robust z display clamp (visual only)",   minval=1.0, step=1.0, group=grpTB)

// ── Artifact window parameters (v3.3, observation only) ────────────
grpRoll   = "Artifact windows (observation only)"
showRoll  = input.bool(true, "Mark quarterly roll/expiry weeks",        group=grpRoll)

// ── v3.4 measurement-layer parameters (frozen draft, do not tune) ──
grpV34    = "v3.4 measurement layers (descriptive; Amd 4 withdrawn)"
qK        = input.float(0.5, "CLV flow weight qK (frozen at 0.5)", minval=0.0, maxval=1.0, step=0.1, group=grpV34)
showV34   = input.bool(true, "v3.4 panel rows",                     group=grpV34)

// ── PGT plane-geometry layer (Amendment 5 draft, do not tune) ──────
grpPGT    = "PGT plane geometry (descriptive; Amd 5 withdrawn)"
oscWin    = input.int(26,    "Truncated-oscillation window (registered 26)", minval=2, group=grpPGT)
showPGT   = input.bool(true, "PGT markers (weekly charts only)",              group=grpPGT)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity per v3.3.1 finding
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v3.4 MEASUREMENT LAYERS (new; everything above is untouched by these)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 1. Garman-Klass OHLC vol floor (uses the open; Parkinson uses H/L only).
//    |ln(C/O)| <= ln(H/L) whenever O, C sit inside the range, so the
//    per-week GK variance is non-negative by construction.
float u_gk   = math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0))
float c_gk   = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : 0.0
float varGk  = math.max(0.5 * u_gk * u_gk - (2.0 * math.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk * c_gk, 0.0)
float sigmaGK  = math.sqrt(math.max(ewm(varGk, hlVol), EPS))
float sigma3GK = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaGK * sigmaGK))

// 2. Close-location-value flow variant: exactly one input differs from
//    the core chain (the multiplicative result-quality factor).
float clv      = math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, (2.0 * close - high - low) / math.max(high - low, EPS)))
float dv3Clv   = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + qK * clv)
float flow3Clv = ewm(dv3Clv, hlFlow)
float avfi3Clv_ = ta.ema(flow3Clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
float avfi3Clv  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3Clv_ : na
float spreadClv = avfi3Clv - emaV2
float mu_c  = ta.sma(spreadClv, normWin)
float sd_c  = ta.stdev(spreadClv, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity
float spZClv = (spreadClv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

// 3. Up/down vol asymmetry: separate na-tolerant EWMs of |r| over down and
//    up weeks only; the EWM carries state across the opposite-sign weeks.
//    DESCRIPTIVE ONLY; never modifies the flow.
float absR   = math.abs(r)
float absRUp = r > 0 ? absR : na
float absRDn = r < 0 ? absR : na
float sigmaUp = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRUp, hlVol)
float sigmaDn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRDn, hlVol)
float asym    = sigmaDn / (sigmaUp + EPS)

// v3.4 series exposed in the Data Window (not charted; observation only)
plot(sigmaGK,  "v3.4 sigma GK",       display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3GK, "v3.4 sigma3 (GK)",     display=display.data_window)
plot(clv,      "v3.4 CLV",            display=display.data_window)
plot(asym,     "v3.4 asym dn/up",     display=display.data_window)
plot(spZClv,   "v3.4 spread z (CLV)", display=display.data_window)

// PGT series in the Data Window (observation only; nothing evaluated)
plot(hiPriorSpz, "PGT prior high-run sp_z", display=display.data_window)
plot(loPriorSpz, "PGT prior low-run sp_z",  display=display.data_window)
plot(zMax26,     "PGT zMax (oscWin)",       display=display.data_window)
plot(zMin26,     "PGT zMin (oscWin)",       display=display.data_window)
plot(g1Top ? 1 : 0, "PGT G1 top",    display=display.data_window)
plot(g1Bot ? 1 : 0, "PGT G1 bottom", display=display.data_window)
plot(g2Top ? 1 : 0, "PGT G2 top",    display=display.data_window)
plot(g2Bot ? 1 : 0, "PGT G2 bottom", display=display.data_window)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (new in v3.2). Weekly-chart logic, observation
// only. Registered in Amendment 3 draft BEFORE any evaluation.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution: a mean/stdev z is
// inflated by the very extreme it tries to measure, and weekly returns
// are fat-tailed, so quartiles beat moments here).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft: wave-free flow fade at price extremes (rule set B core,
// without the wave-count dependency). Top = new high with flow
// innovation below its median; bottom = mirror.
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft: effort-vs-result climax (Williams/VSA). Big volume effort
// (rho vs geometric-mean baseline) with a dead price result (|z| inside
// the dead zone) at a fresh extreme = absorption.
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (programme section 8 standing guard).
// One-sided CUSUM on the spread around the prior-bar rolling mean,
// drift allowance k, alarm h in sigma units; resets after an alarm.
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
// Display-only counter (v3.2.1): TB flags landed since the last CUSUM
// break. Observability; no series or hypothesis reads it.
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
// Stage 0d evidence: 7 of 11 canonical-vs-index divergence weeks sit in
// expiry/roll-adjacent windows. The third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec is
// quarterly expiry; the following week is the roll week. W-FRI weekly
// bars open ON the Friday, so the expiry bar's time IS the third
// Friday. On other weekly conventions the marker silently stays off
// (check the panel row).
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
// Warmup uses "" rather than na: Pine string-na handling is unreliable.
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── PGT plane-geometry terminal layer (v3.5, Amendment 5 draft) ────
// Frozen defaults: oscWin 26, band plane = core bandK. The window gate
// sp_z[oscWin-1] not na is exact pandas parity (na region is a
// contiguous prefix, so a valid bar at t-25 makes the whole window
// valid; pandas min_periods=oscWin returns na otherwise).
bool zWinOK  = not na(sp_z[oscWin - 1])
float zMax26 = zWinOK ? ta.highest(sp_z, oscWin) : na
float zMin26 = zWinOK ? ta.lowest(sp_z, oscWin)  : na

// G1 run tracking: prior reference = END bar of the previous
// fresh-extreme run (matches the validated pandas layer exactly).
var float hiRunEndSpz = na
var bool  hiInRun = false
var float loRunEndSpz = na
var bool  loInRun = false
float hiPriorSpz = na
float loPriorSpz = na
if newHigh and not hiInRun
    hiPriorSpz := hiRunEndSpz
if newLow and not loInRun
    loPriorSpz := loRunEndSpz

bool g1Top = newHigh and not na(sp_z) and not na(hiPriorSpz) and hiPriorSpz >= bandK and sp_z < hiPriorSpz
bool g1Bot = newLow  and not na(sp_z) and not na(loPriorSpz) and loPriorSpz <= -bandK and sp_z > loPriorSpz
bool g2Top = newHigh and not na(zMax26) and zMax26 >= bandK and zMin26 > -bandK
bool g2Bot = newLow  and not na(zMin26) and zMin26 <= -bandK and zMax26 < bandK
bool crossedDn = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z < 0 and sp_z[1] >= 0
bool crossedUp = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z > 0 and sp_z[1] <= 0
bool g3Top = crossedDn and stateNow == "Momentum up"
bool g3Bot = crossedUp and stateNow == "Momentum down"
bool pgtTop = g1Top or g2Top or g3Top
bool pgtBot = g1Bot or g2Bot or g3Bot

// Run-state update AFTER flag computation (G1 fires at run starts only).
if newHigh
    hiInRun := true
    hiRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    hiInRun := false
if newLow
    loInRun := true
    loRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    loInRun := false

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers + robust z ────────────────────────────────
// Markers on weekly charts only (v3.2.1): pbN is registered in weeks.
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)

// PGT plane-geometry markers (v3.5; descriptive only, Amendment 5
// withdrawn 2026-11-12 without execution; weekly charts only)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top",    shape.triangledown, location.top,    color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", shape.triangleup,   location.bottom, color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and g2Top and not g1Top, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation top",    shape.circle, location.top,    color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and g2Bot and not g1Bot, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation bottom", shape.circle, location.bottom, color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)
// Robust z display-clamped (v3.2.1): the value is untouched; only the
// plotted copy is clamped so a 200-sigma spike cannot squash the pane.
float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")
plotshape(showRoll and rollWindow, "Roll/expiry window", shape.diamond, location.bottom, color.new(color.gray, 30), size=size.tiny)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

// Last COMPLETED weekly bar's decision (what the paper log records)
float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for
// exact parity with the pandas programme (ta.stdev is population, ddof=0).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (pending Amendment 2): T signal x vol factor x regime throttle
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

// Rule set V (registered 2026-11-12, docs/ruleset_v_spec.md). Signal = T;
// sizing = graduated target modulation (20% -> 15% in reversal_down), the
// vol target then shrinks the position automatically when realized vol
// rises. Forward-track only: no p-value exists for V, the spent holdout is
// never evaluated; stage0h design bookkeeping was run once, pre-frozen spec.
float vTgt     = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? defTgt / 100.0 : volTgt / 100.0
float vFactor  = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, vTgt / (vol8 + EPS))
float vSize    = tLong ? vFactor : 0.0
plot(vTgt,    "V target (ann.)", display=display.data_window)
plot(vFactor, "V vol factor",    display=display.data_window)
plot(vSize,   "V paper size",    display=display.data_window)

// Signal flip + state change events (for alerts)
bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// Background tint while the trend signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 27, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 26)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)

    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, "12w log return", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "T if this wk closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, tLong ? "LONG" : "FLAT", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=tLong ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // U3 sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(volFactor, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8 ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

    string bkStr = nearBreak ? "within " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastBreakBar) + "w" : "quiet"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 15, "CUSUM break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 15, bkStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=nearBreak ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : na, text_size=size.small)
    string fxStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : newHigh and newLow ? "high & low" : newHigh ? "20w high" : newLow ? "20w low" : "—"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 16, "Fresh 20w extreme", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 16, fxStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 17, "TB flags since break", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 18, "Roll/expiry window", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 18, rwStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=rollWindow ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : na, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 19, "Volume source", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 19, hasRealVol ? "real" : "PROXY (no volume)", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? na : color.new(color.red, 55), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 20, "Decision cadence", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 20, "Fri close → Mon open", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 21, "Lookbacks frozen", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 21, "12w / 8w / 20w / thr 0.5 / V tgt 20-15%", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 22, "Holdout", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 22, "spent 2021+ (battery)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 23, "Tops/bottoms flags", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 23, "descriptive (tested null)", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

    // v3.4 measurement rows
    if showV34
        string gkStr = na(sigma3GK) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(sigma3GK, "0.####") + " vs " + (na(sigma3) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(sigma3, "0.####"))
        table.cell(tbl, 0, 24, "sigma3 GK vs PK floor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
        table.cell(tbl, 1, 24, gkStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
        string clvStr = na(clv) ? "n/a" : (clv > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(clv, "0.##")
        table.cell(tbl, 0, 25, "CLV (close location)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
        table.cell(tbl, 1, 25, clvStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
        string zcStr = na(spZClv) ? "n/a" : (spZClv > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(spZClv, "0.##") + " σ"
        table.cell(tbl, 0, 26, "Spread z (CLV flow)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
        table.cell(tbl, 1, 26, zcStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
        string asymStr = na(asym) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(asym, "0.##") + "×"
        table.cell(tbl, 0, 27, "Down/up vol asym", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
        table.cell(tbl, 1, 27, asymStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // PGT row (v3.5; descriptive, Amendment 5 withdrawn)
    if showPGT
        string pgtStr = not weeklyOK ? "n/a (weekly)" : ""
        if weeklyOK
            if g1Top
                pgtStr := pgtStr + (str.length(pgtStr) > 0 ? "/" : "") + "G1T"
            if g1Bot
                pgtStr := pgtStr + (str.length(pgtStr) > 0 ? "/" : "") + "G1B"
            if g2Top
                pgtStr := pgtStr + (str.length(pgtStr) > 0 ? "/" : "") + "G2T"
            if g2Bot
                pgtStr := pgtStr + (str.length(pgtStr) > 0 ? "/" : "") + "G2B"
            pgtStr := str.length(pgtStr) == 0 ? "—" : pgtStr
        table.cell(tbl, 0, 28, "PGT (draft, unevaluated)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
        table.cell(tbl, 1, 28, pgtStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=pgtTop or pgtBot ? color.new(color.yellow, 55) : na, text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Momentum down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Momentum down"), "Momentum-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered momentum_down (deep-bear regime, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top", "AVFI v3.5: fresh 20w high, effort plane refused the band excursion (descriptive; Amendment 5 withdrawn)")
alertcondition(g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", "AVFI v3.5: fresh 20w low, effort plane refused the band excursion (descriptive; Amendment 5 withdrawn)")
alertcondition(climaxTop or climaxBot, "Climax effort>result (descriptive)", "AVFI v3.3: volume surprise with dead price result at a fresh extreme (descriptive; H9 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(cusumAlarm, "CUSUM regime break", "AVFI v3.3: spread CUSUM regime-break alarm (z-score window suspect)")

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v4h_paperbot.pine (17464 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v4h [paper-bot edition]", shorttitle="AVFI v4h", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=1000)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AVFI v4h PAPER-BOT EDITION (Nov 2026). The 4-hour display twin of the
// registered live paper engine (research/v4h_bot.py; spec
// docs/v4h_spec.md; rule set V4h REGISTERED 2026-11-12; track opened
// flat at 2026-11-12 02:42 Malta, equity 100,000 USD). Paste on an NQ
// futures 4H chart (session-anchored 4h bars).
//
// WHAT THIS IS: the AVFI "v3 vs v2 [spread axis]" core VERBATIM
// (validated pandas parity, stage 1; changing it breaks replication)
// plus the V4h rule block with the CORRECT 4h annualization
// sqrt(barsPerWeek x 52) = sqrt(1560). The weekly v4 hardcodes
// sqrt(52); on a 4h chart it would silently disable vol targeting -
// that is why V4h is its own registered rule set, not a port.
//
// WHAT THIS IS NOT: not a strategy, no orders, no equity curve, no
// evaluation, no performance statistic. The ENGINE is the sole
// booking source (daily 23:30 Malta run, append-only log); this chart
// observes and cross-checks.
//
// DATA-FEED CAVEAT: TradingView NQ bars and the engine's Yahoo-derived
// CME-anchored buckets come from different feeds; expect small
// differences at warmup and bar edges. Cross-check standard: same
// regime state, same signal direction, similar size - not tick parity.
//
// Discipline: V4h is EXPERIMENTAL, forward paper only; NO p-value
// exists for it and none may be computed; the engine never books a bar
// completed before its track start; nothing here places, sizes or
// evaluates a real trade.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── V4h rule parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ────────
grpV4h  = "Rule set V4h (registered; do not tune)"
momLen     = input.int(12,    "Momentum lookback, 4h bars (frozen 12 = 48h)", minval=1, maxval=200, group=grpV4h)
volWin     = input.int(8,     "Vol window, 4h bars (frozen 8)",                minval=2, group=grpV4h)
barsPerWeek= input.int(30,    "4h bars per week (frozen 30; ann. sqrt(1560))", minval=1, group=grpV4h)
volTgt     = input.float(20.0,"Vol target % ann (frozen)",                    minval=1.0, group=grpV4h)
defTgt     = input.float(15.0,"Reversal-down target % ann (frozen)",           minval=1.0, group=grpV4h)
showBg     = input.bool(true, "Trend signal background",                      group=grpV4h)
showTbl    = input.bool(true, "V4h panel",                                     group=grpV4h)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722039781

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity per v3.3.1 finding
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ── Regime state (same formula as the weekly edition) ─────────────
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool is4h = timeframe.period == "240"
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE SET V4h (docs/v4h_spec.md; registered 2026-11-12; the engine
// research/v4h_bot.py computes this on its own Yahoo-derived 4h bars
// and books the paper track; this chart mirrors it for cross-checks)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 48h momentum signal: 12-bar 4h log return (T structure on the 4h grid)
float mom12h = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong4 = not na(mom12h) and mom12h > 0
float mom12hLast = mom12h[1]
bool  tLong4Last = not na(mom12hLast) and mom12hLast > 0

// 8-bar (32h) realized vol, annualized sqrt(barsPerWeek*52) = sqrt(1560).
// Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) for exact parity with the engine/pandas
// (ta.stdev default is population, ddof=0 - the v3.3.1 lesson).
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8h   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(barsPerWeek * 52.0)

// Graduated target modulation (the V mechanism): 20% ann., cut to 15%
// in reversal_down. NOT a position throttle.
float tgt4h    = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? defTgt / 100.0 : volTgt / 100.0
float vFactor4 = na(vol8h) or vol8h <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, tgt4h / (vol8h + EPS))
float vSize4   = tLong4 ? vFactor4 : 0.0

// signal age (bars since the last 48h-momentum flip)
bool flip4 = not na(tLong4[1]) and tLong4 != tLong4[1]
var int sinceBar4 = na
if flip4
    sinceBar4 := bar_index

// Background tint while the 4h signal is long (observation display)
bgcolor(showBg and is4h and tLong4 ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="V4h long")

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Data-window plots (engine cross-check against v4h_card.json) ──
plot(mom12h,   "v4h mom12h (48h log ret)", display=display.data_window)
plot(vol8h,    "v4h vol8h ann.",            display=display.data_window)
plot(tgt4h,    "v4h target ann.",           display=display.data_window)
plot(vFactor4, "v4h vol factor",            display=display.data_window)
plot(vSize4,   "v4h paper size",            display=display.data_window)

// ── V4h decision panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 15)

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 14)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = is4h ? "" : "  [4H chart only]"

    // header
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI v4h", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, "paper-bot edition" + tfNote, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime (4h)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // V4h signal: last CLOSED bar's decision (what the engine books)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "V4h signal (last closed bar)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 3, is4h and tLong4Last ? "LONG" : is4h ? "FLAT" : "n/a", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=is4h and tLong4Last ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string mStr = na(mom12hLast) ? "n/a" : (mom12hLast > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mom12hLast * 100, "0.0") + "% " + str.tostring(momLen * 4) + "h"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 4, str.tostring(momLen * 4) + "h log return (last close)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 4, mStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 5, "V4h if this bar closes now", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 5, is4h and tLong4 ? "LONG" : is4h ? "FLAT" : "n/a", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=is4h and tLong4 ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)

    // sizing
    string vStr = na(vol8h) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8h * 100, "0.0") + "% / x" + str.tostring(vFactor4, "0.00")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 6, "vol8h ann / factor", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 6, vStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string tgtStr = str.tostring(stateNow == "Reversal down" ? defTgt : volTgt, "0") + "% ann." + (stateNow == "Reversal down" ? " (defensive 15%)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 7, "V4h target", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 7, tgtStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    string szStr = is4h ? str.tostring(vSize4, "0.00") + "×" : "n/a"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 8, "V4h paper size", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 8, szStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=vSize4 > 0 and is4h ? cLong : cFlat, text_size=size.small)
    string ageStr = na(sinceBar4) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(bar_index - sinceBar4) + " bars"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 9, "Signal age (since flip)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 9, ageStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // provenance + discipline
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 10, "Booking source", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 10, "engine v4h_bot.py (23:30 Malta)", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 11, "Engine track", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 11, "registered 2026-11-12; no backtest", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 12, "Data feed", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 12, "TV ≠ engine feed (cross-check)", text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 13, "Volume source", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 13, hasRealVol ? "real" : "PROXY (no volume)", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? na : color.new(color.red, 55), text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 14, "Discipline", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 14, "no p-value; paper only", text_color=color.new(color.orange, 0), text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label ──────────────────────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate + "  |  V4h " + (tLong4 ? "LONG x" + str.tostring(vSize4, "0.00") : "FLAT")
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]
alertcondition(flip4, "V4h signal flip", "AVFI v4h: 48h momentum signal flipped (paper bot cross-check)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "V4h reversal-down entry", "AVFI v4h: 4h regime entered reversal_down (target cut to 15%, paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI v4h: 4h AVFI regime state changed")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v4h_paperbot.pine

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v5_clean.pine (45832 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v5 [book layers + cross-market clock]", shorttitle="AVFI v5", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=5000)

// ═══ AVFI v5 RESEARCH EDITION · CLEAN BUILD (14 Nov 2026) ═══
// Code identical to pipeline/strategies/avfi_v5_research.pine
// (session 73): comments and provenance notes removed, nothing
// else changed; the annotated file remains the source of truth.
// INDICATOR, not a strategy: observation only, nothing evaluated,
// paper only, NQ 2021+ holdout spent and never touched. Weekly NQ
// W-FRI chart. For the TradingView paste cross-check, copy from
// the stored file or its canvas, never from chat text (chat
// corrupts quote marks).

// ── Core parameters (inherited, unchanged) ─────────────────────────
hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

// ── Visual parameters ──────────────────────────────────────────────
normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

// ── Research overlay parameters (registered constants, do not tune) ─
grpRes  = "Research overlay"
momLen  = input.int(12,     "Trend lookback (frozen at 12)", minval=1, maxval=52, group=grpRes)
volWin  = input.int(8,      "Vol window (frozen at 8)",      minval=2, group=grpRes)
volTgt  = input.float(20.0, "Vol target % ann (frozen)",    minval=1.0, group=grpRes)
thrAmt  = input.float(50.0, "Reversal-down throttle % (frozen)", minval=1.0, maxval=100.0, group=grpRes)
showBg  = input.bool(true,  "Trend signal background",      group=grpRes)
showTbl = input.bool(true,  "Research panel",               group=grpRes)

// ── Tops/bottoms layer parameters (Amendment 3, do not tune) ───────
grpTB     = "Tops/bottoms layer (descriptive; H8/H9 tested null)"
pbN       = input.int(20,    "Price extreme lookback (registered 20)", minval=5, group=grpTB)
fadeZ     = input.float(0.0, "Flow fade threshold (robust z)",        group=grpTB)
climRho   = input.float(1.5, "Climax volume surprise ρ",              minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
cusumK    = input.float(0.5, "CUSUM drift allowance k (σ)",            minval=0.1, group=grpTB)
cusumH    = input.float(4.0, "CUSUM alarm h (σ)",                     minval=1.0, group=grpTB)
breakHold = input.int(13,    "Break flag hold (weeks)",                minval=1, group=grpTB)
showTB    = input.bool(true, "Top/bottom markers",                     group=grpTB)
showRZ    = input.bool(false,"Show robust z line",                      group=grpTB)
rzClamp   = input.float(6.0, "Robust z display clamp (visual only)",   minval=1.0, step=1.0, group=grpTB)

// ── Artifact window parameters (v3.3, observation only) ────────────
grpRoll   = "Artifact windows (observation only)"
showRoll  = input.bool(true, "Mark quarterly roll/expiry weeks",        group=grpRoll)

// ── v3.4 measurement-layer parameters (frozen draft, do not tune) ──
grpV34    = "v3.4 measurement layers (descriptive; Amd 4 withdrawn)"
qK        = input.float(0.5, "CLV flow weight qK (frozen at 0.5)", minval=0.0, maxval=1.0, step=0.1, group=grpV34)
showV34   = input.bool(true, "v3.4 panel rows",                     group=grpV34)

// ── PGT plane-geometry layer (Amendment 5 withdrawn, do not tune) ──
grpPGT    = "PGT plane geometry (descriptive; Amd 5 withdrawn)"
oscWin    = input.int(26,    "Truncated-oscillation window (registered 26)", minval=2, group=grpPGT)
showPGT   = input.bool(true, "PGT markers (weekly charts only)",              group=grpPGT)

// ── SMH compression state (registered definition, do not tune) ────
grpSMH    = "SMH compression state (registered; design bookkeeping)"
lowCutIn  = input.float(0.01739, "Compression cut LOW (frozen; do not tune)", minval=0.0, group=grpSMH)
showSmhBg = input.bool(true,  "Compression-region background (dim blue)",       group=grpSMH)

// ── v4 draft primed state (Andrea spec; DRAFT, unevaluated) ────────
grpV4     = "Primed draft state (Andrea spec; UNEVALUATED, not a finding)"
atrLen    = input.int(30,    "ATR length (bars; weekly chart = WEEKS, not days)", minval=1, group=grpV4)
rvolWin   = input.int(52,    "rvol percentile window (bars)",                    minval=10, group=grpV4)
primedCut = input.float(33.0,"rvol percentile cut (%)",                        minval=1.0, maxval=99.0, group=grpV4)
compMin   = input.int(20,    "Min consecutive compressed weeks",                minval=1, group=grpV4)
rangeWin  = input.int(52,    "Range window (weeks; spec's 365 days translated)", minval=10, group=grpV4)
rangeCut  = input.float(0.35, "Range position cut",                            minval=0.0, maxval=1.0, step=0.05, group=grpV4)
showPrimedBg = input.bool(true, "Primed background (bright teal, DRAFT)",      group=grpV4)

// ── v4 curvature layer (descriptive) ───────────────────────────────
grpCurv   = "Spread curvature (descriptive; H8 fade family tested null)"
showCurv  = input.bool(false, "Curvature line (curvature/sd_s, clamped ±3)",    group=grpCurv)

// ── v4 extremity exports (Amendment 11 DRAFT, do not tune) ────────
grpExt    = "Extremity exports (Amendment 11 DRAFT; descriptive, unevaluated)"
extMinCov = input.int(100,  "Pctile min valid prior bars (coverage gate)", minval=10, group=grpExt)

// ── v5 book layers (descriptive; nothing registered, nothing evaluated) ─
grpV5      = "v5 book layers (descriptive displays; no claims)"
ewmaLam    = input.float(0.94, "EWMA vol lambda (RiskMetrics; frozen display)", minval=0.5, maxval=0.999, step=0.01, group=grpV5)
rollCovWin = input.int(52,    "Roll spread cov window (display-only choice)",   minval=13, group=grpV5)

// ── Amendment 9 cross-market clock (registered cuts, do not tune) ──
grpXM     = "Amd 9 cross-market clock (registered; booking tool books, chart observes)"
xmMarket  = input.string("Auto", "Market", options=["Auto", "NQ", "GOLD", "NIKKEI", "DAX", "Manual"], group=grpXM)
xmLowMan  = input.float(0.0,   "Manual LOW cut (weekly sigma_w)",  minval=0.0, group=grpXM)
xmHighMan = input.float(0.0,   "Manual HIGH cut (weekly sigma_w)", minval=0.0, group=grpXM)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781
K_YZ      = 0.34 / 2.34
SPZ_EXT   = 2.0
RZ_EXT    = 2.0
RHO_EXT   = 2.0
ASYM_HI   = 2.0
ASYM_LO   = 0.5

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Prior-bar percentile rank helper (v4; non-repainting core math) ─
pctRank(float src, int win, int minCov) =>
    float result = na
    if not na(src)
        int cnt = 0
        int tot = 0
        for i = 1 to win
            float v = src[i]
            if not na(v)
                tot += 1
                if v < src
                    cnt += 1
        result := tot >= minCov ? cnt * 1.0 / tot : na
    result

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// 1. Garman-Klass OHLC vol floor.
float u_gk   = math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0))
float c_gk   = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : 0.0
float varGk  = math.max(0.5 * u_gk * u_gk - (2.0 * math.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk * c_gk, 0.0)
float sigmaGK  = math.sqrt(math.max(ewm(varGk, hlVol), EPS))
float sigma3GK = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaGK * sigmaGK))

// 2. Close-location-value flow variant (Amendment 4 withdrawn; descriptive).
float clv      = math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, (2.0 * close - high - low) / math.max(high - low, EPS)))
float dv3Clv   = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + qK * clv)
float flow3Clv = ewm(dv3Clv, hlFlow)
float avfi3Clv_ = ta.ema(flow3Clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
float avfi3Clv  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3Clv_ : na
float spreadClv = avfi3Clv - emaV2
float mu_c  = ta.sma(spreadClv, normWin)
float sd_c  = ta.stdev(spreadClv, normWin, false)
float spZClv = (spreadClv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

// 3. Up/down vol asymmetry (descriptive only).
float absR   = math.abs(r)
float absRUp = r > 0 ? absR : na
float absRDn = r < 0 ? absR : na
float sigmaUp = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRUp, hlVol)
float sigmaDn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRDn, hlVol)
float asym    = sigmaDn / (sigmaUp + EPS)

float oYz = open > 0 and not na(close[1]) and close[1] > 0 ? math.log(open / close[1]) : na
float cYz = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : na
float uYz = open > 0 ? math.log(high / open) : na
float dYz = open > 0 ? math.log(low / open) : na
float rsBar = na(uYz) or na(cYz) or na(dYz) ? na : math.max(uYz * (uYz - cYz) + dYz * (dYz - cYz), 0.0)
float varOYz = ewm(oYz * oYz, hlVol)
float varCYz = ewm(cYz * cYz, hlVol)
float varRS  = ewm(rsBar, hlVol)
float sigmaYZ = math.sqrt(math.max(varOYz + K_YZ * varCYz + (1.0 - K_YZ) * varRS, 0.0))
float sigma3YZ = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaYZ * sigmaYZ))

float velocity  = ta.change(spread)
float curvature = ta.change(velocity)
float curvZ     = na(curvature) or na(sd_s) ? na : curvature / (sd_s + EPS)

float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── PGT plane-geometry terminal layer (v3.5; Amendment 5 withdrawn) ─
bool zWinOK  = not na(sp_z[oscWin - 1])
float zMax26 = zWinOK ? ta.highest(sp_z, oscWin) : na
float zMin26 = zWinOK ? ta.lowest(sp_z, oscWin)  : na

var float hiRunEndSpz = na
var bool  hiInRun = false
var float loRunEndSpz = na
var bool  loInRun = false
float hiPriorSpz = na
float loPriorSpz = na
if newHigh and not hiInRun
    hiPriorSpz := hiRunEndSpz
if newLow and not loInRun
    loPriorSpz := loRunEndSpz

bool g1Top = newHigh and not na(sp_z) and not na(hiPriorSpz) and hiPriorSpz >= bandK and sp_z < hiPriorSpz
bool g1Bot = newLow  and not na(sp_z) and not na(loPriorSpz) and loPriorSpz <= -bandK and sp_z > loPriorSpz
bool g2Top = newHigh and not na(zMax26) and zMax26 >= bandK and zMin26 > -bandK
bool g2Bot = newLow  and not na(zMin26) and zMin26 <= -bandK and zMax26 < bandK
bool crossedDn = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z < 0 and sp_z[1] >= 0
bool crossedUp = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z > 0 and sp_z[1] <= 0
bool g3Top = crossedDn and stateNow == "Momentum up"
bool g3Bot = crossedUp and stateNow == "Momentum down"
bool pgtTop = g1Top or g2Top or g3Top
bool pgtBot = g1Bot or g2Bot or g3Bot

if newHigh
    hiInRun := true
    hiRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    hiInRun := false
if newLow
    loInRun := true
    loRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    loInRun := false

float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

string tkrUp  = str.upper(syminfo.ticker)
bool goldSym = str.contains(tkrUp, "GC") or str.contains(tkrUp, "XAU") or str.contains(tkrUp, "GOLD")
bool nkSym   = str.contains(tkrUp, "N225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NI225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NIKKEI") or str.contains(tkrUp, "JP225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "JPN225")
bool daxSym  = str.contains(tkrUp, "GDAXI") or str.contains(tkrUp, "DAX") or str.contains(tkrUp, "GER40")
bool nqSym   = str.contains(tkrUp, "NQ") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NDX") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NASDAQ") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NAS100") or str.contains(tkrUp, "USTEC")
string xmDet = xmMarket == "GOLD" ? "GOLD" : xmMarket == "NIKKEI" ? "NIKKEI" : xmMarket == "DAX" ? "DAX" : xmMarket == "Manual" ? "MANUAL" : xmMarket == "NQ" ? "NQ" : goldSym ? "GOLD" : nkSym ? "NIKKEI" : daxSym ? "DAX" : nqSym ? "NQ" : "NQ (default)"
float clockLow  = xmDet == "GOLD" ? 0.016023 : xmDet == "NIKKEI" ? 0.019622 : xmDet == "DAX" ? 0.019349 : xmDet == "MANUAL" ? xmLowMan  : lowCutIn
float clockHigh = xmDet == "GOLD" ? 0.022098 : xmDet == "NIKKEI" ? 0.027155 : xmDet == "DAX" ? 0.026782 : xmDet == "MANUAL" ? xmHighMan : 0.02697
bool  isMacroReg = xmDet == "GOLD" or xmDet == "NIKKEI" or xmDet == "DAX"

float sigW8 = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var)
bool  inCompression = weeklyOK and not na(sigW8) and sigW8 <= clockLow
bool  inStress      = weeklyOK and not na(sigW8) and sigW8 >  clockHigh
var int weeksInComp = 0
weeksInComp := inCompression ? weeksInComp + 1 : 0
var int weeksInStress = 0
weeksInStress := inStress ? weeksInStress + 1 : 0
bool compCross  = inCompression and not inCompression[1]
bool stressCross = inStress and not inStress[1]

float atrRaw  = ta.atr(atrLen)
float rvolPct = na(atrRaw) ? na : pctRank(atrRaw, rvolWin, rvolWin) * 100.0
var int compWeeks = 0
if not na(rvolPct) and rvolPct <= primedCut
    compWeeks += 1
else
    compWeeks := 0
float loR = ta.lowest(low, rangeWin)
float hiR = ta.highest(high, rangeWin)
float rangePos = (close - loR) / math.max(hiR - loR, syminfo.mintick)
bool isPrimed = weeklyOK and not na(rvolPct) and compWeeks >= compMin and rvolPct <= primedCut and not na(rangePos) and rangePos <= rangeCut
bool primedCross = isPrimed and not isPrimed[1]

float absSpz   = na(sp_z) ? na : math.abs(sp_z)
float absRz    = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.abs(sp_rz)
float extPctSpz = pctRank(absSpz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctRz  = pctRank(absRz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctMom = pctRank(na(mom12) ? na : math.abs(mom12), normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctVol = pctRank(vol8, normWin, extMinCov)
bool  spzHi    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z >= SPZ_EXT
bool  spzLo    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z <= -SPZ_EXT
bool  spzAbs2  = spzHi or spzLo
bool  rzAbs2   = not na(sp_rz) and math.abs(sp_rz) >= RZ_EXT
bool  rhoBurst = not na(rho) and rho >= RHO_EXT
bool  asymHi   = not na(asym) and asym >= ASYM_HI
bool  asymLo   = not na(asym) and asym <= ASYM_LO
bool  anyExt   = spzAbs2 or rzAbs2 or rhoBurst or asymHi or asymLo

var float varEw = na
varEw := na(varEw) ? r * r : ewmaLam * varEw + (1.0 - ewmaLam) * r * r
float sigWEwma  = math.sqrt(math.max(varEw, 0.0))
float volEwmaAnn = sigWEwma * math.sqrt(52.0)

float covRoll = ta.covariance(r, r[1], rollCovWin)
float rollSpreadPct = na(covRoll) or covRoll >= 0.0 ? na : 200.0 * math.sqrt(-covRoll)

float vxnClose = request.security("CBOE:VXN", timeframe.period, close, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
float vxnGap = na(vxnClose) or na(vol8) ? na : vxnClose - vol8 * 100.0

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers (v4: confirmed-bar gated, no intrabar flicker)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)

// PGT markers (confirmed-bar gated)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top",    shape.triangledown, location.top,    color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", shape.triangleup,   location.bottom, color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Top and not g1Top, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation top",    shape.circle, location.top,    color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Bot and not g1Bot, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation bottom", shape.circle, location.bottom, color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)

float rzDisp = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.max(-rzClamp, math.min(rzClamp, sp_rz))
plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)

float curvDisp = na(curvZ) ? na : math.max(-3.0, math.min(3.0, curvZ))
plot(showCurv ? curvDisp : na, "Spread curvature (curv/sd_s, clamped; descriptive)", color=color.new(color.gray, 60), linewidth=1)

// Backgrounds (all labeled, all observation only)
bgcolor(showBg and timeframe.isweekly and tLong ? color.new(color.green, 93) : na, title="T long")
bgcolor(showTB and cusumAlarm ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title="CUSUM regime break")
bgcolor(showSmhBg and inCompression ? color.new(color.blue, 90) : na, title="SMH compression region (registered; bookkeeping)")
bgcolor(showSmhBg and inStress ? color.new(color.red, 92) : na, title="SMH stress region (registered mirror clock; bookkeeping)")
bgcolor(showPrimedBg and isPrimed ? color.new(color.teal, 80) : na, title="Primed (DRAFT, unevaluated)")
plotshape(showRoll and rollWindow, "Roll/expiry window", shape.diamond, location.bottom, color.new(color.gray, 30), size=size.tiny)

// ── Data Window exports (observation only; NOTHING evaluated) ─────

// registered core (avfi_series.csv parity; "state" is a string column)
plot(sigma3, "sigma3", display=display.data_window)
plot(rho,    "rho3",   display=display.data_window)
plot(spread, "spread", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_z,   "sp_z",   display=display.data_window)

// research overlay + the SMH trigger series (registered)
plot(mom12, "mom12",  display=display.data_window)
plot(vol8,  "vol8",   display=display.data_window)
plot(sigW8, "sig_w8", display=display.data_window)

// tops/bottoms layer (avfi_v32_tb_series.csv parity)
plot(med_s,  "med_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q25_s,  "q25_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q75_s,  "q75_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(rsig_s, "rsig_s", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_rz,  "sp_rz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(na(hhN)   ? na : newHigh ? 1 : 0, "newHigh",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(llN)   ? na : newLow  ? 1 : 0, "newLow",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : divTop  ? 1 : 0, "divTop",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : divBot  ? 1 : 0, "divBot",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(rho)   ? na : climax  ? 1 : 0, "climax",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sd_s[1]) ? na : cusumAlarm ? 1 : 0, "cusumAlarm", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sd_s[1]) ? na : nearBreak   ? 1 : 0, "nearBreak",   display=display.data_window)

// v3.4 measurement layers (avfi_v34_series.csv parity)
plot(sigmaGK,  "sigma_gk",  display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3GK, "sigma3_gk", display=display.data_window)
plot(clv,      "clv",       display=display.data_window)
plot(spZClv,   "sp_z_clv",  display=display.data_window)
plot(asym,     "asym",      display=display.data_window)

// PGT plane-geometry layer (v3.5 lineage)
plot(hiPriorSpz, "hiPriorSpz", display=display.data_window)
plot(loPriorSpz, "loPriorSpz", display=display.data_window)
plot(zMax26,     "zMax26",     display=display.data_window)
plot(zMin26,     "zMin26",     display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)   ? na : g1Top ? 1 : 0, "g1Top", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)   ? na : g1Bot ? 1 : 0, "g1Bot", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(zMax26) ? na : g2Top ? 1 : 0, "g2Top", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(zMin26) ? na : g2Bot ? 1 : 0, "g2Bot", display=display.data_window)

// v4 layers (all descriptive; Amendment 11 draft cells unevaluated)
plot(sigmaYZ,   "sigma_yz",   display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3YZ,  "sigma3_yz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(velocity,  "velocity",   display=display.data_window)
plot(curvature, "curvature", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctSpz, "ext_pct_spz", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctRz,  "ext_pct_rz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctMom, "ext_pct_mom", display=display.data_window)
plot(extPctVol, "ext_pct_vol", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)  ? na : spzHi    ? 1 : 0, "ext_spz_hi",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_z)  ? na : spzLo    ? 1 : 0, "ext_spz_lo",    display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sp_rz) ? na : rzAbs2   ? 1 : 0, "ext_rz_abs2",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(rho)   ? na : rhoBurst ? 1 : 0, "ext_rho_burst", display=display.data_window)
plot(na(asym)  ? na : asymHi   ? 1 : 0, "ext_asym_hi",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(asym)  ? na : asymLo   ? 1 : 0, "ext_asym_lo",   display=display.data_window)
plot(na(sigW8)   ? na : inCompression ? 1 : 0, "smh_in_compression", display=display.data_window)

// v5 exports (registered clock mirror + book measurement)
plot(na(sigW8) ? na : inStress ? 1 : 0, "smh_in_stress", display=display.data_window)
plot(sigWEwma,  "sig_w_ewma",  display=display.data_window)

// ── Research panel (corner table, last bar only) ───────────────────
var table tbl = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 40, border_width=1, frame_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.gray, 60))

if barstate.islast and showTbl
    table.clear(tbl, 0, 0, 1, 39)
    color cHead = color.new(color.black, 0)
    color cTxt  = color.white
    color cDim  = color.new(color.silver, 0)
    color cLong = color.new(color.green, 55)
    color cFlat = color.new(color.red, 55)
    string tfNote = timeframe.isweekly ? "" : "  [weekly chart only]"

    // header (v4: proxy-volume + live-bar badges)
    string hdr2 = "paper only" + tfNote + (hasRealVol ? "" : " | PROXY VOL") + (barstate.isrealtime ? " | LIVE BAR (forming)" : "")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 0, "AVFI RESEARCH", text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=cHead, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 0, hdr2, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=hasRealVol ? cHead : color.new(color.red, 40), text_size=size.small)

    // regime
    string stNow = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 1, "Regime", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 1, stNow, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 75) : color.new(color.red, 75)), text_size=size.small)
    string zStr = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " σ"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 2, "Spread z", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 2, zStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    // rule set T
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 3, "T signal (last closed wk)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 34, "Extremity flags (Amd 11 draft)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 35, "|mom12| / vol8 prior pctile", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 36, "EWMA vol (λ 0.94)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 36, ewStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    table.cell(tbl, 0, 37, "Roll implied spread (52w)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 37, rlStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    string vxStr = na(vxnClose) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vxnClose, "0.0") + " vs vol8 " + (na(vol8) ? "n/a" : str.tostring(vol8 * 100.0, "0.0")) + " (" + (na(vxnGap) ? "n/a" : (vxnGap > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(vxnGap, "0.0")) + "; VRP survives)"
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 38, "VXN vs vol8 (gap)", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 38, vxStr, text_color=cTxt, text_size=size.small)

    string xmStr = xmDet + " | LOW " + str.tostring(clockLow, "0.#####") + " / HIGH " + str.tostring(clockHigh, "0.#####")
    table.cell(tbl, 0, 39, "Clock market / cuts", text_color=cDim, text_size=size.small)
    table.cell(tbl, 1, 39, xmStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=isMacroReg ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : na, text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
