##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v5_mathcore.pine (8579 bytes)
//@version=5
indicator("AVFI v5 [math core]", shorttitle="AVFI v5 core", overlay=false, precision=4, max_bars_back=5000)

// AVFI v5 MATH CORE (14 Nov 2026): every formula line verbatim from
// avfi_v5_research.pine; display edition without research layers,
// flags, panel or alerts. Nothing evaluated; paper only.

hlVol    = input.float(30.0, "Vol halflife (σ)",        minval=1)
hlFlow   = input.float(65.0, "Flow halflife",           minval=1)
k        = input.float(1.0,  "Gate steepness k",        minval=0.1, step=0.1)
deadZone = input.float(0.2,  "Dead zone a (σ units)",   minval=0.0, step=0.05)
gamma    = input.float(2.5,  "Soft volume cap γ",       minval=1.0, step=0.5)
smooth   = input.int(3,      "EMA smoothing",           minval=1)
warmup   = input.int(260,    "Warmup bars",             minval=0)
emaLen   = input.int(105,    "EMA(v2) length",          minval=1)

normWin  = input.int(500,      "Normalization window",  minval=50)
bandK    = input.float(1.5,    "Guide band width (σ)",  minval=0.5, step=0.5)
fillMode = input.string("Ribbon", "Visualization", options=["Ribbon", "Normalized histogram", "Both"])

qK      = input.float(0.5, "CLV flow weight qK (frozen at 0.5)", minval=0.0, maxval=1.0, step=0.1)
ewmaLam = input.float(0.94, "EWMA vol lambda (RiskMetrics)", minval=0.5, maxval=0.999, step=0.01)

EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781
K_YZ      = 0.34 / 2.34

ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// AVFI v3: range-aware sigma floor, log-volume normalization, gated weight
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// AVFI v2 + slow EMA: the disagreement baseline
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// Spread + rolling distribution
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// Garman-Klass OHLC vol floor
float u_gk   = math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0))
float c_gk   = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : 0.0
float varGk  = math.max(0.5 * u_gk * u_gk - (2.0 * math.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk * c_gk, 0.0)
float sigmaGK  = math.sqrt(math.max(ewm(varGk, hlVol), EPS))
float sigma3GK = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaGK * sigmaGK))

// Close-location-value flow variant (Williams effort-result)
float clv      = math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, (2.0 * close - high - low) / math.max(high - low, EPS)))
float dv3Clv   = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + qK * clv)
float flow3Clv = ewm(dv3Clv, hlFlow)
float avfi3Clv_ = ta.ema(flow3Clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
float avfi3Clv  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3Clv_ : na
float spreadClv = avfi3Clv - emaV2
float mu_c  = ta.sma(spreadClv, normWin)
float sd_c  = ta.stdev(spreadClv, normWin, false)
float spZClv = (spreadClv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

// Up/down vol asymmetry
float absR   = math.abs(r)
float absRUp = r > 0 ? absR : na
float absRDn = r < 0 ? absR : na
float sigmaUp = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRUp, hlVol)
float sigmaDn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRDn, hlVol)
float asym    = sigmaDn / (sigmaUp + EPS)

// Yang-Zhang OHLC vol floor (overnight gap included)
float oYz = open > 0 and not na(close[1]) and close[1] > 0 ? math.log(open / close[1]) : na
float cYz = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : na
float uYz = open > 0 ? math.log(high / open) : na
float dYz = open > 0 ? math.log(low / open) : na
float rsBar = na(uYz) or na(cYz) or na(dYz) ? na : math.max(uYz * (uYz - cYz) + dYz * (dYz - cYz), 0.0)
float varOYz = ewm(oYz * oYz, hlVol)
float varCYz = ewm(cYz * cYz, hlVol)
float varRS  = ewm(rsBar, hlVol)
float sigmaYZ = math.sqrt(math.max(varOYz + K_YZ * varCYz + (1.0 - K_YZ) * varRS, 0.0))
float sigma3YZ = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaYZ * sigmaYZ))

// EWMA vol (RiskMetrics lambda)
var float varEw = na
varEw := na(varEw) ? r * r : ewmaLam * varEw + (1.0 - ewmaLam) * r * r
float sigWEwma  = math.sqrt(math.max(varEw, 0.0))

// Robust IQR z (prior-bar window, registered post-null definition)
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Regime state
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// Ribbon: AVFI v3 vs EMA(v2), spread fill + sigma bands
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// Histogram: sigma-normalized spread
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// Data window: the measured values
plot(sigma3, "sigma3", display=display.data_window)
plot(rho,    "rho3",   display=display.data_window)
plot(spread, "spread", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_z,   "sp_z",   display=display.data_window)
plot(med_s,  "med_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q25_s,  "q25_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(q75_s,  "q75_s",  display=display.data_window)
plot(rsig_s, "rsig_s", display=display.data_window)
plot(sp_rz,  "sp_rz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(sigmaGK,  "sigma_gk",  display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3GK, "sigma3_gk", display=display.data_window)
plot(clv,      "clv",       display=display.data_window)
plot(spZClv,   "sp_z_clv",  display=display.data_window)
plot(asym,     "asym",      display=display.data_window)
plot(sigmaYZ,   "sigma_yz",   display=display.data_window)
plot(sigma3YZ,  "sigma3_yz",  display=display.data_window)
plot(sigWEwma,  "sig_w_ewma",  display=display.data_window)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v5_mathcore.pine

// ── Amendment 9 cross-market clock (registered cuts, do not tune) ──
grpXM     = "Amd 9 cross-market clock (registered; booking tool books, chart observes)"
xmMarket  = input.string("Auto", "Market", options=["Auto", "NQ", "GOLD", "NIKKEI", "DAX", "Manual"], group=grpXM)
xmLowMan  = input.float(0.0,   "Manual LOW cut (weekly sigma_w)",  minval=0.0, group=grpXM)
xmHighMan = input.float(0.0,   "Manual HIGH cut (weekly sigma_w)", minval=0.0, group=grpXM)

// ── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
EPS       = 1e-12
SQRT_PI_2 = 1.2533141373155001
FOUR_LN2  = 2.772588722239781
K_YZ      = 0.34 / 2.34   // Yang-Zhang 2000 optimal weight, large-n limit
SPZ_EXT   = 2.0           // |spread z| extremity threshold (Amd 11 draft)
RZ_EXT    = 2.0           // |robust z| extremity threshold (Amd 11 draft)
RHO_EXT   = 2.0           // volume-surprise burst threshold (Amd 11 draft)
ASYM_HI   = 2.0           // down/up vol asymmetry high tail (Amd 11 draft)
ASYM_LO   = 0.5           // down/up vol asymmetry low tail (Amd 11 draft)

// ── EWM with halflife (Pine == pandas adjust=False) ───────────────
ewm(float x, float hl) =>
    float alpha = 1.0 - math.pow(0.5, 1.0 / hl)
    var float e = na
    e := na(x) ? e : na(e) ? x : e + alpha * (x - e)
    e

// ── Prior-bar percentile rank helper (v4; non-repainting core math) ─
// Counts, among the `win` PRIOR bars (current bar excluded by
// construction: the loop starts at i = 1), the share of valid values
// strictly below the current one. Coverage gate: na until at least
// `minCov` valid prior values exist. This is a RANK, not a quantile:
// no interpolation, no current-bar self-inclusion.
pctRank(float src, int win, int minCov) =>
    float result = na
    if not na(src)
        int cnt = 0
        int tot = 0
        for i = 1 to win
            float v = src[i]
            if not na(v)
                tot += 1
                if v < src
                    cnt += 1
        result := tot >= minCov ? cnt * 1.0 / tot : na
    result

// ── Volume fallback for no-volume symbols ─────────────────────────
hasRealVol = nz(volume, 0) > 0
proxyVol   = math.max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick)
vol        = hasRealVol ? volume : proxyVol

tp = hlc3
r  = tp > 0 and tp[1] > 0 ? math.log(tp / tp[1]) : 0.0

z_of(float sig) => r / (sig + EPS)

tanh_custom(float x) =>
    float e2x = math.exp(2.0 * math.max(-20.0, math.min(20.0, x)))
    (e2x - 1.0) / (e2x + 1.0)

w_of(float z) =>
    float x = k * math.max(0.0, math.abs(z) - deadZone)
    tanh_custom(x) * math.sign(z)

// ── AVFI v3 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sigmaR  = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
sigmaPk = math.sqrt(ewm(math.pow(math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0)), 2), hlVol) / FOUR_LN2)
sigma3  = math.sqrt(math.max(math.pow(sigmaR, 2), math.pow(sigmaPk, 2)))
w3      = w_of(z_of(sigma3))

lv      = vol > 0 ? math.log(vol) : na
lva     = ewm(lv, hlFlow)[1]
va      = math.exp(lva)
rho     = vol > 0 ? vol / (va + EPS) : 0.0
m3      = rho / (1.0 + rho / gamma)
dv3     = w3 * m3 * va
flow3   = ewm(dv3, hlFlow)
avfi3_  = ta.ema(flow3 / (va + EPS), smooth)
avfi3   = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3_ : na

// ── AVFI v2 + slow EMA ────────────────────────────────────────────
sigma2 = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(math.abs(r), hlVol)
w2     = w_of(z_of(sigma2))
vave2  = ewm(vol, hlFlow)[1]
rho2   = vol > 0 ? vol / (vave2 + EPS) : 0.0
m2     = rho2 / (1.0 + rho2 / gamma)
flow2  = ewm(w2 * m2 * vave2, hlFlow)
avfi2_ = ta.ema(flow2 / (vave2 + EPS), smooth)
avfi2  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi2_ : na
emaV2  = ta.ema(avfi2, emaLen)

// ── Spread + rolling distribution ─────────────────────────────────
spread = avfi3 - emaV2
mu_s   = ta.sma(spread, normWin)
sd_s   = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity per v3.3.1 finding
sp_z   = (spread - mu_s) / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v3.4 MEASUREMENT LAYERS (unchanged by v4; everything above untouched)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 1. Garman-Klass OHLC vol floor.
float u_gk   = math.log(math.max(high / low, 1.0))
float c_gk   = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : 0.0
float varGk  = math.max(0.5 * u_gk * u_gk - (2.0 * math.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c_gk * c_gk, 0.0)
float sigmaGK  = math.sqrt(math.max(ewm(varGk, hlVol), EPS))
float sigma3GK = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaGK * sigmaGK))

// 2. Close-location-value flow variant (Amendment 4 withdrawn; descriptive).
float clv      = math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, (2.0 * close - high - low) / math.max(high - low, EPS)))
float dv3Clv   = w3 * m3 * va * (1.0 + qK * clv)
float flow3Clv = ewm(dv3Clv, hlFlow)
float avfi3Clv_ = ta.ema(flow3Clv / (va + EPS), smooth)
float avfi3Clv  = bar_index >= warmup ? avfi3Clv_ : na
float spreadClv = avfi3Clv - emaV2
float mu_c  = ta.sma(spreadClv, normWin)
float sd_c  = ta.stdev(spreadClv, normWin, false)   // sample stdev (ddof=1), pandas parity
float spZClv = (spreadClv - mu_c) / (sd_c + EPS)

// 3. Up/down vol asymmetry (descriptive only).
float absR   = math.abs(r)
float absRUp = r > 0 ? absR : na
float absRDn = r < 0 ? absR : na
float sigmaUp = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRUp, hlVol)
float sigmaDn = SQRT_PI_2 * ewm(absRDn, hlVol)
float asym    = sigmaDn / (sigmaUp + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: YANG-ZHANG VOL FLOOR (Tsay ch.3; PK -> GK -> YZ)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Yang-Zhang (2000): σ²_YZ = σ²_o + k·σ²_c + (1−k)·σ²_RS with the
// paper's optimal k = 0.34/(1.34+(n+1)/(n−1)) → 0.34/2.34 at large n.
// o = ln(O/C[1]) overnight gap; c = ln(C/O); Rogers-Satchell per-bar
// rs = u(u−c) + d(d−c) with u = ln(H/O), d = ln(L/O), non-negative by
// construction (u >= 0 >= d; H >= C, L <= C inside the range); the
// clamp guards data errors only. EWM(hlVol) adaptation as in the GK
// layer: per-bar raw second moments (drift² is second-order at the
// weekly horizon, the same approximation the GK layer makes).
// Adds what GK lacks: overnight gaps and drift robustness. DESCRIPTIVE
// FLOOR ONLY; feeds nothing back.
float oYz = open > 0 and not na(close[1]) and close[1] > 0 ? math.log(open / close[1]) : na
float cYz = open > 0 and close > 0 ? math.log(close / open) : na
float uYz = open > 0 ? math.log(high / open) : na
float dYz = open > 0 ? math.log(low / open) : na
float rsBar = na(uYz) or na(cYz) or na(dYz) ? na : math.max(uYz * (uYz - cYz) + dYz * (dYz - cYz), 0.0)
float varOYz = ewm(oYz * oYz, hlVol)
float varCYz = ewm(cYz * cYz, hlVol)
float varRS  = ewm(rsBar, hlVol)
float sigmaYZ = math.sqrt(math.max(varOYz + K_YZ * varCYz + (1.0 - K_YZ) * varRS, 0.0))
float sigma3YZ = math.sqrt(math.max(sigmaR * sigmaR, sigmaYZ * sigmaYZ))

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: SPREAD VELOCITY + CURVATURE (second derivative)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// velocity = first difference of the spread; curvature = second
// difference. HONEST CAVEAT: the spread is near-integrated (lag-1
// ~0.985), so differences are small and noisy; curvature is noise-
// dominated at the weekly grain and NO reversal claim is licensed by
// it. The fade-at-extremes family (H8) was executed and returned
// NULL. Descriptive display only; curvZ = curvature / sd_s is a
// scale-free DISPLAY normalization, not a registered series.
float velocity  = ta.change(spread)
float curvature = ta.change(velocity)
float curvZ     = na(curvature) or na(sd_s) ? na : curvature / (sd_s + EPS)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TOPS/BOTTOMS LAYER (v3.2; weekly-chart logic, observation only)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Robust spread z: median/IQR scale, PRIOR-bar window (the current bar
// is excluded from its own reference distribution). REGISTERED
// nearest-rank percentiles; the linear-interpolation substitution is
// REFUSED (post-null registered definition, H8/H9 executed on it).
float med_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 50)
float q75_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 75)
float q25_s  = ta.percentile_nearest_rank(spread[1], normWin, 25)
float rsig_s = (q75_s - q25_s) / 1.349 + EPS
float sp_rz  = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s

// Fresh registered 20-week closing extremes
float hhN = ta.highest(close, pbN)
float llN = ta.lowest(close, pbN)
bool newHigh = not na(hhN) and close >= hhN
bool newLow  = not na(llN) and close <= llN

// H8 draft flags (executed NULL; descriptive only)
bool divTop = newHigh and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz <= fadeZ
bool divBot = newLow  and not na(sp_rz) and sp_rz >= -fadeZ

// H9 draft flags (executed NULL; descriptive only)
bool climax    = not na(rho) and rho >= climRho and math.abs(z_of(sigma3)) <= deadZone
bool climaxTop = climax and newHigh
bool climaxBot = climax and newLow

// CUSUM regime-break guard (prior-bar moments only: mu_s[1], sd_s[1]).
var float cPos = 0.0
var float cNeg = 0.0
if na(spread) or na(mu_s[1]) or na(sd_s[1])
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
else
    cPos := math.max(0.0, cPos + (spread - mu_s[1]) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
    cNeg := math.max(0.0, cNeg + (mu_s[1] - spread) - cusumK * sd_s[1])
bool cusumAlarm = not na(sd_s[1]) and math.max(cPos, cNeg) > cusumH * (sd_s[1] + EPS)
if cusumAlarm
    cPos := 0.0
    cNeg := 0.0
var int lastBreakBar = -1
var int tbSinceBreak = 0
if cusumAlarm
    lastBreakBar := bar_index
    tbSinceBreak := 0
bool nearBreak = lastBreakBar >= 0 and bar_index - lastBreakBar <= breakHold
bool tbFlagged = (divTop or divBot or climaxTop or climaxBot) and nearBreak
if tbFlagged
    tbSinceBreak += 1

// ── Quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows (v3.3, observation only) ─
bool isExpiryWk = timeframe.isweekly and dayofweek(time) == dayofweek.friday and (month(time) == 3 or month(time) == 6 or month(time) == 9 or month(time) == 12) and dayofmonth(time) >= 15 and dayofmonth(time) <= 21
bool rollWindow = isExpiryWk or isExpiryWk[1]

// ── Regime state as a series (same formula as the original label) ──
string stateUp = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Momentum up"   : "Reversal up"
string stateDn = emaV2 >= 0 ? "Reversal down" : "Momentum down"
string stateNow = na(spread) or na(emaV2) ? "" : (spread >= 0 ? stateUp : stateDn)

float mag    = na(sp_z) ? 0.0 : math.min(math.abs(sp_z), 3.0) / 3.0
int   transp = int(90 - 70 * mag)
sp_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, transp) : color.new(color.red, transp)

// ── Mode flags ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool weeklyOK  = timeframe.isweekly
showRibbon = fillMode == "Ribbon" or fillMode == "Both"
showHisto  = fillMode == "Normalized histogram" or fillMode == "Both"

// ── PGT plane-geometry terminal layer (v3.5; Amendment 5 withdrawn) ─
bool zWinOK  = not na(sp_z[oscWin - 1])
float zMax26 = zWinOK ? ta.highest(sp_z, oscWin) : na
float zMin26 = zWinOK ? ta.lowest(sp_z, oscWin)  : na

var float hiRunEndSpz = na
var bool  hiInRun = false
var float loRunEndSpz = na
var bool  loInRun = false
float hiPriorSpz = na
float loPriorSpz = na
if newHigh and not hiInRun
    hiPriorSpz := hiRunEndSpz
if newLow and not loInRun
    loPriorSpz := loRunEndSpz

bool g1Top = newHigh and not na(sp_z) and not na(hiPriorSpz) and hiPriorSpz >= bandK and sp_z < hiPriorSpz
bool g1Bot = newLow  and not na(sp_z) and not na(loPriorSpz) and loPriorSpz <= -bandK and sp_z > loPriorSpz
bool g2Top = newHigh and not na(zMax26) and zMax26 >= bandK and zMin26 > -bandK
bool g2Bot = newLow  and not na(zMin26) and zMin26 <= -bandK and zMax26 < bandK
bool crossedDn = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z < 0 and sp_z[1] >= 0
bool crossedUp = not na(sp_z) and not na(sp_z[1]) and sp_z > 0 and sp_z[1] <= 0
bool g3Top = crossedDn and stateNow == "Momentum up"
bool g3Bot = crossedUp and stateNow == "Momentum down"
bool pgtTop = g1Top or g2Top or g3Top
bool pgtBot = g1Bot or g2Bot or g3Bot

if newHigh
    hiInRun := true
    hiRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    hiInRun := false
if newLow
    loInRun := true
    loRunEndSpz := sp_z
else
    loInRun := false

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESEARCH OVERLAY (v3.1, retained). Weekly-chart logic, observation only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Rule set T: 12-week log return of weekly closes (frozen lookback)
float mom12    = na(close[momLen]) or close[momLen] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[momLen])
bool  tLong    = not na(mom12) and mom12 > 0

float mom12Last = na(close[momLen + 1]) or close[momLen + 1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close[1] / close[momLen + 1])
bool  tLongLast = not na(mom12Last) and mom12Last > 0

// 8-week realized vol, annualized. Manual sample stdev (ddof=1) parity.
float r_w    = na(close[1]) or close[1] <= 0 ? na : math.log(close / close[1])
float r_mean = ta.sma(r_w, volWin)
float r_var  = math.sum(math.pow(r_w - r_mean, 2), volWin) / (volWin - 1)
float vol8   = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var) * math.sqrt(52.0)

// U3 sizing (throttle regime; U3 failed its holdout test, parallel-logged)
float volFactor = na(vol8) or vol8 <= 0 ? 1.0 : math.min(1.0, volTgt / 100.0 / (vol8 + EPS))
float throttle   = stateNow == "Reversal down" ? thrAmt / 100.0 : 1.0
float u3Size     = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0.0

bool  tFlip    = not na(tLong[1]) and tLong != tLong[1]
bool  stateChg = stateNow[1] != "" and stateNow != stateNow[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: SMH COMPRESSION STATE (registered definition)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// sig_w8 = sqrt(r_var): the 8-week sample stdev of weekly log returns
// BEFORE annualization · exactly the registered SMH trigger series.
// The cut LOW = 0.01739 is the FROZEN registered constant (sits at
// the 27.52nd percentile of the pre-2021 sig_w distribution). The
// registered trigger is the CROSS into the region; this layer shows
// the state and a running weeks-in-region counter, both descriptive
// bookkeeping. DESIGN BOOKKEEPING ONLY: pre-2021 cum12 0.634 [0.487,
// 0.782] vs matched climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471]; the dependence
// audit (block 70) shows the independence band understates the
// rival's uncertainty 3.4x-6.1x, so "disjoint bands" does NOT survive
// dependence-honest construction. Proof object = the forward log
// (EMPTY; kill lines 3/8/19/31). NOT a signal.
// v5 (Amendment 9, signed 2026-11-14): resolve the registered clock
// cuts PER CHART SYMBOL. Auto-detect by ticker root; Manual override;
// UNMATCHED symbols keep the NQ cuts (rev 2 behavior preserved, NQ
// parity exact). Cuts are FROZEN rank-transfer constants from each
// market's OWN pre-2021 sig_w distribution (Stage A report
// 2026-11-14); do not tune. The chart observes; book_macro_rows.py
// books the forward rows on Sundays.
string tkrUp  = str.upper(syminfo.ticker)
bool goldSym = str.contains(tkrUp, "GC") or str.contains(tkrUp, "XAU") or str.contains(tkrUp, "GOLD")
bool nkSym   = str.contains(tkrUp, "N225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NI225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NIKKEI") or str.contains(tkrUp, "JP225") or str.contains(tkrUp, "JPN225")
bool daxSym  = str.contains(tkrUp, "GDAXI") or str.contains(tkrUp, "DAX") or str.contains(tkrUp, "GER40")
bool nqSym   = str.contains(tkrUp, "NQ") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NDX") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NASDAQ") or str.contains(tkrUp, "NAS100") or str.contains(tkrUp, "USTEC")
string xmDet = xmMarket == "GOLD" ? "GOLD" : xmMarket == "NIKKEI" ? "NIKKEI" : xmMarket == "DAX" ? "DAX" : xmMarket == "Manual" ? "MANUAL" : xmMarket == "NQ" ? "NQ" : goldSym ? "GOLD" : nkSym ? "NIKKEI" : daxSym ? "DAX" : nqSym ? "NQ" : "NQ (default)"
float clockLow  = xmDet == "GOLD" ? 0.016023 : xmDet == "NIKKEI" ? 0.019622 : xmDet == "DAX" ? 0.019349 : xmDet == "MANUAL" ? xmLowMan  : lowCutIn
float clockHigh = xmDet == "GOLD" ? 0.022098 : xmDet == "NIKKEI" ? 0.027155 : xmDet == "DAX" ? 0.026782 : xmDet == "MANUAL" ? xmHighMan : 0.02697
bool  isMacroReg = xmDet == "GOLD" or xmDet == "NIKKEI" or xmDet == "DAX"

float sigW8 = na(r_var) or r_var <= 0 ? na : math.sqrt(r_var)
bool  inCompression = weeklyOK and not na(sigW8) and sigW8 <= clockLow
bool  inStress      = weeklyOK and not na(sigW8) and sigW8 >  clockHigh
var int weeksInComp = 0
weeksInComp := inCompression ? weeksInComp + 1 : 0
var int weeksInStress = 0
weeksInStress := inStress ? weeksInStress + 1 : 0
bool compCross  = inCompression and not inCompression[1]
bool stressCross = inStress and not inStress[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: PRIMED DRAFT STATE (Andrea's spec; UNEVALUATED)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Andrea's chat spec, verbatim semantics, grain-translated to the
// weekly registered universe (his spec used daily units on a weekly
// chart; see the hardening notes for the translation table):
//   rvol_pct   = prior-bar percentile rank of the ATR over 52 weeks
//   comp_weeks = consecutive weeks with rvol_pct <= 33
//   range_pos  = (close - lowest(low, 52w)) / (highest(high,52w) - lowest(low,52w))
//   is_primed  = comp_weeks >= 20 and rvol_pct <= 33 and range_pos <= 0.35
// This is a DRAFT DEFINITION. It is NOT the registered SMH machinery
// (which is sig_w8 vs LOW above), it has never been evaluated, no
// finding exists, and no "significance" is claimed or implied. The
// teal background makes the draft state visible for observation only.
float atrRaw  = ta.atr(atrLen)
float rvolPct = na(atrRaw) ? na : pctRank(atrRaw, rvolWin, rvolWin) * 100.0
var int compWeeks = 0
if not na(rvolPct) and rvolPct <= primedCut
    compWeeks += 1
else
    compWeeks := 0
float loR = ta.lowest(low, rangeWin)
float hiR = ta.highest(high, rangeWin)
float rangePos = (close - loR) / math.max(hiR - loR, syminfo.mintick)
bool isPrimed = weeklyOK and not na(rvolPct) and compWeeks >= compMin and rvolPct <= primedCut and not na(rangePos) and rangePos <= rangeCut
bool primedCross = isPrimed and not isPrimed[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v4 LAYER: EXTREMITY EXPORTS (Amendment 11 DRAFT; hard-gated)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// All ranks use the PRIOR-bar pctRank helper (current bar excluded);
// all flags use fixed constants declared above (Amendment 11 draft
// cells; see docs/amendment11_extremity_structure.md). DESCRIPTIVE
// ONLY: no backtest, no forward return, no evaluation is performed or
// licensed by these series. They exist so the eventual cross-check
// paste and the hard-gated Python engine read the SAME cells.
float absSpz   = na(sp_z) ? na : math.abs(sp_z)
float absRz    = na(sp_rz) ? na : math.abs(sp_rz)
float extPctSpz = pctRank(absSpz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctRz  = pctRank(absRz, normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctMom = pctRank(na(mom12) ? na : math.abs(mom12), normWin, extMinCov)
float extPctVol = pctRank(vol8, normWin, extMinCov)
bool  spzHi    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z >= SPZ_EXT
bool  spzLo    = not na(sp_z) and sp_z <= -SPZ_EXT
bool  spzAbs2  = spzHi or spzLo
bool  rzAbs2   = not na(sp_rz) and math.abs(sp_rz) >= RZ_EXT
bool  rhoBurst = not na(rho) and rho >= RHO_EXT
bool  asymHi   = not na(asym) and asym >= ASYM_HI
bool  asymLo   = not na(asym) and asym <= ASYM_LO
bool  anyExt   = spzAbs2 or rzAbs2 or rhoBurst or asymHi or asymLo

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v5 LAYERS: EWMA VOL + ROLL IMPLIED SPREAD + VXN (book displays)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Book concepts displayed, nothing registered, nothing evaluated, no
// feedback into any prior series.
// EWMA (RiskMetrics; Tsay ch.3 / Levy): lambda 0.94 frozen display,
// variance recursion without mean; weekly grain, non-annualized, so
// directly comparable to sig_w8 (export) and vol8 (annualized panel).
var float varEw = na
varEw := na(varEw) ? r * r : ewmaLam * varEw + (1.0 - ewmaLam) * r * r
float sigWEwma  = math.sqrt(math.max(varEw, 0.0))
float volEwmaAnn = sigWEwma * math.sqrt(52.0)

// Roll (1984; Tsay microstructure chapter): implied spread from the
// autocovariance of adjacent returns, s = 2*sqrt(-cov) in return
// units, shown as percent of price. na when cov >= 0 (no spread
// information: na, never 0). WEEKLY-GRAIN CAVEAT: Roll models the
// bid-ask bounce, which weekly bars aggregate away; this reads slower
// frictions / short-horizon mean reversion, NOT a trading spread.
// Panel row only (plot budget); recomputable from exported closes.
float covRoll = ta.covariance(r, r[1], rollCovWin)
float rollSpreadPct = na(covRoll) or covRoll >= 0.0 ? na : 200.0 * math.sqrt(-covRoll)

// VXN (unseen-ness benchmark, NQ context; panel-only): level + the
// implied-minus-trailing-realized gap vs vol8. Programme design
// facts (pre-2021, unsealed): LEVEL-cheap (entries at the 25.8th VXN
// percentile), MEAN-priced (VRP survives the trigger: +3.71 vol pts
// at entries vs +4.55 all weeks). NOT the event-structure unseenness
// test (blocked on the options term structure). Foreign symbol
// fetch: na outside coverage / plan limits; repaints only on the
// forming bar like every series here.
float vxnClose = request.security("CBOE:VXN", timeframe.period, close, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
float vxnGap = na(vxnClose) or na(vol8) ? na : vxnClose - vol8 * 100.0

// ── Ribbon mode: two lines + area between + σ-bands ───────────────
p3 = plot(showRibbon ? avfi3 : na, "AVFI v3", color=color.new(color.orange, 0), linewidth=2)
p2 = plot(showRibbon ? emaV2 : na, "EMA(v2)", color=color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth=1)

fill_col = showRibbon ? sp_col : color.new(color.gray, 100)
fill(p3, p2, color=fill_col, title="Spread (v3 − EMA v2)")

plot(showRibbon ? mu_s + bandK * sd_s : na, "Upper band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s - bandK * sd_s : na, "Lower band", color=color.new(color.gray, 70), linewidth=1)
plot(showRibbon ? mu_s : na, "mu(spread)", color=color.new(color.gray, 50), linewidth=1)

// ── Histogram mode: σ-normalized spread + ruler ───────────────────
hist_col = spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.lime, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20)
plot(showHisto ? sp_z : na, "Spread (z)", color=hist_col, style=plot.style_columns)

hline(0, "Agreement", color=color.gray, linestyle=hline.style_dashed)

plot(showHisto ?  3 : na, "3 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  2 : na, "2 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ?  1 : na, "1 sigma",  color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -1 : na, "-1 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 85), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -2 : na, "-2 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 75), linewidth=1)
plot(showHisto ? -3 : na, "-3 sigma", color=color.new(color.gray, 80), linewidth=1)

// ── Tops/bottoms markers (v4: confirmed-bar gated, no intrabar flicker)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", shape.triangledown, location.top, color.new(color.red, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", shape.triangleup, location.bottom, color.new(color.lime, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxTop and not divTop, "Climax at high (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.top, color.new(color.orange, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showTB and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and climaxBot and not divBot, "Climax at low (effort>result)", shape.xcross, location.bottom, color.new(color.aqua, 0), size=size.tiny)

// PGT markers (confirmed-bar gated)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top",    shape.triangledown, location.top,    color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", shape.triangleup,   location.bottom, color.new(color.yellow, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Top and not g1Top, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation top",    shape.circle, location.top,    color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)
plotshape(showPGT and weeklyOK and barstate.isconfirmed and g2Bot and not g1Bot, "PGT G2 truncated oscillation bottom", shape.circle, location.bottom, color.new(color.white, 0), size=size.tiny)

plot(showRZ ? rzDisp : na, "Spread robust z (IQR, prior-bar win; display-clamped)", color=color.new(color.teal, 30), linewidth=1)

    table.cell(tbl, 1, 39, xmStr, text_color=cTxt, bgcolor=isMacroReg ? color.new(color.gray, 70) : na, text_size=size.small)

// ── Last-bar state label (original, kept) ──────────────────────────
var label lbl = na

if barstate.islast
    if not na(lbl)
        label.delete(lbl)
    string rstate = stateNow == "" ? "warming up" : stateNow
    string z_str2 = na(sp_z) ? "n/a" : (sp_z > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(sp_z, "0.##") + " sigma"
    color lbl_bg = na(spread) ? color.new(color.gray, 30) : (spread >= 0 ? color.new(color.green, 20) : color.new(color.red, 20))
    string lbl_txt = "Spread " + z_str2 + "  |  " + rstate
    lbl := label.new(bar_index, nz(avfi3, 0.0), lbl_txt, style=label.style_label_left, color=lbl_bg, textcolor=color.white, size=size.small)

// ── Alerts (set each to "Once Per Bar Close" in TradingView) ───────
alertcondition(tFlip, "T signal flip", "AVFI research: 12-week trend signal flipped (paper)")
alertcondition(stateChg, "Regime state change", "AVFI research: AVFI regime state changed")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Reversal down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Reversal down"), "Reversal-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered reversal_down (throttle active, paper)")
alertcondition(stateNow == "Momentum down" and not (stateNow[1] == "Momentum down"), "Momentum-down entry", "AVFI research: regime entered momentum_down (deep-bear regime, paper)")
alertcondition(divTop, "Top fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v5: fresh 20-week high, flow fade below median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(divBot, "Bottom fade (descriptive)", "AVFI v5: fresh 20-week low, flow fade above median (descriptive; H8 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(g1Top, "PGT G1 plane divergence top", "AVFI v5: fresh 20w high, effort plane refused the band excursion (descriptive; Amendment 5 withdrawn)")
alertcondition(g1Bot, "PGT G1 plane divergence bottom", "AVFI v5: fresh 20w low, effort plane refused the band excursion (descriptive; Amendment 5 withdrawn)")
alertcondition(climaxTop or climaxBot, "Climax effort>result (descriptive)", "AVFI v5: volume surprise with dead price result at a fresh extreme (descriptive; H9 tested null, paper)")
alertcondition(cusumAlarm, "CUSUM regime break", "AVFI v5: spread CUSUM regime-break alarm (z-score window suspect)")
alertcondition(tbFlagged, "TB flag in break window", "AVFI v5: tops/bottoms flag inside CUSUM break window (descriptive; tested null, paper)")

alertcondition(primedCross, "Primed state entry (DRAFT, unevaluated)", "AVFI v5: primed draft state (Andrea's spec; unevaluated, NOT a research finding; paper)")
alertcondition(spzAbs2 and not spzAbs2[1], "Spread z extremity (Amd 11 draft)", "AVFI v5: |spread z| >= 2 (Amendment 11 draft export; descriptive only, never evaluated; paper)")

alertcondition((compCross or stressCross) and isMacroReg, "Amd 9 macro clock entry (bookkeeping)", "AVFI v5: registered macro market clock cross (the Sunday booking tool books the forward rows; this chart observes; design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof; paper)")

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v_ruleset.py (3153 bytes)
"""Rule set V: the from-scratch synthesis strategy (docs/ruleset_v_spec.md).

Registered 2026-11-12 on Andrea's instruction. Signal identical to rule
set T; sizing = vol targeting with a graduated AVFI-regime modulation of
the target (20% normally, 15% in reversal_down), replacing U3's binary
throttle. Frozen constants, never re-tuned:

  momLen 12, volWin 8, volTgt 0.20, defTgt 0.15, cap 1.0, no leverage,
  no short side.

Conventions (identical to the stage 3/4 conventions and the Pine vol8
parity): vol8 = sample stdev (ddof=1) of the last volWin weekly log
returns, annualized with sqrt(52); the size decided at a Friday decision
close applies to the FOLLOWING week (1-bar lag, like every rule here).

compute_v(df) -> DataFrame aligned to df with columns:
  date, close, state, mom12, t_long, vol8_ann, tgt, v_size, u3_size

Discipline: this module computes signal/sizing series ONLY. It never
computes returns, Sharpe, equity, or any performance statistic; the
2021+ holdout is spent and is never evaluated; V's only evaluation path
is the stage 7 forward paper track. No p-value exists for V.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from avfi_replication import compute_avfi

V_DEFAULT_PARAMS = {
    "momLen": 12, "volWin": 8, "volTgt": 0.20, "defTgt": 0.15,
    "cap": 1.0,
    # U3 constants (parity/parallel row only, unchanged from Amendment 2)
    "u3VolTgt": 0.20, "u3Throttle": 0.5,
}


def compute_v(df: pd.DataFrame, **overrides) -> pd.DataFrame:
    p = dict(V_DEFAULT_PARAMS)
    p.update(overrides)
    close = df["close"].astype(float)

    mom12 = pd.Series(
        np.where(close.shift(p["momLen"]) > 0,
                 np.log(close / close.shift(p["momLen"])), np.nan),
        index=df.index)
    t_long = mom12 > 0

    r = pd.Series(np.where((close > 0) & (close.shift(1) > 0),
                           np.log(close / close.shift(1)), np.nan),
                  index=df.index)
    vol8 = r.rolling(p["volWin"], min_periods=p["volWin"]).std() * np.sqrt(52.0)

    av = compute_avfi(df)
    state = av["state"]
    rdn = state == "reversal_down"

    tgt = pd.Series(np.where(rdn, p["defTgt"], p["volTgt"]), index=df.index)
    # spec: size = min(cap, tgt/vol8) when long, 0 when flat; NaN only
    # where the rule cannot compute yet (long & vol8 undefined, warmup)
    long_ok = t_long & vol8.notna() & (vol8 > 0)
    v_size = pd.Series(0.0, index=df.index)
    v_size[long_ok] = np.minimum(p["cap"], tgt / vol8)[long_ok]
    v_size[t_long & ~long_ok] = np.nan

    # U3 parallel row (unchanged Amendment 2 constants, for the card)
    u3_factor = pd.Series(
        np.where(vol8.notna() & (vol8 > 0),
                 np.minimum(1.0, p["u3VolTgt"] / vol8), np.nan),
        index=df.index)
    u3_long = t_long & u3_factor.notna()
    u3_size = pd.Series(0.0, index=df.index)
    u3_size[u3_long] = (u3_factor * np.where(rdn, p["u3Throttle"], 1.0))[u3_long]
    u3_size[t_long & ~u3_long] = np.nan

    return pd.DataFrame({
        "date": df["date"], "close": close, "state": state,
        "mom12": mom12, "t_long": t_long, "vol8_ann": vol8,
        "tgt": tgt, "v_size": v_size, "u3_size": u3_size,
    })

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/avfi_v_ruleset.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/backtest.py (5650 bytes)
"""Weekly-bar backtest engine. Decisions at weekly close, next-open fills.

Timing contract (programme section 5):
  - all rule decisions are made on information through weekly close i
  - conservative fill: next bar's open (i+1); sensitivity: same close i
  - costs per side (parameterizable, stage 5 stress): baseline
    1 tick slippage (0.25 pts) + commission ($2.25/side at $20/pt =
    0.1125 pts) -> 0.3625 pts adverse adjustment per fill
  - initial stop: stop_mult x ATR(20) at the decision bar, breached
    intraweek against bar low (long) / high (short) -> exit at stop price
  - per-bar PnL: marked to weekly close (entry bar: close - entry fill;
    exit bar via stop: stop - prior close; flat weeks: 0). Trade records
    use actual fill prices; the two differ by the exit-week gap, by design.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

TICK = 0.25
COMM = 2.25 / 20.0
COST_PER_SIDE = TICK + COMM  # 0.3625 pts


def atr20(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    h, l, c = df["high"], df["low"], df["close"]
    pc = c.shift(1)
    tr = pd.concat([h - l, (h - pc).abs(), (l - pc).abs()], axis=1).max(axis=1)
    return tr.ewm(alpha=1.0 / 20.0, adjust=False).mean()  # Wilder ATR(20)


def run_backtest(df: pd.DataFrame, entries: pd.Series, exits: pd.Series,
                 direction: str = "long", fill: str = "next_open",
                 stop_mult: float = 2.0, stop_series: pd.Series = None,
                 cost_per_side: float = COST_PER_SIDE):
    assert direction in ("long", "short")
    sgn = 1.0 if direction == "long" else -1.0
    atr = (stop_series if stop_series is not None else atr20(df)).to_numpy(float)
    o, h, l, c = (df[k].to_numpy(float) for k in ("open", "high", "low", "close"))
    dates = df["date"]
    ent = entries.fillna(False).to_numpy(bool)
    ext = exits.fillna(False).to_numpy(bool)
    n = len(df)

    pos = np.zeros(n, int)
    pnl = np.zeros(n)
    trades = []
    holding = False
    pending = None
    entry_px = stop_px = np.nan
    entry_bar = -1

    for i in range(n):
        if pending == "entry":
            fp = (o[i] + sgn * cost_per_side) if fill == "next_open" \
                else (c[i - 1] + sgn * cost_per_side)
            entry_px, stop_px = fp, fp - sgn * stop_mult * atr[i - 1]
            entry_bar = i
            pos[i] = 1
            holding = True
            trades.append({"entry_bar": i,
                           "entry_date": str(pd.Timestamp(dates.iloc[i]).date()),
                           "entry_px": float(fp), "stop_px": float(stop_px),
                           "decision_date": str(pd.Timestamp(dates.iloc[i - 1]).date())})
        elif pending == "exit":
            ep = (o[i] - sgn * cost_per_side) if fill == "next_open" \
                else (c[i - 1] - sgn * cost_per_side)
            trades[-1].update({"exit_bar": i,
                               "exit_date": str(pd.Timestamp(dates.iloc[i]).date()),
                               "exit_px": float(ep), "reason": "signal",
                               "ret_pts": float(sgn * (ep - trades[-1]["entry_px"]))})
            holding = False
        pending = None

        if holding:
            pos[i] = 1
            stop_hit = (l[i] <= stop_px) if direction == "long" else (h[i] >= stop_px)
            if stop_hit:
                trades[-1].update({"exit_bar": i,
                                   "exit_date": str(pd.Timestamp(dates.iloc[i]).date()),
                                   "exit_px": float(stop_px), "reason": "stop",
                                   "ret_pts": float(sgn * (stop_px - trades[-1]["entry_px"]))})
                pnl[i] += sgn * (stop_px - (c[i - 1] if i > entry_bar else entry_px))
                holding = False
            else:
                if i == entry_bar:
                    pnl[i] += sgn * (c[i] - entry_px)
                else:
                    pnl[i] += sgn * (c[i] - c[i - 1])

        if i < n - 1:
            if holding:
                if ext[i]:
                    pending = "exit"
            else:
                if ent[i]:
                    pending = "entry"

    if holding and trades:
        i = n - 1
        trades[-1].update({"exit_bar": i, "exit_date": "open",
                          "exit_px": float(c[i]), "reason": "eod",
                          "ret_pts": float(sgn * (c[i] - trades[-1]["entry_px"]))})

    out = pd.DataFrame({"date": df["date"].values, "close": c,
                        "pos": pos, "pnl_pts": pnl})
    return out, trades


def summarize(bt: pd.DataFrame, trades: list, label: str) -> dict:
    ret_pct = bt["pnl_pts"] / bt["close"]
    sd = ret_pct.std()
    tot = float(ret_pct.sum())
    sharpe = float(ret_pct.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0
    eq = (1 + ret_pct).cumprod()
    dd = float((eq / eq.cummax() - 1).min()) if len(eq) else 0.0
    done = [t for t in trades if "ret_pts" in t]
    wins = [t for t in done if t["ret_pts"] > 0]
    return {
        "label": label,
        "n_trades": len(done),
        "hit_rate": round(len(wins) / len(done), 3) if done else None,
        "mean_trade_pct": round(float(np.mean([t["ret_pts"] for t in done]) /
                                      np.mean([t["entry_px"] for t in done])), 5)
        if done else None,
        "total_ret_pct": round(tot, 4),
        "ann_sharpe": round(sharpe, 3),
        "max_drawdown_pct": round(dd, 4),
        "exposure_pct": round(float((bt["pos"] > 0).mean()), 3),
        "stop_exits": sum(1 for t in done if t["reason"] == "stop"),
        "signal_exits": sum(1 for t in done if t["reason"] == "signal"),
        "eod_exits": sum(1 for t in done if t["reason"] == "eod"),
    }

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/strategies/backtest.py

# Quant Resources Application Map (v1.0)

What this is: the three reference artifacts (ORDERFLOW MASTER PROMPT, Top 1000 starter catalogue, Top 100 SALIENT resource list) applied to our work. Three applications were made: (1) the SALIENT artefact checklist (items 091-100) audited against the live weekly pipeline, (2) both catalogues triaged against the market-studies knowledge base with errors the KB itself catches, (3) the genuinely new material assigned to a prioritized paper queue and to the future platform's procurement plan. Nothing in rule set T changed; no amendment implied by anything here.

## 1. SALIENT checklist audit of the weekly pipeline (items 091-100)

Audited against the pipeline mirror (research/, data/, docs/) on 11 Nov 2026. Results:

- 091 Data dictionary: PARTIAL. Column meanings are implicit in stage0c_canonical.py and the reports (week_end, n_sessions, thin_vol_flag all defined in code), but no standalone dictionary doc. GAP.
- 092 Data provenance manifest: PARTIAL. stage0c_report.json records source (Yahoo v8 chart NQ=F, adjustdisabled), fetched_at_utc, null drops and the 22 bad-highlow repairs; stage0d_source_crosscheck_report.json records the cross-source check. No file hashes. GAP (minor).
- 093 Contract-roll specification: GAP. The programme doc allows last-trade continuous at the weekly horizon and requires the splice to be documented; the Yahoo continuous series' roll dates are not documented anywhere. This is exactly what the standing broker/CME 1999-2000 export item would fix. REMAINS OPEN.
- 094 Session calendar: PRESENT. n_sessions and thin_vol_flag columns handle holiday weeks; the stage 7 fetch-staleness guard keys on fetch date vs Friday close precisely to avoid partial-week booking.
- 095 Order-flow feature specification: PRESENT IN SPIRIT. AVFI/VPVI is consistently labeled an OHLCV-derived proxy (the master prompt's Tier 1 proxy-flow tier), never claimed as true order flow.
- 096 VPVI baseline specification: PRESENT. The v3 reference Pine plus stage1/stage1b replication reports preserve formula, undefined cases and warm-up behaviour.
- 097 Pine/Python parity test suite: STRONG. stage1, stage1b (v32, v34, overlay, PGT) replication reports, the TS mirrors, and verify_all.sh. This is the checklist item we exceed.
- 098 Feature leakage audit: MOSTLY PRESENT. Past-only rolling statistics with explicit shift(1) conventions throughout, parity tests, the amendment discipline, the holdout-opened-once record. A formal per-feature shift/truncation test table does not exist as a single document; the discipline is enforced in code and reports.
- 099 Labeling specification: PRESENT. Forward 1-4 week returns, event definitions frozen in the amendments, outcomes timed in the append-only stage 7 log.
- 100 Chronological split manifest: PRESENT. 1999-2013 in-sample, 2014-2020 validation, 2021+ holdout spent exactly once at stage 6b and never touched again.

Verdict: 7 of 10 present or strong, 3 gaps, all three gaps documentation-grade, none affecting results. Action items (no rule change, docs only, next Python-enabled session): write the standalone data dictionary and provenance manifest (091, 092, with SHA256 of the canonical CSV); the roll spec (093) stays open pending the broker/CME export decision.

## 2. Catalogue triage (104 records against the market-studies KB)

Already covered by our notes (no re-reading, mapped to the note files): Fama efficiency reviews (context in The Quants/Björk notes), Madhavan 2000 survey, Biais-Glosten-Spatt 2005, Glosten-Milgrom 1985, Hasbrouck 1991 (book notes), Stoll 1989, O'Hara 1995 (full notes, new), Harris books, Tsay book, Bouchaud book (paused partial), Lo adaptive markets (The Quants notes), Engle 1982/Bollerslev 1986 GARCH (Tsay/Levy notes), Almgren-Chriss 2000 (Cartea/Johnson notes).

Errors the KB itself catches (confirmed against O'Hara's bibliography and our survey notes; fix before any citation):
- Record 051 "A Theory of Intraday Patterns: Volume and Price Variability" is attributed to Lawrence Harris (1986): it is Admati and Pfleiderer (1988), Review of Financial Studies 1.
- Record 075 "The Supply of Dealer Services in Securities Markets" attributed to Glosten/Milgrom-related (1978): it is Stoll (1978), Journal of Finance 33.
- Record 050 "A Simple Implicit Measure of the Effective Bid-Ask Spread in an Efficient Market" attributed to Glosten and Harris (1988): it is Roll (1984), Journal of Finance 39. Glosten-Harris (1988) is "Estimating the Components of the Bid/Ask Spread".
- Record 054 "Inferring Trade Direction from Intraday Data" attributed to Glosten/Harris: almost certainly Lee and Ready (1991), "Inferring Trade Direction from Intraday Transactions Data", Journal of Finance 46. Verify before citing.
- Record 073 "One Security, Many Markets: Determining the Contributions to Price Discovery" attributed to de Jong (1995): the canonical paper is Hasbrouck (1995), Journal of Finance 50. Verify before citing.
- Record 045 (Mandelbrot long memory, 1971) title/venue flagged by the catalogue itself: treat as a research lead only.
- Records 013/014 and 101/102 are internal duplicates of the same papers; the catalogue's own audit flags (093-104) are correct and were honored.

## 3. The paper queue (new study phase, ordered by programme value, fitted to the 10-20 h cap)

Books are done; papers are the next study phase. Ordered waves so medicine never pays for this:

Wave 1, validation spine (6 short papers, a few evenings total; upgrades stage 6 statistics and the future platform's gauntlet): White (2000) Reality Check; Hansen (2005) SPA; Benjamini-Hochberg (1995) FDR; Bailey and López de Prado (2014) Deflated Sharpe Ratio; Bailey, Borwein, López de Prado, Zhu (2017) PBO; Lo (2002) Statistics of Sharpe Ratios. These formalize what stage 6 already does "max-t/deflated-Sharpe-style" and give the exact machinery for the 26-entry stage 7 evaluation, where the honest trial count is the full H1-H11 plus amendments registry.

Wave 2, regime machinery (feeds H6, H10, and the vol-regime clock; also the future regime layer): Hamilton (1989) Markov switching; Maheu and McCurdy (2000) bull/bear identification; Diebold and Inoue (2001) long memory vs regime switching, directly relevant to the long-memory R/S findings in the Härdle notes and a standing caution for Elliott-style structure claims; Bai-Perron multiple structural change. Tools when implementing: ruptures, hmmlearn (both new to our stack).

Wave 3, order-flow core (the future platform's spine; also the Tier 2 confirmation layer for the weekly track's proxy flow): Cont, Kukanov, Stoikov (2014) OFI, the canonical paper for feature family E; Easley, López de Prado, O'Hara (2012) volume clock and flow toxicity, with the registered caution that VPIN is a contested proxy; Cont and Xu (2025) order-book dynamics framework; Kolm, Turiel, Westray (2023) deep OFI as the comparison class the master prompt requires (complex models must beat simple ones net of costs).

Wave 4, method-cautious background (for Addendum B's state-space ideas and the N1-N4 nulls): Theiler et al (1992) surrogate data, the exact technique the Elliott null tests N1-N2 specify; Schreiber (2000) transfer entropy; Peyré and Cuturi (2020) optimal transport tutorial; WATCH (2021) Wasserstein change-point; Chernozhukov et al (2018) double ML for the causal-discipline layer. Strogatz and Kantz-Schreiber stay as reference books, not queue items.

- Category A (data): the only paid decision anywhere in this material. CME DataMine and Databento are the Tier 2 candidates for true NQ flow when the ORDERFLOW platform starts; nothing to buy now. LOBSTER's free sample files allow a zero-cost book-reconstruction learning exercise whenever we want it. The standing broker/CME 1999-2000 export item remains the weekly track's data fix and is free.

- Categories C, I (search, software): already our stack. Genuinely new: ruptures and hmmlearn (wave 2 implementations).
- Categories D, E, F, G, H (papers): triaged into waves 1-4 above.
- Category J (checklists): audited in section 1.

## 5. Master prompt mapping (no execution, for the record)

The ORDERFLOW platform's KB protocol (KB-1 through KB-7) is already substantially satisfied by the market-studies knowledge base: source registry = the notes files, claim extraction = the durable rules/ratios in each file, contradiction log = the inter-book connections and honest assessments, mechanism-first = the mechanism sections. The platform, if and when Andrea green-lights it, starts from a genuinely populated KB, which is rare and valuable. The phases needing Andrea's decisions before any start: data budget (category A), compute location, and the go/no-go itself. Until then: parked. The weekly programme and the stage 7 cadence are unchanged and take precedence.
